===== quickcartbackend/api-gateway/src/main/java/com/quickcart/gateway/error/GatewayErrorHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.gateway.error;
@Component
@Order(-2)
public class GatewayErrorHandler implements ErrorWebExceptionHandler {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GatewayErrorHandler.class);
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    public GatewayErrorHandler(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> handle(ServerWebExchange exchange, Throwable ex) {
        if (exchange.getResponse().isCommitted()) {
            return Mono.error(ex);
        }
        HttpStatus status = resolveStatus(ex);
        String code;
        String message;
        switch (status) {
            case NOT_FOUND -> {
                code = "NOT_FOUND";
                message = "Resource not found.";
            }
            case BAD_REQUEST -> {
                code = "VALIDATION_ERROR";
                message = "Bad request.";
            }
            case UNAUTHORIZED -> {
                code = "UNAUTHORIZED";
                message = "Authentication is required.";
            }
            case FORBIDDEN -> {
                code = "FORBIDDEN";
                message = "You do not have permission to perform this action.";
            }
            case BAD_GATEWAY, SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, GATEWAY_TIMEOUT -> {
                status = HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE;
                code = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE";
                message = "A dependent service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.";
                log.warn("Upstream problem on {}: {}", exchange.getRequest().getPath(), ex.getMessage());
            }
            default -> {
                status = HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
                code = "INTERNAL_ERROR";
                message = "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.";
                log.error("Unexpected gateway error on {}", exchange.getRequest().getPath(), ex);
            }
        }
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(),
                status.getReasonPhrase(),
                code,
                message,
                exchange.getRequest().getPath().value(),
                List.of());
        exchange.getResponse().setStatusCode(status);
        exchange.getResponse().getHeaders().setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        try {
            byte[] bytes = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(body);
            DataBuffer buffer = exchange.getResponse().bufferFactory().wrap(bytes);
            return exchange.getResponse().writeWith(Mono.just(buffer));
        } catch (Exception writeFailure) {
            log.error("Could not serialise error body", writeFailure);
            return exchange.getResponse().setComplete();
        }
    }
        private HttpStatus resolveStatus(Throwable ex) {
        Throwable t = ex;
        for (int depth = 0; t != null && depth < 10; depth++, t = t.getCause()) {
            if (t instanceof ResponseStatusException rse) {
                HttpStatus resolved = HttpStatus.resolve(rse.getStatusCode().value());
                if (resolved != null) {
                    return resolved;
                }
            }
            if (t instanceof java.net.ConnectException
                    || t instanceof java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException
                    || t instanceof reactor.netty.http.client.PrematureCloseException) {
                return HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE;
            }
        }
        return HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/api-gateway/src/main/java/com/quickcart/gateway/filter/JwtAuthGlobalFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.gateway.filter;
@Component
public class JwtAuthGlobalFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {
    private static final String PREFIX = "Bearer ";
    private final JwtVerifier verifier;
    public JwtAuthGlobalFilter(JwtVerifier verifier) {
        this.verifier = verifier;
    }
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        HttpMethod method = exchange.getRequest().getMethod();
        String path = exchange.getRequest().getURI().getRawPath();
        String lower = path.toLowerCase();
        if (path.contains("..") || path.contains("//") || lower.contains("%2e") || lower.contains("%2f")) {
            return Mono.error(new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST));
        }
        if (HttpMethod.OPTIONS.equals(method) || isPublic(method, path)) {
            return chain.filter(exchange);
        }
        Optional<String> role = extractRole(exchange);
        if (role.isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.error(new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED));
        }
        if (under("/api/v1/admin", path) && !"ADMIN".equals(role.get())) {
            return Mono.error(new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN));
        }
        return chain.filter(exchange);
    }
    private boolean isPublic(HttpMethod method, String path) {
        if (HttpMethod.POST.equals(method) && under("/api/v1/auth", path)) {
            return true;
        }
        return HttpMethod.GET.equals(method)
                && (under("/api/v1/categories", path) || under("/api/v1/products", path));
    }
    private Optional<String> extractRole(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String header = exchange.getRequest().getHeaders().getFirst("Authorization");
        if (header == null || !header.startsWith(PREFIX)) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return verifier.verifyAndGetRole(header.substring(PREFIX.length()).trim());
    }
    private boolean under(String base, String path) {
        return path.equals(base) || path.startsWith(base + "/");
    }
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -100;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/api-gateway/src/main/java/com/quickcart/gateway/filter/RequestIdFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.gateway.filter;
@Component
public class RequestIdFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {
    public static final String HEADER = "X-Request-Id";
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        String incoming = exchange.getRequest().getHeaders().getFirst(HEADER);
        final String requestId = (incoming == null || incoming.isBlank())
                ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                : incoming;
        ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest().mutate()
                .headers(h -> h.set(HEADER, requestId))
                .build();
        exchange.getResponse().getHeaders().set(HEADER, requestId);
        return chain.filter(exchange.mutate().request(request).build());
    }
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return Ordered.HIGHEST_PRECEDENCE;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/api-gateway/src/main/java/com/quickcart/gateway/security/JwtVerifier.java =====
package com.quickcart.gateway.security;
@Component
public class JwtVerifier {
    private final SecretKey key;
    private final String issuer;
    public JwtVerifier(@Value("${quickcart.jwt.secret}") String secret,
                       @Value("${quickcart.jwt.issuer}") String issuer) {
        byte[] bytes = secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (bytes.length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("JWT_SECRET must be at least 32 characters");
        }
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(bytes);
        this.issuer = issuer;
    }
    public Optional<String> verifyAndGetRole(String token) {
        try {
            Jws<Claims> jws = Jwts.parser()
                    .verifyWith(key)
                    .requireIssuer(issuer)
                    .build()
                    .parseSignedClaims(token);
            if (!"HS256".equals(jws.getHeader().getAlgorithm())) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            String role = jws.getPayload().get("role", String.class);
            if ("CUSTOMER".equals(role) || "ADMIN".equals(role)) {
                return Optional.of(role);
            }
            return Optional.empty();
        } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/api-gateway/src/test/java/com/quickcart/gateway/GatewaySkeletonTest.java =====
package com.quickcart.gateway;
@SpringBootTest(
        webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT,
        properties = {"eureka.client.enabled=false"})
@AutoConfigureWebTestClient
class GatewaySkeletonTest {
    @Autowired
    WebTestClient client;
    @Test
    void internalPath_isNotRouted_returns404StandardBody() {
        client.post().uri("/internal/inventory/reserve")
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus().isNotFound()
                .expectBody()
                .jsonPath("$.code").isEqualTo("NOT_FOUND")
                .jsonPath("$.status").isEqualTo(404)
                .jsonPath("$.path").isEqualTo("/internal/inventory/reserve")
                .jsonPath("$.details").isArray();
    }
    @Test
    void unknownPath_returns404StandardBody() {
        client.get().uri("/api/v1/does-not-exist")
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus().isNotFound()
                .expectBody()
                .jsonPath("$.code").isEqualTo("NOT_FOUND");
    }
    @Test
    void routedPathWithNoInstances_returns503StandardBody() {
        client.get().uri("/api/v1/products")
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus().isEqualTo(503)
                .expectBody()
                .jsonPath("$.code").isEqualTo("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")
                .jsonPath("$.message").isNotEmpty();
    }
    @Test
    void requestId_isGeneratedWhenAbsent() {
        client.get().uri("/api/v1/does-not-exist")
                .exchange()
                .expectHeader().exists("X-Request-Id");
    }
    @Test
    void requestId_isEchoedWhenProvided() {
        client.get().uri("/api/v1/does-not-exist")
                .header("X-Request-Id", "abc-123")
                .exchange()
                .expectHeader().valueEquals("X-Request-Id", "abc-123");
    }
    @Test
    void corsPreflight_allowsConfiguredOrigin() {
        client.options().uri("/api/v1/products")
                .header("Origin", "http://localhost:5173")
                .header("Access-Control-Request-Method", "GET")
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus().isOk()
                .expectHeader().valueEquals("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "http://localhost:5173");
    }
    @Test
    void corsPreflight_rejectsUnknownOrigin() {
        client.options().uri("/api/v1/products")
                .header("Origin", "http://evil.example")
                .header("Access-Control-Request-Method", "GET")
                .exchange()
                .expectStatus().isForbidden();
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/config/OpenApiConfig.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.config;
@Configuration
public class OpenApiConfig {
    @Bean
    OpenAPI catalogServiceOpenApi() {
        return new OpenAPI()
                .info(new Info().title("QuickCart catalog-service").version("v1")
                        .description("Summary only. The master contract document is authoritative."))
                .components(new Components().addSecuritySchemes("bearerAuth",
                        new SecurityScheme().type(SecurityScheme.Type.HTTP).scheme("bearer").bearerFormat("JWT")))
                .addSecurityItem(new SecurityRequirement().addList("bearerAuth"));
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/config/SecurityConfig.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.config;
@Configuration
@EnableMethodSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http,
                                            JwtVerifier verifier,
                                            SecurityErrorWriter errorWriter,
                                            RestAuthenticationEntryPoint entryPoint,
                                            RestAccessDeniedHandler accessDeniedHandler,
                                            @Value("${quickcart.internal.api-key}") String internalApiKey)
            throws Exception {
        http
                .csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .httpBasic(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .formLogin(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .sessionManagement(s -> s.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .exceptionHandling(e -> e
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(entryPoint)
                        .accessDeniedHandler(accessDeniedHandler))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(a -> a
                        .requestMatchers("/actuator/health", "/actuator/health/**", "/actuator/info").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/swagger-ui.html", "/swagger-ui/**", "/v3/api-docs/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, "/api/v1/categories/**", "/api/v1/products/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/admin/**").hasRole("ADMIN")
                        .requestMatchers("/internal/**").hasRole("INTERNAL")
                        .anyRequest().authenticated())
                .addFilterBefore(new JwtAuthenticationFilter(verifier), UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class)
                .addFilterBefore(new InternalApiKeyFilter(internalApiKey, errorWriter),
                        UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class);
        return http.build();
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/controller/AdminCategoryController.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/admin/categories")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
public class AdminCategoryController {
    private final CategoryService service;
    public AdminCategoryController(CategoryService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A1 All categories incl. inactive")
    @GetMapping
    public List<CategoryResponse> list() {
        return service.listAll();
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A2 Create category")
    @PostMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public CategoryResponse create(@Valid @RequestBody CategoryRequest request) {
        return service.create(request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A3 Update category")
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public CategoryResponse update(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody CategoryRequest request) {
        return service.update(id, request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A4 Activate / deactivate category")
    @PatchMapping("/{id}/status")
    public CategoryResponse status(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody ActiveRequest request) {
        return service.setActive(id, request.active());
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/controller/AdminProductController.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/admin/products")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
public class AdminProductController {
    private final ProductService service;
    public AdminProductController(ProductService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A5 List all products")
    @GetMapping
    public PageResponse<AdminProductResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) String q,
                                                   @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
                                                   @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean active,
                                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
                                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        return service.listAdmin(q, categoryId, active, page, size);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A6 Product (any status)")
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public AdminProductResponse get(@PathVariable long id) {
        return service.getAdmin(id);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A7 Create product + inventory row")
    @PostMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public AdminProductResponse create(@Valid @RequestBody ProductCreateRequest request) {
        return service.create(request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A8 Update product details (not price/stock)")
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public AdminProductResponse update(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody ProductUpdateRequest request) {
        return service.update(id, request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A9 Update price")
    @PatchMapping("/{id}/price")
    public AdminProductResponse price(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody PriceRequest request) {
        return service.updatePrice(id, request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "A10 Activate / deactivate (soft delete)")
    @PatchMapping("/{id}/status")
    public AdminProductResponse status(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody ActiveRequest request) {
        return service.setActive(id, request.active());
    }
}

package com.quickcart.catalog.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/products")
public class ProductController {
    private final ProductService service;
    public ProductController(ProductService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "C2 Browse / search active products")
    @GetMapping
    public PageResponse<ProductResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) String q,
                                              @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
                                              @RequestParam(defaultValue = "name") String sort,
                                              @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
                                              @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        return service.listPublic(q, categoryId, sort, page, size);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "C3 Product details")
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ProductResponse get(@PathVariable long id) {
        return service.getPublic(id);
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/dto/request/InternalStockRequest.java =====

@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "categories")
public class Category extends BaseEntity {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 80)
    private String name;
    @Column(length = 255)
    private String description;
    @Column(name = "image_url", length = 500)
    private String imageUrl;
    @Setter
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private boolean active = true;
    public Category(String name, String description, String imageUrl) {
        this.name = name;
        this.description = description;
        this.imageUrl = imageUrl;
    }
    public void update(String name, String description, String imageUrl) {
        this.name = name;
        this.description = description;
        this.imageUrl = imageUrl;
    }

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/entity/Product.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.entity;
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "products")
public class Product extends BaseEntity {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "category_id", nullable = false)
    private Category category;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String name;
    @Column(length = 1000)
    private String description;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal price;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String unit;
    @Column(name = "image_url", length = 500)
    private String imageUrl;
    @Setter
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private boolean active = true;
    public Product(Category category, String name, String description, BigDecimal price,
                   String unit, String imageUrl) {
        this.category = category;
        this.name = name;
        this.description = description;
        this.price = price.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        this.unit = unit;
        this.imageUrl = imageUrl;
    }
    public void updateDetails(Category category, String name, String description, String unit, String imageUrl) {
        this.category = category;
        this.name = name;
        this.description = description;
        this.unit = unit;
        this.imageUrl = imageUrl;
    }
    public void changePrice(BigDecimal price) {
        this.price = price.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/exception/ApiException.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.exception;
public class ApiException extends RuntimeException {
    private final HttpStatus status;
    private final String code;
    private final List<ErrorDetail> details;
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message) {
        this(status, code, message, List.of());
    }
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        super(message);
        this.status = status;
        this.code = code;
        this.details = details;
    }
    public HttpStatus getStatus() { return status; }
    public String getCode() { return code; }
    public List<ErrorDetail> getDetails() { return details; }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/exception/GlobalExceptionHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.exception;
@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionHandler.class);
    @ExceptionHandler(ApiException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleApi(ApiException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(ex.getStatus(), ex.getCode(), ex.getMessage(), req, ex.getDetails());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleBeanValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException ex,
                                                              HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getBindingResult().getAllErrors().stream()
                .map(e -> new ErrorDetail(
                        e instanceof FieldError fe ? fe.getField() : e.getObjectName(),
                        e.getDefaultMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleConstraint(ConstraintViolationException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getConstraintViolations().stream()
                .map(v -> new ErrorDetail(lastNode(v.getPropertyPath()), v.getMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnreadable(HttpMessageNotReadableException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Malformed request body.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleTypeMismatch(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Invalid request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getName(), "Invalid value")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleMissingParam(MissingServletRequestParameterException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Missing request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getParameterName(), "Required")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler({HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class, HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException.class})
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnsupported(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Unsupported request.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(NoResourceFoundException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleNoResource(NoResourceFoundException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Resource not found.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AuthenticationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAuthentication(AuthenticationException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AccessDeniedException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAccessDenied(AccessDeniedException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN", "You do not have permission to perform this action.",
                req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnexpected(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        log.error("Unexpected error on {} {}", req.getMethod(), req.getRequestURI(), ex);
        return build(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.", req, List.of());
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> validation(String message, HttpServletRequest req,
                                                     List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", message, req, details);
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> build(HttpStatus status, String code, String message,
                                                HttpServletRequest req, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(),
                status.getReasonPhrase(),
                code,
                message,
                req.getRequestURI(),
                details == null ? List.of() : details);
        return ResponseEntity.status(status).body(body);
    }
    private String lastNode(jakarta.validation.Path path) {
        return StreamSupport.stream(path.spliterator(), false)
                .reduce((first, second) -> second)
                .map(Object::toString)
                .orElse("");
    }
}

package com.quickcart.catalog.internal;
@Hidden
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/internal")
public class InternalCatalogController {
    private final ProductService productService;
    private final InventoryService inventoryService;
    public InternalCatalogController(ProductService productService, InventoryService inventoryService) {
        this.productService = productService;
        this.inventoryService = inventoryService;
    }
    @PostMapping("/products/batch")
    public InternalProductBatchResponse batch(@Valid @RequestBody InternalProductBatchRequest request) {
        return new InternalProductBatchResponse(productService.snapshots(request.productIds()));
    }
    @PostMapping("/inventory/reserve")
    public InternalReserveResponse reserve(@Valid @RequestBody InternalStockRequest request) {
        return new InternalReserveResponse(inventoryService.reserve(request.items()));
    }
    @PostMapping("/inventory/release")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void release(@Valid @RequestBody InternalStockRequest request) {
        inventoryService.release(request.items());
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/inventory/InventoryRepository.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.inventory;
public interface InventoryRepository extends JpaRepository<Inventory, Long> {
    @Query(value = "select new com.quickcart.catalog.dto.response.InventoryItemResponse("
            + "p.id, p.name, p.unit, i.quantity, i.updatedAt) "
            + "from Inventory i, Product p "
            + "where p.id = i.productId and lower(p.name) like :pattern escape '!' "
            + "order by lower(p.name), p.id",
            countQuery = "select count(i) from Inventory i, Product p "
                    + "where p.id = i.productId and lower(p.name) like :pattern escape '!'")
    Page<InventoryItemResponse> search(@Param("pattern") String pattern, Pageable pageable);
    @Modifying
    @Query("update Inventory i set i.quantity = i.quantity - :qty, i.updatedAt = :now "
            + "where i.productId = :id and i.quantity >= :qty")
    int decrementIfAvailable(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("qty") int qty, @Param("now") Instant now);
    @Modifying
    @Query("update Inventory i set i.quantity = i.quantity + :qty, i.updatedAt = :now where i.productId = :id")
    int increment(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("qty") int qty, @Param("now") Instant now);
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/inventory/InventoryService.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.inventory;
@Service
public class InventoryService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(InventoryService.class);
    private final InventoryRepository inventoryRepository;
    private final ProductRepository productRepository;
    public InventoryService(InventoryRepository inventoryRepository, ProductRepository productRepository) {
        this.inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
        this.productRepository = productRepository;
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Map<Long, Integer> quantities(Collection<Long> productIds) {
        Map<Long, Integer> result = new HashMap<>();
        inventoryRepository.findAllById(productIds)
                .forEach(i -> result.put(i.getProductId(), i.getQuantity()));
        return result;
    }
    @Transactional
    public void create(long productId, int quantity) {
        inventoryRepository.save(new Inventory(productId, quantity));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<InventoryItemResponse> list(String q, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = QueryUtils.pageable(page, size, Sort.unsorted());
        return PageResponse.of(inventoryRepository.search(QueryUtils.likePattern(q), pageable));
    }
    @Transactional
    public InventoryItemResponse setQuantity(long productId, int quantity) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Product not found."));
        Inventory inventory = inventoryRepository.findById(productId).orElse(null);
        if (inventory == null) {
            inventory = inventoryRepository.save(new Inventory(productId, quantity));
        } else {
            inventory.setQuantity(quantity);
        }
        return new InventoryItemResponse(productId, product.getName(), product.getUnit(),
                inventory.getQuantity(), inventory.getUpdatedAt().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS));
    }
    @Transactional
    public List<ReservedItem> reserve(List<InternalStockRequest.Item> items) {
        Map<Long, Integer> wanted = merge(items);
        Map<Long, Product> products = productRepository.findWithCategoryByIdIn(wanted.keySet()).stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Product::getId, Function.identity()));
        List<ErrorDetail> unavailable = new ArrayList<>();
        wanted.keySet().forEach(id -> {
            Product p = products.get(id);
            if (p == null || !p.isActive() || !p.getCategory().isActive()) {
                unavailable.add(new ErrorDetail("productId:" + id, "Product is not available"));
            }
        });
        if (!unavailable.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE",
                    "Some products are no longer available.", unavailable);
        }
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        List<ErrorDetail> shortages = new ArrayList<>();
        List<ReservedItem> reserved = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Map.Entry<Long, Integer> e : wanted.entrySet()) {
            long id = e.getKey();
            int qty = e.getValue();
            if (inventoryRepository.decrementIfAvailable(id, qty, now) == 1) {
                Product p = products.get(id);
                reserved.add(new ReservedItem(id, p.getName(), p.getUnit(), p.getPrice(), qty));
            } else {
                int available = inventoryRepository.findById(id).map(Inventory::getQuantity).orElse(0);
                shortages.add(new ErrorDetail("productId:" + id, "Requested " + qty + ", available " + available));
            }
        }
        if (!shortages.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "INSUFFICIENT_STOCK",
                    "Some items are not available in the requested quantity.", shortages);
        }
        return reserved;
    }
    @Transactional
    public void release(List<InternalStockRequest.Item> items) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        merge(items).forEach((id, qty) -> {
            if (inventoryRepository.increment(id, qty, now) == 0) {
                log.warn("Release ignored unknown product id {}", id);
            }
        });
    }
    private Map<Long, Integer> merge(List<InternalStockRequest.Item> items) {
        Map<Long, Integer> merged = new TreeMap<>();
        items.forEach(i -> merged.merge(i.productId(), i.quantity(), Integer::sum));
        return merged;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/mapper/CatalogMapper.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.mapper;
@Component
public class CatalogMapper {
    public CategoryResponse toCategory(Category c) {
        return new CategoryResponse(c.getId(), c.getName(), c.getDescription(), c.getImageUrl(), c.isActive());
    }
    public ProductResponse toProduct(Product p, int quantity) {
        return new ProductResponse(p.getId(), p.getCategory().getId(), p.getCategory().getName(),
                p.getName(), p.getDescription(), p.getPrice(), p.getUnit(), p.getImageUrl(),
                quantity, quantity > 0);
    }
    public AdminProductResponse toAdminProduct(Product p, int quantity) {
        return new AdminProductResponse(p.getId(), p.getCategory().getId(), p.getCategory().getName(),
                p.getName(), p.getDescription(), p.getPrice(), p.getUnit(), p.getImageUrl(),
                quantity, quantity > 0, p.isActive(),
                p.getCreatedAt().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                p.getUpdatedAt().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS));
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/repository/ProductSpecifications.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.repository;
public final class ProductSpecifications {
    private ProductSpecifications() { }
    public static Specification<Product> visibleToCustomers() {
        return (root, query, cb) -> cb.and(
                cb.isTrue(root.get("active")),
                cb.isTrue(root.get("category").get("active")));
    }
    public static Specification<Product> nameLike(String pattern) {
        return (root, query, cb) -> cb.like(cb.lower(root.get("name")), pattern, '!');
    }
    public static Specification<Product> inCategory(Long categoryId) {
        return categoryId == null ? null
                : (root, query, cb) -> cb.equal(root.get("category").get("id"), categoryId);
    }
    public static Specification<Product> hasActive(Boolean active) {
        return active == null ? null : (root, query, cb) -> cb.equal(root.get("active"), active);
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/InternalApiKeyFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
public class InternalApiKeyFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {
    public static final String HEADER = "X-Internal-Api-Key";
    private final byte[] expected;
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public InternalApiKeyFilter(String apiKey, SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        if (apiKey == null || apiKey.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("INTERNAL_API_KEY must be set");
        }
        this.expected = apiKey.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    protected boolean shouldNotFilter(HttpServletRequest request) {
        return !request.getRequestURI().startsWith("/internal/");
    }
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response,
                                    FilterChain chain) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String provided = request.getHeader(HEADER);
        if (provided != null && MessageDigest.isEqual(provided.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), expected)) {
            SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                    "internal-caller", null, List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_INTERNAL"))));
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
            return;
        }
        writer.write(request, response, HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
public class JwtAuthenticationFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {
    private static final String PREFIX = "Bearer ";
    private final JwtVerifier verifier;
    public JwtAuthenticationFilter(JwtVerifier verifier) {
        this.verifier = verifier;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response,
                                    FilterChain chain) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String header = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (header != null && header.startsWith(PREFIX)) {
            verifier.parse(header.substring(PREFIX.length()).trim()).ifPresent(user -> {
                var auth = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                        user, null, List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_" + user.role())));
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(auth);
            });
        }
        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
    @Override
    protected boolean shouldNotFilter(HttpServletRequest request) {
        return request.getRequestURI().startsWith("/internal/");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/JwtVerifier.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
@Component
public class JwtVerifier {
    private final SecretKey key;
    private final String issuer;
    public JwtVerifier(@Value("${quickcart.jwt.secret}") String secret,
                       @Value("${quickcart.jwt.issuer}") String issuer) {
        byte[] bytes = secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (bytes.length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("JWT_SECRET must be at least 32 characters");
        }
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(bytes);
        this.issuer = issuer;
    }
    public Optional<AuthenticatedUser> parse(String token) {
        try {
            Jws<Claims> jws = Jwts.parser()
                    .verifyWith(key)
                    .requireIssuer(issuer)
                    .build()
                    .parseSignedClaims(token);
            if (!"HS256".equals(jws.getHeader().getAlgorithm())) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            Claims c = jws.getPayload();
            String role = c.get("role", String.class);
            if (!"CUSTOMER".equals(role) && !"ADMIN".equals(role)) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            return Optional.of(new AuthenticatedUser(
                    Long.parseLong(c.getSubject()), c.get("email", String.class), role));
        } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/RestAccessDeniedHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
@Component
public class RestAccessDeniedHandler implements AccessDeniedHandler {
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public RestAccessDeniedHandler(SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void handle(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AccessDeniedException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN",
                "You do not have permission to perform this action.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/RestAuthenticationEntryPoint.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
@Component
public class RestAuthenticationEntryPoint implements AuthenticationEntryPoint {
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public RestAuthenticationEntryPoint(SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void commence(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AuthenticationException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/security/SecurityErrorWriter.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.security;
@Component
public class SecurityErrorWriter {
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    public SecurityErrorWriter(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }
    public void write(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res,
                      HttpStatus status, String code, String message) throws IOException {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(), status.getReasonPhrase(), code, message, req.getRequestURI(), List.of());
        res.setStatus(status.value());
        res.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE);
        res.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        objectMapper.writeValue(res.getWriter(), body);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/service/CategoryService.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.service;
@Service
public class CategoryService {
    private static final Sort BY_NAME = Sort.by(Sort.Order.asc("name").ignoreCase(), Sort.Order.asc("id"));
    private final CategoryRepository repository;
    private final CatalogMapper mapper;
    public CategoryService(CategoryRepository repository, CatalogMapper mapper) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CategoryResponse> listActive() {
        return repository.findByActiveTrue(BY_NAME).stream().map(mapper::toCategory).toList();
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CategoryResponse> listAll() {
        return repository.findAll(BY_NAME).stream().map(mapper::toCategory).toList();
    }
    public CategoryResponse create(CategoryRequest req) {
        String name = req.name().trim();
        if (repository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)) {
            throw exists();
        }
        Category category = new Category(name, QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.description()),
                QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.imageUrl()));
        try {
            return mapper.toCategory(repository.saveAndFlush(category));
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException race) {
            throw exists();
        }
    }
    public CategoryResponse update(long id, CategoryRequest req) {
        Category category = find(id);
        String name = req.name().trim();
        if (repository.existsByNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(name, id)) {
            throw exists();
        }
        category.update(name, QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.description()), QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.imageUrl()));
        try {
            return mapper.toCategory(repository.saveAndFlush(category));
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException race) {
            throw exists();
        }
    }
        @Transactional
    public CategoryResponse setActive(long id, boolean active) {
        Category category = find(id);
        category.setActive(active);
        return mapper.toCategory(repository.saveAndFlush(category));
    }
    private Category find(long id) {
        return repository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Category not found."));
    }
    private ApiException exists() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS",
                "A category with this name already exists.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/service/ProductService.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.service;
@Service
public class ProductService {
    private static final Sort.Order BY_NAME = Sort.Order.asc("name").ignoreCase();
    private static final Sort.Order BY_ID = Sort.Order.asc("id");
    private final ProductRepository productRepository;
    private final CategoryRepository categoryRepository;
    private final InventoryService inventoryService;
    private final CatalogMapper mapper;
    public ProductService(ProductRepository productRepository, CategoryRepository categoryRepository,
                          InventoryService inventoryService, CatalogMapper mapper) {
        this.productRepository = productRepository;
        this.categoryRepository = categoryRepository;
        this.inventoryService = inventoryService;
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ProductResponse> listPublic(String q, Long categoryId, String sort, int page, int size) {
        Specification<Product> spec = Specification.where(ProductSpecifications.visibleToCustomers())
                .and(QueryUtils.hasText(q) ? ProductSpecifications.nameLike(QueryUtils.likePattern(q)) : null)
                .and(ProductSpecifications.inCategory(categoryId));
        Page<Product> result = productRepository.findAll(spec,
                QueryUtils.pageable(page, size, parseSort(sort)));
        Map<Long, Integer> stock = inventoryService.quantities(result.map(Product::getId).getContent());
        return PageResponse.of(result.map(p -> mapper.toProduct(p, stock.getOrDefault(p.getId(), 0))));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ProductResponse getPublic(long id) {
        Product p = productRepository.findWithCategoryById(id)
                .filter(x -> x.isActive() && x.getCategory().isActive())
                .orElseThrow(this::productNotFound);
        return mapper.toProduct(p, inventoryService.quantities(List.of(id)).getOrDefault(id, 0));
    }
    private Sort parseSort(String sort) {
        String s = sort == null || sort.isBlank() ? "name" : sort;
        return switch (s) {
            case "name" -> Sort.by(BY_NAME, BY_ID);
            case "price_asc" -> Sort.by(Sort.Order.asc("price"), BY_ID);
            case "price_desc" -> Sort.by(Sort.Order.desc("price"), BY_ID);
            default -> throw QueryUtils.invalid("sort", "Must be one of: name, price_asc, price_desc");
        };
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<AdminProductResponse> listAdmin(String q, Long categoryId, Boolean active,
                                                        int page, int size) {
        Specification<Product> spec = Specification
                .where(QueryUtils.hasText(q) ? ProductSpecifications.nameLike(QueryUtils.likePattern(q)) : null)
                .and(ProductSpecifications.inCategory(categoryId))
                .and(ProductSpecifications.hasActive(active));
        Page<Product> result = productRepository.findAll(spec,
                QueryUtils.pageable(page, size, Sort.by(BY_NAME, BY_ID)));
        Map<Long, Integer> stock = inventoryService.quantities(result.map(Product::getId).getContent());
        return PageResponse.of(result.map(p -> mapper.toAdminProduct(p, stock.getOrDefault(p.getId(), 0))));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AdminProductResponse getAdmin(long id) {
        return toAdmin(find(id));
    }
    @Transactional
    public AdminProductResponse create(ProductCreateRequest req) {
        Category category = findCategory(req.categoryId());
        Product product = productRepository.save(new Product(category, req.name().trim(),
                QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.description()), req.price(), req.unit().trim(),
                QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.imageUrl())));
        int quantity = req.initialQuantity() == null ? 0 : req.initialQuantity();
        inventoryService.create(product.getId(), quantity);
        return mapper.toAdminProduct(product, quantity);
    }
        @Transactional
    public AdminProductResponse update(long id, ProductUpdateRequest req) {
        Product product = find(id);
        product.updateDetails(findCategory(req.categoryId()), req.name().trim(),
                QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.description()), req.unit().trim(),
                QueryUtils.blankToNull(req.imageUrl()));
        return toAdmin(productRepository.saveAndFlush(product));
    }
    @Transactional
    public AdminProductResponse updatePrice(long id, PriceRequest req) {
        Product product = find(id);
        product.changePrice(req.price());
        return toAdmin(productRepository.saveAndFlush(product));
    }
    @Transactional
    public AdminProductResponse setActive(long id, boolean active) {
        Product product = find(id);
        product.setActive(active);
        return toAdmin(productRepository.saveAndFlush(product));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<InternalProductSnapshot> snapshots(List<Long> ids) {
        List<Long> distinct = ids.stream().distinct().toList();
        if (distinct.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }
        Map<Long, Integer> stock = inventoryService.quantities(distinct);
        return productRepository.findWithCategoryByIdIn(distinct).stream()
                .map(p -> new InternalProductSnapshot(p.getId(), p.getName(), p.getUnit(), p.getImageUrl(),
                        p.getPrice(), p.isActive() && p.getCategory().isActive(),
                        stock.getOrDefault(p.getId(), 0)))
                .toList();
    }
    private AdminProductResponse toAdmin(Product p) {
        return mapper.toAdminProduct(p, inventoryService.quantities(List.of(p.getId())).getOrDefault(p.getId(), 0));
    }
    private Product find(long id) {
        return productRepository.findWithCategoryById(id).orElseThrow(this::productNotFound);
    }
    private Category findCategory(long id) {
        return categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Category not found."));
    }
    private ApiException productNotFound() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Product not found.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/catalog/service/QueryUtils.java =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.service;
public final class QueryUtils {
    public static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    private QueryUtils() { }
    public static Pageable pageable(int page, int size, Sort sort) {
        if (page < 0) {
            throw invalid("page", "Must be 0 or greater");
        }
        if (size < 1 || size > MAX_PAGE_SIZE) {
            throw invalid("size", "Must be between 1 and " + MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        }
        return PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);
    }
    public static String likePattern(String q) {
        if (q == null || q.isBlank()) {
            return "%";
        }
        String escaped = q.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
                .replace("!", "!!").replace("%", "!%").replace("_", "!_");
        return "%" + escaped + "%";
    }
    public static boolean hasText(String q) {
        return q != null && !q.isBlank();
    }
    public static String blankToNull(String s) {
        return s == null || s.isBlank() ? null : s.trim();
    }
    public static ApiException invalid(String field, String message) {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "Validation failed.",
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(field, message)));
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__init.sql =====
CREATE TABLE categories (
    id          BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    name        VARCHAR(80)  NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(255),
    image_url   VARCHAR(500),
    active      BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at  TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_categories_name_lower ON categories (lower(name));
CREATE TABLE products (
    id          BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    category_id BIGINT         NOT NULL,
    name        VARCHAR(150)   NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(1000),
    price       NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    unit        VARCHAR(30)    NOT NULL,
    image_url   VARCHAR(500),
    active      BOOLEAN        NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ    NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at  TIMESTAMPTZ    NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT fk_products_category FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES categories (id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_products_price CHECK (price > 0)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_products_category_id ON products (category_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_products_name_lower ON products (lower(name));
CREATE TABLE inventory (
    product_id BIGINT      NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    quantity   INTEGER     NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT fk_inventory_product FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT ck_inventory_quantity CHECK (quantity >= 0)
);

===== quickcartbackend/catalog-service/src/test/java/com/quickcart/catalog/exception/GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ja =====
package com.quickcart.catalog.exception;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.containsString;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.not;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.AutoConfigureMockMvc;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.WebMvcTest;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
@WebMvcTest
@AutoConfigureMockMvc(addFilters = false)
@Import(GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ProbeController.class)
class GlobalExceptionHandlerTest {
    @Autowired
    MockMvc mockMvc;
    @Test
    void beanValidationFailure_returns400WithFieldDetails() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\"\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value(400))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.path").value("/probe/validate"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.timestamp").exists())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details[0].field").value("name"));
    }
    @Test
    void malformedJson_returns400WithEmptyDetailsArray() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{not json"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details").isArray());
    }
    @Test
    void apiException_usesItsOwnStatusAndCode() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/conflict"))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.error").value("Conflict"));
    }
    @Test
    void accessDenied_returns403Forbidden() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/forbidden"))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("FORBIDDEN"));
    }
    @Test
    void unexpectedError_returns500WithoutLeakingInternals() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/boom"))
                .andExpect(status().isInternalServerError())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INTERNAL_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message", not(containsString("secret internal detail"))));
    }
    @Test
    void unknownPath_returns404NotFound() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/does-not-exist"))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
    }
    @RestController
    @RequestMapping("/probe")
    static class ProbeController {
        record Body(@NotBlank String name) { }
        @PostMapping("/validate")
        void validate(@Valid @RequestBody Body body) { }
        @GetMapping("/conflict")
        void conflict() {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS", "Category already exists.");
        }
        @GetMapping("/forbidden")
        void forbidden() {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("nope");
        }
        @GetMapping("/boom")
        void boom() {
            throw new IllegalStateException("secret internal detail");
        }
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/infra/postgres/init/01-create-databases.sql =====
\getenv user_pw    USER_DB_PASSWORD
\getenv catalog_pw CATALOG_DB_PASSWORD
\getenv order_pw   ORDER_DB_PASSWORD
CREATE ROLE user_svc    LOGIN PASSWORD :'user_pw';
CREATE ROLE catalog_svc LOGIN PASSWORD :'catalog_pw';
CREATE ROLE order_svc   LOGIN PASSWORD :'order_pw';
CREATE DATABASE user_db    OWNER user_svc;
CREATE DATABASE catalog_db OWNER catalog_svc;
CREATE DATABASE order_db   OWNER order_svc;
REVOKE CONNECT ON DATABASE user_db    FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE CONNECT ON DATABASE catalog_db FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE CONNECT ON DATABASE order_db   FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE user_db    TO user_svc;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE catalog_db TO catalog_svc;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE order_db   TO order_svc;

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/client/CatalogClient.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.client;
@Component
public class CatalogClient {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CatalogClient.class);
    private static final int MAX_IDS_PER_CALL = 50;
    private static final int ATTEMPTS = 2;
    private final RestClient restClient;
    public CatalogClient(@Qualifier("catalogRestClient") RestClient restClient) {
        this.restClient = restClient;
    }
    public Map<Long, CatalogProduct> getProducts(Collection<Long> productIds) {
        List<Long> distinct = productIds.stream().distinct().toList();
        Map<Long, CatalogProduct> result = new HashMap<>();
        for (int from = 0; from < distinct.size(); from += MAX_IDS_PER_CALL) {
            List<Long> chunk = new ArrayList<>(
                    distinct.subList(from, Math.min(from + MAX_IDS_PER_CALL, distinct.size())));
            for (CatalogProduct p : fetchBatch(chunk)) {
                result.put(p.id(), p);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
    public Optional<CatalogProduct> findProduct(long productId) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(getProducts(List.of(productId)).get(productId));
    }
    private List<CatalogProduct> fetchBatch(List<Long> ids) {
        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= ATTEMPTS; attempt++) {
            try {
                BatchResponse response = restClient.post()
                        .uri("/internal/products/batch")
                        .body(new BatchRequest(ids))
                        .retrieve()
                        .body(BatchResponse.class);
                return response == null || response.products() == null ? List.of() : response.products();
            } catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {
                throw mapClientError(e);
            } catch (ResourceAccessException | HttpServerErrorException | IllegalStateException e) {
                log.warn("catalog-service I1 attempt {}/{} failed: {}", attempt, ATTEMPTS, e.getMessage());
            } catch (RestClientException e) {
                log.error("catalog-service I1 returned an unusable response", e);
                throw unavailable();
            }
        }
        throw unavailable();
    }
    private ApiException mapClientError(HttpClientErrorException e) {
        int status = e.getStatusCode().value();
        HttpStatus resolved = HttpStatus.resolve(status);
        ErrorBody body = null;
        try {
            body = e.getResponseBodyAs(ErrorBody.class);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
        }
        if (status == 401 || status == 403 || resolved == null || body == null || body.code() == null) {
            log.error("catalog-service rejected an internal call with HTTP {}", status);
            return unavailable();
        }
        List<ErrorDetail> details = body.details() == null ? List.of()
                : body.details().stream().map(d -> new ErrorDetail(d.field(), d.message())).toList();
        return new ApiException(resolved, body.code(),
                body.message() == null ? "The request could not be completed." : body.message(), details);
    }
    private ApiException unavailable() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
                "A dependent service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.");
    }
    public List<ReservedItem> reserve(List<StockItem> items) {
        try {
            ReserveResponse response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/internal/inventory/reserve")
                    .body(new StockRequest(items))
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(ReserveResponse.class);
            if (response == null || response.items() == null) {
                log.error("catalog-service reserve returned an empty body");
                throw unavailable();
            }
            return response.items();
        } catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {
            throw mapClientError(e);
        } catch (ResourceAccessException | HttpServerErrorException | IllegalStateException e) {
            log.warn("catalog-service reserve failed (not retried): {}", e.getMessage());
            throw unavailable();
        } catch (RestClientException e) {
            log.error("catalog-service reserve returned an unusable response", e);
            throw unavailable();
        }
    }
    public void release(List<StockItem> items) {
        try {
            restClient.post()
                    .uri("/internal/inventory/release")
                    .body(new StockRequest(items))
                    .retrieve()
                    .toBodilessEntity();
        } catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {
            throw mapClientError(e);
        } catch (ResourceAccessException | HttpServerErrorException | IllegalStateException e) {
            log.warn("catalog-service release failed (not retried): {}", e.getMessage());
            throw unavailable();
        } catch (RestClientException e) {
            log.error("catalog-service release returned an unusable response", e);
            throw unavailable();
        }
    }
    public record BatchRequest(List<Long> productIds) { }
    public record BatchResponse(List<CatalogProduct> products) { }
    public record ErrorBody(String code, String message, List<ErrorBodyDetail> details) { }
    public record ErrorBodyDetail(String field, String message) { }
    public record StockItem(Long productId, int quantity) { }
    public record StockRequest(List<StockItem> items) { }
    public record ReservedItem(Long productId, String name, String unit, BigDecimal unitPrice, int quantity) { }
    public record ReserveResponse(List<ReservedItem> items) { }
}

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/config/CatalogClientConfig.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.config;
@Configuration
@EnableConfigurationProperties({OrderProperties.class, CatalogClientProperties.class})
public class CatalogClientConfig {
    @Bean
    @LoadBalanced
    RestClient.Builder catalogRestClientBuilder() {
        return RestClient.builder();
    }
    @Bean
    RestClient catalogRestClient(@LoadBalanced RestClient.Builder builder,
                                 CatalogClientProperties props,
                                 @Value("${quickcart.internal.api-key}") String internalApiKey) {
        if (internalApiKey == null || internalApiKey.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("INTERNAL_API_KEY must be set");
        }
        SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory factory = new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
        factory.setConnectTimeout(props.connectTimeoutMs());
        factory.setReadTimeout(props.readTimeoutMs());
        return builder
                .baseUrl("http://catalog-service")
                .requestFactory(factory)
                .defaultHeader("X-Internal-Api-Key", internalApiKey)
                .build();
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/config/SecurityConfig.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.config;
@Configuration
@EnableMethodSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http,
                                            JwtVerifier verifier,
                                            RestAuthenticationEntryPoint entryPoint,
                                            RestAccessDeniedHandler accessDeniedHandler) throws Exception {
        http
                .csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .httpBasic(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .formLogin(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .sessionManagement(s -> s.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .exceptionHandling(e -> e
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(entryPoint)
                        .accessDeniedHandler(accessDeniedHandler))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(a -> a
                        .requestMatchers("/actuator/health", "/actuator/health/**", "/actuator/info").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/swagger-ui.html", "/swagger-ui/**", "/v3/api-docs/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/admin/**").hasRole("ADMIN")
                        .requestMatchers("/api/v1/cart/**", "/api/v1/orders/**").hasRole("CUSTOMER")
                        .anyRequest().authenticated())
                .addFilterBefore(new JwtAuthenticationFilter(verifier), UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class);
        return http.build();
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/controller/AdminOrderController.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/admin/orders")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
public class AdminOrderController {
    private final OrderService service;
    public AdminOrderController(OrderService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "R1 All orders")
    @GetMapping
    public PageResponse<AdminOrderSummaryResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) OrderStatus status,
                                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
                                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        return service.listAll(status, page, size);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "R2 Any order detail")
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public AdminOrderResponse get(@PathVariable long id) {
        return service.getAny(id);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "R3 Move order status")
    @PatchMapping("/{id}/status")
    public OrderResponse status(@PathVariable long id, @Valid @RequestBody UpdateOrderStatusRequest request) {
        return service.updateStatus(id, request.status());
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/controller/CartController.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/cart")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('CUSTOMER')")
public class CartController {
    private final CartService service;
    public CartController(CartService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O1 View cart (enriched)")
    @GetMapping
    public CartResponse get(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user) {
        return service.getCart(user.id());
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O2 Add item (adds to existing quantity)")
    @PostMapping("/items")
    public CartResponse add(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user,
                            @Valid @RequestBody AddCartItemRequest request) {
        return service.addItem(user.id(), request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O3 Set item quantity (absolute)")
    @PutMapping("/items/{productId}")
    public CartResponse set(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user, @PathVariable long productId,
                            @Valid @RequestBody SetCartItemQuantityRequest request) {
        return service.setQuantity(user.id(), productId, request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O4 Remove item")
    @DeleteMapping("/items/{productId}")
    public CartResponse remove(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user, @PathVariable long productId) {
        return service.removeItem(user.id(), productId);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O5 Clear cart")
    @DeleteMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void clear(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user) {
        service.clear(user.id());
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/controller/OrderController.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.controller;
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/orders")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('CUSTOMER')")
public class OrderController {
    private final OrderService service;
    public OrderController(OrderService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O6 Place order from cart")
    @PostMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public OrderResponse place(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user,
                               @Valid @RequestBody PlaceOrderRequest request) {
        return service.placeOrder(user.id(), request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O7 My orders (newest first)")
    @GetMapping
    public PageResponse<OrderSummaryResponse> list(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user,
                                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
                                                   @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        return service.listMine(user.id(), page, size);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "O8 My order detail")
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public OrderResponse get(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user, @PathVariable long id) {
        return service.getMine(user.id(), id);
    }
}

public record DeliveryAddressRequest(
        @NotBlank @Size(min = 2, max = 100) String recipientName,
        @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "^[6-9][0-9]{9}$", message = "Invalid phone number") String phone,
        @NotBlank @Size(min = 3, max = 150) String line1,
        @Size(max = 150) String line2,
        @NotBlank @Size(min = 2, max = 60) String city,
        @NotBlank @Size(min = 2, max = 60) String state,
        @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "^[1-9][0-9]{5}$", message = "Invalid pincode") String pincode) {
}

    public static <T> PageResponse<T> of(Page<T> p) {

    }

@MappedSuperclass
@EntityListeners(AuditingEntityListener.class)
public abstract class BaseEntity {
    @CreatedDate
    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;
    @LastModifiedDate
    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant updatedAt;
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/entity/Cart.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.entity;
@Entity
@Table(name = "carts")
public class Cart {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private Long userId;
    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant updatedAt;
    protected Cart() { }
    public void touch() {
        this.updatedAt = Instant.now();
    }
    public Long getId() { return id; }
    public Long getUserId() { return userId; }
    public Instant getUpdatedAt() { return updatedAt; }
}

package com.quickcart.order.entity;
@Entity
@Table(name = "cart_items")
public class CartItem {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "cart_id", nullable = false)
    private Cart cart;
    @Column(name = "product_id", nullable = false)
    private Long productId;
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private int quantity;
    protected CartItem() { }
    public CartItem(Cart cart, Long productId, int quantity) {
        this.cart = cart;
        this.productId = productId;
        this.quantity = quantity;
    }
    public void setQuantity(int quantity) { this.quantity = quantity; }
    public Long getId() { return id; }
    public Long getProductId() { return productId; }
    public int getQuantity() { return quantity; }
}
