===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/entity/Order.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.entity;
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "orders")
public class Order extends BaseEntity {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @Column(name = "order_number", nullable = false, updatable = false, length = 20)
    private String orderNumber;
    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private Long userId;
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private OrderStatus status;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal subtotal;
    @Column(name = "delivery_fee", nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal deliveryFee;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal total;
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "payment_method", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PaymentMethod paymentMethod;
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "payment_status", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PaymentStatus paymentStatus;
    @Embedded
    private DeliveryAddress deliveryAddress;
    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "order", cascade = CascadeType.ALL, orphanRemoval = true)
    @OrderBy("id ASC")
    private List<OrderItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
    @Builder
    private Order(String orderNumber, Long userId, OrderStatus status, BigDecimal subtotal,
                  BigDecimal deliveryFee, BigDecimal total, PaymentMethod paymentMethod,
                  PaymentStatus paymentStatus, DeliveryAddress deliveryAddress) {
        this.orderNumber = orderNumber;
        this.userId = userId;
        this.status = status;
        this.subtotal = subtotal;
        this.deliveryFee = deliveryFee;
        this.total = total;
        this.paymentMethod = paymentMethod;
        this.paymentStatus = paymentStatus;
        this.deliveryAddress = deliveryAddress;
    }
    public void addItem(OrderItem item) {
        items.add(item);
    }
    public void changeStatus(OrderStatus next) {
        this.status = next;
    }
    public void markPaid() {
        this.paymentStatus = PaymentStatus.PAID;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/entity/OrderItem.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.entity;
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "order_items")
public class OrderItem {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "order_id", nullable = false)
    private Order order;
    @Column(name = "product_id", nullable = false)
    private Long productId;
    @Column(name = "product_name", nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String productName;
    @Column(name = "unit_price", nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal unitPrice;
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private int quantity;
    @Column(name = "line_total", nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal lineTotal;
    public OrderItem(Order order, Long productId, String productName, BigDecimal unitPrice,
                     int quantity, BigDecimal lineTotal) {
        this.order = order;
        this.productId = productId;
        this.productName = productName;
        this.unitPrice = unitPrice;
        this.quantity = quantity;
        this.lineTotal = lineTotal;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/entity/OrderStatus.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.entity;
public enum OrderStatus {
    PLACED, CONFIRMED, PACKED, OUT_FOR_DELIVERY, DELIVERED, CANCELLED;
    public Set<OrderStatus> allowedNext() {
        return switch (this) {
            case PLACED -> EnumSet.of(CONFIRMED, CANCELLED);
            case CONFIRMED -> EnumSet.of(PACKED, CANCELLED);
            case PACKED -> EnumSet.of(OUT_FOR_DELIVERY);
            case OUT_FOR_DELIVERY -> EnumSet.of(DELIVERED);
            case DELIVERED, CANCELLED -> EnumSet.noneOf(OrderStatus.class);
        };
    }
    public boolean canMoveTo(OrderStatus next) {
        return allowedNext().contains(next);
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/exception/ApiException.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.exception;
public class ApiException extends RuntimeException {
    private final HttpStatus status;
    private final String code;
    private final List<ErrorDetail> details;
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message) {
        this(status, code, message, List.of());
    }
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        super(message);
        this.status = status;
        this.code = code;
        this.details = details;
    }
    public HttpStatus getStatus() { return status; }
    public String getCode() { return code; }
    public List<ErrorDetail> getDetails() { return details; }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/exception/GlobalExceptionHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.exception;
@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionHandler.class);
    @ExceptionHandler(ApiException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleApi(ApiException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(ex.getStatus(), ex.getCode(), ex.getMessage(), req, ex.getDetails());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleBeanValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException ex,
                                                              HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getBindingResult().getAllErrors().stream()
                .map(e -> new ErrorDetail(
                        e instanceof FieldError fe ? fe.getField() : e.getObjectName(),
                        e.getDefaultMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleConstraint(ConstraintViolationException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getConstraintViolations().stream()
                .map(v -> new ErrorDetail(lastNode(v.getPropertyPath()), v.getMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnreadable(HttpMessageNotReadableException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Malformed request body.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleTypeMismatch(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Invalid request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getName(), "Invalid value")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleMissingParam(MissingServletRequestParameterException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Missing request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getParameterName(), "Required")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler({HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class, HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException.class})
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnsupported(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Unsupported request.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(NoResourceFoundException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleNoResource(NoResourceFoundException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Resource not found.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AuthenticationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAuthentication(AuthenticationException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AccessDeniedException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAccessDenied(AccessDeniedException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN", "You do not have permission to perform this action.",
                req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnexpected(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        log.error("Unexpected error on {} {}", req.getMethod(), req.getRequestURI(), ex);
        return build(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.", req, List.of());
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> validation(String message, HttpServletRequest req,
                                                     List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", message, req, details);
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> build(HttpStatus status, String code, String message,
                                                HttpServletRequest req, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(),
                status.getReasonPhrase(),
                code,
                message,
                req.getRequestURI(),
                details == null ? List.of() : details);
        return ResponseEntity.status(status).body(body);
    }
    private String lastNode(jakarta.validation.Path path) {
        return StreamSupport.stream(path.spliterator(), false)
                .reduce((first, second) -> second)
                .map(Object::toString)
                .orElse("");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/mapper/OrderMapper.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.mapper;
@Component
public class OrderMapper {
    public OrderResponse toOrder(Order o) {
        return new OrderResponse(o.getId(), o.getOrderNumber(), o.getStatus(), o.getPaymentMethod(),
                o.getPaymentStatus(), o.getSubtotal(), o.getDeliveryFee(), o.getTotal(),
                address(o.getDeliveryAddress()), items(o), secs(o.getCreatedAt()), secs(o.getUpdatedAt()));
    }
    public AdminOrderResponse toAdminOrder(Order o) {
        return new AdminOrderResponse(o.getId(), o.getUserId(), o.getOrderNumber(), o.getStatus(),
                o.getPaymentMethod(), o.getPaymentStatus(), o.getSubtotal(), o.getDeliveryFee(), o.getTotal(),
                address(o.getDeliveryAddress()), items(o), secs(o.getCreatedAt()), secs(o.getUpdatedAt()));
    }
    public OrderSummaryResponse toSummary(Order o, int itemCount) {
        return new OrderSummaryResponse(o.getId(), o.getOrderNumber(), o.getStatus(), o.getTotal(),
                itemCount, secs(o.getCreatedAt()));
    }
    public AdminOrderSummaryResponse toAdminSummary(Order o, int itemCount) {
        return new AdminOrderSummaryResponse(o.getId(), o.getOrderNumber(), o.getUserId(),
                o.getDeliveryAddress().getRecipientName(), o.getStatus(), o.getPaymentStatus(),
                o.getTotal(), itemCount, secs(o.getCreatedAt()));
    }
    private List<OrderItemResponse> items(Order o) {
        return o.getItems().stream()
                .map(i -> new OrderItemResponse(i.getProductId(), i.getProductName(), i.getUnitPrice(),
                        i.getQuantity(), i.getLineTotal()))
                .toList();
    }
    private DeliveryAddressResponse address(DeliveryAddress a) {
        return new DeliveryAddressResponse(a.getRecipientName(), a.getPhone(), a.getLine1(), a.getLine2(),
                a.getCity(), a.getState(), a.getPincode());
    }
    private Instant secs(Instant t) {
        return t == null ? null : t.truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.security;
public class JwtAuthenticationFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {
    private static final String PREFIX = "Bearer ";
    private final JwtVerifier verifier;
    public JwtAuthenticationFilter(JwtVerifier verifier) {
        this.verifier = verifier;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response,
                                    FilterChain chain) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String header = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (header != null && header.startsWith(PREFIX)) {
            verifier.parse(header.substring(PREFIX.length()).trim()).ifPresent(user -> {
                var auth = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                        user, null, List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_" + user.role())));
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(auth);
            });
        }
        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/security/JwtVerifier.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.security;
@Component
public class JwtVerifier {
    private final SecretKey key;
    private final String issuer;
    public JwtVerifier(@Value("${quickcart.jwt.secret}") String secret,
                       @Value("${quickcart.jwt.issuer}") String issuer) {
        byte[] bytes = secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (bytes.length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("JWT_SECRET must be at least 32 characters");
        }
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(bytes);
        this.issuer = issuer;
    }
    public Optional<AuthenticatedUser> parse(String token) {
        try {
            Jws<Claims> jws = Jwts.parser()
                    .verifyWith(key)
                    .requireIssuer(issuer)
                    .build()
                    .parseSignedClaims(token);
            if (!"HS256".equals(jws.getHeader().getAlgorithm())) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            Claims c = jws.getPayload();
            String role = c.get("role", String.class);
            if (!"CUSTOMER".equals(role) && !"ADMIN".equals(role)) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            return Optional.of(new AuthenticatedUser(
                    Long.parseLong(c.getSubject()), c.get("email", String.class), role));
        } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}

@Component
public class RestAccessDeniedHandler implements AccessDeniedHandler {

        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void handle(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AccessDeniedException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN",
                "You do not have permission to perform this action.");
    }

public class RestAuthenticationEntryPoint implements AuthenticationEntryPoint {
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public RestAuthenticationEntryPoint(SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void commence(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AuthenticationException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.");
    }

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/security/SecurityErrorWriter.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.security;
@Component
public class SecurityErrorWriter {
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    public SecurityErrorWriter(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }
    public void write(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res,
                      HttpStatus status, String code, String message) throws IOException {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(), status.getReasonPhrase(), code, message, req.getRequestURI(), List.of());
        res.setStatus(status.value());
        res.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE);
        res.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        objectMapper.writeValue(res.getWriter(), body);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/service/CartService.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.service;
@Service
public class CartService {
    private static final BigDecimal ZERO = BigDecimal.ZERO.setScale(2);
    private final CartRepository cartRepository;
    private final CartItemRepository cartItemRepository;
    private final CatalogClient catalogClient;
    private final OrderProperties props;
    private final TransactionTemplate tx;
    public CartService(CartRepository cartRepository, CartItemRepository cartItemRepository,
                       CatalogClient catalogClient, OrderProperties props, TransactionTemplate tx) {
        this.cartRepository = cartRepository;
        this.cartItemRepository = cartItemRepository;
        this.catalogClient = catalogClient;
        this.props = props;
        this.tx = tx;
    }
    public CartResponse getCart(long userId) {
        List<CartItem> items = cartItemRepository.findByCartUserIdOrderByIdAsc(userId);
        Map<Long, CatalogProduct> products = items.isEmpty() ? Map.of()
                : catalogClient.getProducts(items.stream().map(CartItem::getProductId).toList());
        return build(items, products);
    }
    public CartResponse addItem(long userId, AddCartItemRequest req) {
        int max = props.maxCartLineQuantity();
        long productId = req.productId();
        if (req.quantity() > max) {
            throw tooMany(max);
        }
        CatalogProduct product = catalogClient.findProduct(productId)
                .filter(CatalogProduct::active)
                .orElseThrow(this::productNotFound);
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            Cart cart = lockOrCreateCart(userId);
            CartItem item = cartItemRepository.findByCartIdAndProductId(cart.getId(), productId).orElse(null);
            int resulting = (item == null ? 0 : item.getQuantity()) + req.quantity();
            if (resulting > max) {
                throw tooMany(max);
            }
            if (resulting > product.availableQuantity()) {
                throw insufficient(productId, resulting, product.availableQuantity());
            }
            if (item == null) {
                cartItemRepository.save(new CartItem(cart, productId, resulting));
            } else {
                item.setQuantity(resulting);
            }
            cart.touch();
        });
        return getCart(userId);
    }
    public CartResponse setQuantity(long userId, long productId, SetCartItemQuantityRequest req) {
        int quantity = req.quantity();
        if (quantity > props.maxCartLineQuantity()) {
            throw tooMany(props.maxCartLineQuantity());
        }
        if (!cartItemRepository.existsByCartUserIdAndProductId(userId, productId)) {
            throw itemNotFound();
        }
        CatalogProduct product = catalogClient.findProduct(productId)
                .filter(CatalogProduct::active)
                .orElseThrow(this::productNotFound);
        if (quantity > product.availableQuantity()) {
            throw insufficient(productId, quantity, product.availableQuantity());
        }
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            Cart cart = cartRepository.findByUserIdForUpdate(userId).orElseThrow(this::itemNotFound);
            CartItem item = cartItemRepository.findByCartIdAndProductId(cart.getId(), productId)
                    .orElseThrow(this::itemNotFound);
            item.setQuantity(quantity);
            cart.touch();
        });
        return getCart(userId);
    }
    public CartResponse removeItem(long userId, long productId) {
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            Cart cart = cartRepository.findByUserIdForUpdate(userId).orElseThrow(this::itemNotFound);
            CartItem item = cartItemRepository.findByCartIdAndProductId(cart.getId(), productId)
                    .orElseThrow(this::itemNotFound);
            cartItemRepository.delete(item);
            cart.touch();
        });
        return getCart(userId);
    }
    public void clear(long userId) {
        tx.executeWithoutResult(status ->
                cartRepository.findByUserIdForUpdate(userId).ifPresent(cart -> {
                    cartItemRepository.deleteAllByCartId(cart.getId());
                    cart.touch();
                }));
    }
    private CartResponse build(List<CartItem> items, Map<Long, CatalogProduct> products) {
        List<CartLineResponse> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        BigDecimal subtotal = ZERO;
        int itemCount = 0;
        boolean anyUnavailable = false;
        for (CartItem item : items) {
            CatalogProduct p = products.get(item.getProductId());
            int qty = item.getQuantity();
            if (p == null) {
                lines.add(new CartLineResponse(item.getProductId(), "Unavailable item", null, null,
                        ZERO, qty, ZERO, 0, false));
                anyUnavailable = true;
                continue;
            }
            boolean available = p.active() && p.availableQuantity() >= qty;
            if (available) {
                BigDecimal unitPrice = p.price().setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
                BigDecimal lineTotal = unitPrice.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(qty)).setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
                subtotal = subtotal.add(lineTotal);
                itemCount += qty;
                lines.add(new CartLineResponse(p.id(), p.name(), p.unit(), p.imageUrl(),
                        unitPrice, qty, lineTotal, p.availableQuantity(), true));
            } else {
                anyUnavailable = true;
                lines.add(new CartLineResponse(p.id(), p.name(), p.unit(), p.imageUrl(),
                        ZERO, qty, ZERO, p.availableQuantity(), false));
            }
        }
        BigDecimal deliveryFee;
        if (itemCount == 0 || subtotal.compareTo(props.freeDeliveryThreshold()) >= 0) {
            deliveryFee = ZERO;
        } else {
            deliveryFee = props.deliveryFee().setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        }
        return new CartResponse(lines, itemCount, subtotal, deliveryFee,
                subtotal.add(deliveryFee), anyUnavailable);
    }
    private Cart lockOrCreateCart(long userId) {
        cartRepository.insertIfAbsent(userId);
        return cartRepository.findByUserIdForUpdate(userId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Cart row missing after insert"));
    }
    private ApiException productNotFound() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND", "Product not found.");
    }
    private ApiException itemNotFound() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND", "Item is not in your cart.");
    }
    private ApiException insufficient(long productId, int requested, int available) {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "INSUFFICIENT_STOCK",
                "Some items are not available in the requested quantity.",
                List.of(new ErrorDetail("productId:" + productId,
                        "Requested " + requested + ", available " + available)));
    }
    private ApiException tooMany(int max) {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "Validation failed.",
                List.of(new ErrorDetail("quantity", "Cannot exceed " + max + " per item")));
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/service/OrderService.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.service;
@Service
public class OrderService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderService.class);
    private static final Sort NEWEST_FIRST = Sort.by(Sort.Order.desc("createdAt"), Sort.Order.desc("id"));
    private static final BigDecimal ZERO = BigDecimal.ZERO.setScale(2);
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final OrderItemRepository orderItemRepository;
    private final CartRepository cartRepository;
    private final CartItemRepository cartItemRepository;
    private final CartService cartService;
    private final CatalogClient catalogClient;
    private final OrderProperties props;
    private final OrderMapper mapper;
    private final TransactionTemplate tx;
    public OrderService(OrderRepository orderRepository, OrderItemRepository orderItemRepository,
                        CartRepository cartRepository, CartItemRepository cartItemRepository,
                        CartService cartService, CatalogClient catalogClient, OrderProperties props,
                        OrderMapper mapper, TransactionTemplate tx) {
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
        this.orderItemRepository = orderItemRepository;
        this.cartRepository = cartRepository;
        this.cartItemRepository = cartItemRepository;
        this.cartService = cartService;
        this.catalogClient = catalogClient;
        this.props = props;
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.tx = tx;
    }
    public OrderResponse placeOrder(long userId, PlaceOrderRequest req) {
        CartResponse cart = cartService.getCart(userId);
        if (cart.items().isEmpty()) {
            throw cartEmpty();
        }
        if (cart.hasUnavailableItems()) {
            throw unavailableItems(cart);
        }
        Map<Long, Integer> wanted = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        cart.items().forEach(l -> wanted.merge(l.productId(), l.quantity(), Integer::sum));
        List<StockItem> stock = wanted.entrySet().stream()
                .map(e -> new StockItem(e.getKey(), e.getValue())).toList();
        List<ReservedItem> reserved = catalogClient.reserve(stock);
        try {
            return tx.execute(status -> saveOrder(userId, req, wanted, reserved));
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            releaseQuietly(stock);
            throw e;
        }
    }
    private OrderResponse saveOrder(long userId, PlaceOrderRequest req, Map<Long, Integer> wanted,
                                    List<ReservedItem> reserved) {
        Cart cart = cartRepository.findByUserIdForUpdate(userId).orElseThrow(this::cartEmpty);
        List<CartItem> current = cartItemRepository.findByCartUserIdOrderByIdAsc(userId);
        if (current.isEmpty()) {
            throw cartEmpty();
        }
        Map<Long, Integer> now = current.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(CartItem::getProductId, CartItem::getQuantity));
        if (!now.equals(wanted)) {
            throw cartChanged();
        }
        Map<Long, ReservedItem> byId = reserved.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(ReservedItem::productId, Function.identity()));
        List<OrderLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        BigDecimal subtotal = ZERO;
        for (Map.Entry<Long, Integer> e : wanted.entrySet()) {
            ReservedItem r = byId.get(e.getKey());
            if (r == null) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Reserve response is missing product " + e.getKey());
            }
            BigDecimal unitPrice = r.unitPrice().setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
            BigDecimal lineTotal = unitPrice.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(e.getValue())).setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
            subtotal = subtotal.add(lineTotal);
            lines.add(new OrderLine(r.productId(), r.name(), unitPrice, e.getValue(), lineTotal));
        }
        BigDecimal deliveryFee = subtotal.compareTo(props.freeDeliveryThreshold()) >= 0
                ? ZERO : props.deliveryFee().setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        var a = req.deliveryAddress();
        Order order = Order.builder()
                .orderNumber(nextOrderNumber())
                .userId(userId)
                .status(OrderStatus.PLACED)
                .subtotal(subtotal)
                .deliveryFee(deliveryFee)
                .total(subtotal.add(deliveryFee))
                .paymentMethod(req.paymentMethod())
                .paymentStatus(req.paymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.MOCK_PAYMENT
                        ? PaymentStatus.PAID : PaymentStatus.PENDING)
                .deliveryAddress(new DeliveryAddress(a.recipientName().trim(), a.phone(), a.line1().trim(),
                        blankToNull(a.line2()), a.city().trim(), a.state().trim(), a.pincode()))
                .build();
        lines.forEach(l -> order.addItem(new OrderItem(order, l.productId(), l.name(), l.unitPrice(),
                l.quantity(), l.lineTotal())));
        orderRepository.saveAndFlush(order);
        cartItemRepository.deleteAllByCartId(cart.getId());
        cart.touch();
        return mapper.toOrder(order);
    }
    private String nextOrderNumber() {
        String date = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).format(DateTimeFormatter.BASIC_ISO_DATE);
        return String.format("QC%s-%06d", date, orderRepository.nextOrderSequence());
    }
    private void releaseQuietly(List<StockItem> stock) {
        try {
            catalogClient.release(stock);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.error("Compensation release failed; stock may be under-counted for {}", stock, e);
        }
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<OrderSummaryResponse> listMine(long userId, int page, int size) {
        Page<Order> result = orderRepository.findByUserId(userId, QueryUtils.pageable(page, size, NEWEST_FIRST));
        Map<Long, Long> counts = counts(result);
        return PageResponse.of(result.map(o -> mapper.toSummary(o, counts.getOrDefault(o.getId(), 0L).intValue())));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public OrderResponse getMine(long userId, long id) {
        return orderRepository.findWithItemsByIdAndUserId(id, userId)
                .map(mapper::toOrder)
                .orElseThrow(this::notFound);
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<AdminOrderSummaryResponse> listAll(OrderStatus status, int page, int size) {
        Pageable pageable = QueryUtils.pageable(page, size, NEWEST_FIRST);
        Page<Order> result = status == null ? orderRepository.findAll(pageable)
                : orderRepository.findByStatus(status, pageable);
        Map<Long, Long> counts = counts(result);
        return PageResponse.of(result.map(o ->
                mapper.toAdminSummary(o, counts.getOrDefault(o.getId(), 0L).intValue())));
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AdminOrderResponse getAny(long id) {
        return orderRepository.findWithItemsById(id).map(mapper::toAdminOrder).orElseThrow(this::notFound);
    }
    public OrderResponse updateStatus(long id, OrderStatus next) {
        return tx.execute(status -> {
            Order order = orderRepository.findByIdForUpdate(id).orElseThrow(this::notFound);
            OrderStatus current = order.getStatus();
            if (!current.canMoveTo(next)) {
                throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION",
                        "Cannot change order status from " + current + " to " + next + ".",
                        List.of(new ErrorDetail("status", "Allowed: " + current.allowedNext())));
            }
            order.changeStatus(next);
            if (next == OrderStatus.DELIVERED && order.getPaymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.COD) {
                order.markPaid();
            }
            orderRepository.saveAndFlush(order);
            if (next == OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
                List<StockItem> items = order.getItems().stream()
                        .map(i -> new StockItem(i.getProductId(), i.getQuantity())).toList();
                catalogClient.release(items);
            }
            return mapper.toOrder(order);
        });
    }
    private Map<Long, Long> counts(Page<Order> page) {
        List<Long> ids = page.getContent().stream().map(Order::getId).toList();
        if (ids.isEmpty()) {
            return Map.of();
        }
        return orderItemRepository.countByOrderIds(ids).stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(OrderItemCount::orderId, OrderItemCount::quantity));
    }
    private String blankToNull(String s) {
        return s == null || s.isBlank() ? null : s.trim();
    }
    private ApiException cartEmpty() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "CART_EMPTY", "Your cart is empty.");
    }
    private ApiException cartChanged() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE",
                "Your cart changed while placing the order. Please review it and try again.");
    }
    private ApiException unavailableItems(CartResponse cart) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = cart.items().stream()
                .filter(l -> !l.available())
                .map(l -> new ErrorDetail("productId:" + l.productId(),
                        "No longer available in the requested quantity"))
                .toList();
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE",
                "Some items in your cart are unavailable. Remove them to continue.", details);
    }
    private ApiException notFound() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Order not found.");
    }
    private record OrderLine(Long productId, String name, BigDecimal unitPrice, int quantity, BigDecimal lineTotal) { }
}
===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/order/service/QueryUtils.java =====
package com.quickcart.order.service;
public final class QueryUtils {
    public static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    private QueryUtils() { }
    public static Pageable pageable(int page, int size, Sort sort) {
        if (page < 0) {
            throw invalid("page", "Must be 0 or greater");
        }
        if (size < 1 || size > MAX_PAGE_SIZE) {
            throw invalid("size", "Must be between 1 and " + MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        }
        return PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);
    }
    public static ApiException invalid(String field, String message) {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", "Validation failed.",
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(field, message)));
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/order-service/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__init.sql =====
CREATE SEQUENCE order_number_seq START WITH 1 INCREMENT BY 1;
CREATE TABLE carts (
    id         BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id    BIGINT      NOT NULL,   -- no FK: belongs to user-service
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_carts_user_id UNIQUE (user_id)
);
CREATE TABLE cart_items (
    id         BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    cart_id    BIGINT  NOT NULL,
    product_id BIGINT  NOT NULL,       -- no FK: belongs to catalog-service
    quantity   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_cart_items_cart FOREIGN KEY (cart_id) REFERENCES carts (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT uq_cart_items_cart_product UNIQUE (cart_id, product_id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_cart_items_quantity CHECK (quantity BETWEEN 1 AND 20)
);
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id                      BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    order_number            VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL,
    user_id                 BIGINT         NOT NULL,
    status                  VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL,
    subtotal                NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    delivery_fee            NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    total                   NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    payment_method          VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL,
    payment_status          VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL,
    delivery_recipient_name VARCHAR(100)   NOT NULL,
    delivery_phone          VARCHAR(10)    NOT NULL,
    delivery_line1          VARCHAR(150)   NOT NULL,
    delivery_line2          VARCHAR(150),
    delivery_city           VARCHAR(60)    NOT NULL,
    delivery_state          VARCHAR(60)    NOT NULL,
    delivery_pincode        VARCHAR(6)     NOT NULL,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ    NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ    NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_orders_order_number UNIQUE (order_number),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orders_status CHECK (status IN
        ('PLACED', 'CONFIRMED', 'PACKED', 'OUT_FOR_DELIVERY', 'DELIVERED', 'CANCELLED')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orders_payment_method CHECK (payment_method IN ('MOCK_PAYMENT', 'COD')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_orders_payment_status CHECK (payment_status IN ('PAID', 'PENDING'))
);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_user_id ON orders (user_id);
CREATE TABLE order_items (
    id           BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id     BIGINT         NOT NULL,
    product_id   BIGINT         NOT NULL,   -- no FK: belongs to catalog-service
    product_name VARCHAR(150)   NOT NULL,
    unit_price   NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    quantity     INTEGER        NOT NULL,
    line_total   NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_items_order FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT ck_order_items_quantity CHECK (quantity > 0)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_order_items_order_id ON order_items (order_id);

package com.quickcart.order.exception;
@WebMvcTest
@AutoConfigureMockMvc(addFilters = false)
@Import(GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ProbeController.class)
class GlobalExceptionHandlerTest {
    @Autowired
    MockMvc mockMvc;
    @Test
    void beanValidationFailure_returns400WithFieldDetails() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\"\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value(400))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.path").value("/probe/validate"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.timestamp").exists())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details[0].field").value("name"));
    }
    @Test
    void malformedJson_returns400WithEmptyDetailsArray() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{not json"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details").isArray());
    }
    @Test
    void apiException_usesItsOwnStatusAndCode() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/conflict"))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CART_EMPTY"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.error").value("Conflict"));
    }
    @Test
    void serviceUnavailable_returns503WithStandardBody() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/unavailable"))
                .andExpect(status().isServiceUnavailable())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details").isArray());
    }
    @Test
    void accessDenied_returns403Forbidden() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/forbidden"))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("FORBIDDEN"));
    }
    @Test
    void unexpectedError_returns500WithoutLeakingInternals() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/boom"))
                .andExpect(status().isInternalServerError())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INTERNAL_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message", not(containsString("secret internal detail"))));
    }
    @Test
    void unknownPath_returns404NotFound() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/does-not-exist"))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
    }
    @RestController
    @RequestMapping("/probe")
    static class ProbeController {
        record Body(@NotBlank String name) { }
        @PostMapping("/validate")
        void validate(@Valid @RequestBody Body body) { }
        @GetMapping("/conflict")
        void conflict() {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "CART_EMPTY", "Your cart is empty.");
        }
        @GetMapping("/unavailable")
        void unavailable() {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
                    "A dependent service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.");
        }
        @GetMapping("/forbidden")
        void forbidden() {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("nope");
        }
        @GetMapping("/boom")
        void boom() {
            throw new IllegalStateException("secret internal detail");
        }
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/config/AdminSeeder.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.config;
@Component
public class AdminSeeder implements ApplicationRunner {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AdminSeeder.class);
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final String adminEmail;
    private final String adminPassword;
    public AdminSeeder(UserRepository userRepository, PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder,
                       @Value("${quickcart.admin.email}") String adminEmail,
                       @Value("${quickcart.admin.password}") String adminPassword) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.passwordEncoder = passwordEncoder;
        this.adminEmail = adminEmail.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        this.adminPassword = adminPassword;
    }
    @Override
    public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
        if (userRepository.existsByEmail(adminEmail)) {
            return;
        }
        userRepository.save(new User("QuickCart Admin", adminEmail,
                passwordEncoder.encode(adminPassword), "9999999999", Role.ADMIN));
        log.info("Seeded ADMIN account {}", adminEmail);
    }
}

    OpenAPI userServiceOpenApi() {
        return new OpenAPI()
                .info(new Info().title("QuickCart user-service").version("v1")
                        .description("Summary only. The master contract document is authoritative."))
                .components(new Components().addSecuritySchemes("bearerAuth",

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/config/SecurityConfig.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.config;
@Configuration
@EnableMethodSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {
    @Bean
    PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder(10);
    }
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http,
                                            JwtService jwtService,
                                            RestAuthenticationEntryPoint entryPoint,
                                            RestAccessDeniedHandler accessDeniedHandler) throws Exception {
        http
                .csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .httpBasic(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .formLogin(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .sessionManagement(s -> s.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .exceptionHandling(e -> e
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(entryPoint)
                        .accessDeniedHandler(accessDeniedHandler))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(a -> a
                        .requestMatchers("/actuator/health", "/actuator/health/**", "/actuator/info").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/swagger-ui.html", "/swagger-ui/**", "/v3/api-docs/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, "/api/v1/auth/**").permitAll()
                        .anyRequest().authenticated())
                .addFilterBefore(new JwtAuthenticationFilter(jwtService), UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class);
        return http.build();
    }
}

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/users/me/addresses")
@PreAuthorize("hasRole('CUSTOMER')")
public class AddressController {
    private final AddressService service;
    public AddressController(AddressService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Operation(summary = "U5 List my addresses (default first)")
    @GetMapping
    public List<AddressResponse> list(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user) {
        return service.list(user.id());
    }
    @Operation(summary = "U6 Add address")
    @PostMapping
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CREATED)
    public AddressResponse create(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user,
                                  @Valid @RequestBody AddressRequest request) {
        return service.create(user.id(), request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "U7 Update address")
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public AddressResponse update(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user, @PathVariable long id,
                                  @Valid @RequestBody AddressRequest request) {
        return service.update(user.id(), id, request);
    }
    @Operation(summary = "U8 Delete address")
    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
    public void delete(@AuthenticationPrincipal AuthenticatedUser user, @PathVariable long id) {
        service.delete(user.id(), id);
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/entity/Address.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.entity;
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "addresses")
public class Address extends BaseEntity {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private Long userId;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String label;
    @Column(name = "recipient_name", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String recipientName;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private String phone;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String line1;
    @Column(length = 150)
    private String line2;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 60)
    private String city;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 60)
    private String state;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 6)
    private String pincode;
    @Setter
    @Column(name = "is_default", nullable = false)
    private boolean defaultAddress;
    public Address(Long userId, String label, String recipientName, String phone, String line1,
                   String line2, String city, String state, String pincode, boolean defaultAddress) {
        this.userId = userId;
        this.defaultAddress = defaultAddress;
        update(label, recipientName, phone, line1, line2, city, state, pincode);
    }
    public final void update(String label, String recipientName, String phone, String line1,
                             String line2, String city, String state, String pincode) {
        this.label = label;
        this.recipientName = recipientName;
        this.phone = phone;
        this.line1 = line1;
        this.line2 = line2;
        this.city = city;
        this.state = state;
        this.pincode = pincode;
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/entity/User.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.entity;
@Getter
@NoArgsConstructor(access = AccessLevel.PROTECTED)
@Entity
@Table(name = "users")
public class User extends BaseEntity {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @Setter
    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String fullName;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 150, unique = true)
    private String email;
    @Column(name = "password_hash", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String passwordHash;
    @Setter
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private String phone;
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private Role role;
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private boolean active = true;
    public User(String fullName, String email, String passwordHash, String phone, Role role) {
        this.fullName = fullName;
        this.email = email;
        this.passwordHash = passwordHash;
        this.phone = phone;
        this.role = role;
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/exception/ApiException.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.exception;
public class ApiException extends RuntimeException {
    private final HttpStatus status;
    private final String code;
    private final List<ErrorDetail> details;
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message) {
        this(status, code, message, List.of());
    }
    public ApiException(HttpStatus status, String code, String message, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        super(message);
        this.status = status;
        this.code = code;
        this.details = details;
    }
    public HttpStatus getStatus() { return status; }
    public String getCode() { return code; }
    public List<ErrorDetail> getDetails() { return details; }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/exception/GlobalExceptionHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.exception;
@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionHandler.class);
    @ExceptionHandler(ApiException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleApi(ApiException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(ex.getStatus(), ex.getCode(), ex.getMessage(), req, ex.getDetails());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleBeanValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException ex,
                                                              HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getBindingResult().getAllErrors().stream()
                .map(e -> new ErrorDetail(
                        e instanceof org.springframework.validation.FieldError fe ? fe.getField() : e.getObjectName(),
                        e.getDefaultMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleConstraint(ConstraintViolationException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        List<ErrorDetail> details = ex.getConstraintViolations().stream()
                .map(v -> new ErrorDetail(lastNode(v.getPropertyPath()), v.getMessage()))
                .toList();
        return validation("Validation failed.", req, details);
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnreadable(HttpMessageNotReadableException ex,
                                                          HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Malformed request body.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleTypeMismatch(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Invalid request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getName(), "Invalid value")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleMissingParam(MissingServletRequestParameterException ex,
                                                            HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Missing request parameter.", req,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(ex.getParameterName(), "Required")));
    }
    @ExceptionHandler({HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class, HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException.class})
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnsupported(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return validation("Unsupported request.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(NoResourceFoundException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleNoResource(NoResourceFoundException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Resource not found.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AuthenticationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAuthentication(AuthenticationException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.", req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(AccessDeniedException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleAccessDenied(AccessDeniedException ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        return build(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN", "You do not have permission to perform this action.",
                req, List.of());
    }
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> handleUnexpected(Exception ex, HttpServletRequest req) {
        log.error("Unexpected error on {} {}", req.getMethod(), req.getRequestURI(), ex);
        return build(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.", req, List.of());
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> validation(String message, HttpServletRequest req,
                                                     List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "VALIDATION_ERROR", message, req, details);
    }
    private ResponseEntity<ErrorResponse> build(HttpStatus status, String code, String message,
                                                HttpServletRequest req, List<ErrorDetail> details) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(),
                status.getReasonPhrase(),
                code,
                message,
                req.getRequestURI(),
                details == null ? List.of() : details);
        return ResponseEntity.status(status).body(body);
    }
    private String lastNode(jakarta.validation.Path path) {
        return StreamSupport.stream(path.spliterator(), false)
                .reduce((first, second) -> second)
                .map(Object::toString)
                .orElse("");
    }
}

    Optional<Address> findByIdAndUserId(Long id, Long userId);
    Optional<Address> findFirstByUserIdOrderByIdAsc(Long userId);

    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("update Address a set a.defaultAddress = false, a.updatedAt = :now "
            + "where a.userId = :userId and a.defaultAddress = true")

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/AddressService.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.service;
@Service
public class AddressService {
    private static final int MAX_ADDRESSES = 5;
    private final AddressRepository addressRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final AddressMapper mapper;
    public AddressService(AddressRepository addressRepository, UserRepository userRepository,
                          AddressMapper mapper) {
        this.addressRepository = addressRepository;
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AddressResponse> list(long userId) {
        return addressRepository.findByUserIdOrderByDefaultAddressDescIdAsc(userId).stream()
                .map(mapper::toResponse).toList();
    }
    @Transactional
    public AddressResponse create(long userId, AddressRequest req) {
        lockUser(userId);
        long count = addressRepository.countByUserId(userId);
        if (count >= MAX_ADDRESSES) {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "ADDRESS_LIMIT_REACHED",
                    "You can save at most " + MAX_ADDRESSES + " addresses.");
        }
        boolean makeDefault = count == 0 || Boolean.TRUE.equals(req.isDefault());
        if (makeDefault && count > 0) {
            addressRepository.clearDefaults(userId, Instant.now());
        }
        Address saved = addressRepository.save(new Address(userId, req.label().trim(),
                req.recipientName().trim(), req.phone(), req.line1().trim(), blankToNull(req.line2()),
                req.city().trim(), req.state().trim(), req.pincode(), makeDefault));
        return mapper.toResponse(saved);
    }
    @Transactional
    public AddressResponse update(long userId, long id, AddressRequest req) {
        lockUser(userId);
        Address address = find(userId, id);
        address.update(req.label().trim(), req.recipientName().trim(), req.phone(), req.line1().trim(),
                blankToNull(req.line2()), req.city().trim(), req.state().trim(), req.pincode());
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(req.isDefault()) && !address.isDefaultAddress()) {
            addressRepository.clearDefaults(userId, Instant.now());
            address.setDefaultAddress(true);
        }
        return mapper.toResponse(address);
    }
    @Transactional
    public void delete(long userId, long id) {
        lockUser(userId);
        Address address = find(userId, id);
        boolean wasDefault = address.isDefaultAddress();
        addressRepository.delete(address);
        addressRepository.flush();
        if (wasDefault) {
            addressRepository.findFirstByUserIdOrderByIdAsc(userId)
                    .ifPresent(next -> next.setDefaultAddress(true));
        }
    }
    private Address find(long userId, long id) {
        return addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(id, userId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Address not found."));
    }
    private void lockUser(long userId) {
        userRepository.findByIdForUpdate(userId)
                .filter(u -> u.isActive())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED",
                        "Authentication is required."));
    }
    private String blankToNull(String s) {
        return s == null || s.isBlank() ? null : s.trim();
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.security;
public class JwtAuthenticationFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {
    private static final String PREFIX = "Bearer ";
    private final JwtService jwtService;
    public JwtAuthenticationFilter(JwtService jwtService) {
        this.jwtService = jwtService;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response,
                                    FilterChain chain) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String header = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (header != null && header.startsWith(PREFIX)) {
            jwtService.parse(header.substring(PREFIX.length()).trim()).ifPresent(user -> {
                var auth = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                        user, null, List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_" + user.role().name())));
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(auth);
            });
        }
        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/JwtService.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.security;
@Service
public class JwtService {
    private final SecretKey key;
    private final long expirySeconds;
    private final String issuer;
    public JwtService(@Value("${quickcart.jwt.secret}") String secret,
                      @Value("${quickcart.jwt.expiry-seconds}") long expirySeconds,
                      @Value("${quickcart.jwt.issuer}") String issuer) {
        byte[] bytes = secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (bytes.length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("JWT_SECRET must be at least 32 characters");
        }
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(bytes);
        this.expirySeconds = expirySeconds;
        this.issuer = issuer;
    }
    public long getExpirySeconds() {
        return expirySeconds;
    }
    public String issue(User user) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        return Jwts.builder()
                .issuer(issuer)
                .subject(String.valueOf(user.getId()))
                .claim("email", user.getEmail())
                .claim("role", user.getRole().name())
                .issuedAt(Date.from(now))
                .expiration(Date.from(now.plusSeconds(expirySeconds)))
                .signWith(key, Jwts.SIG.HS256)
                .compact();
    }
    public Optional<AuthenticatedUser> parse(String token) {
        try {
            Jws<Claims> jws = Jwts.parser()
                    .verifyWith(key)
                    .requireIssuer(issuer)
                    .build()
                    .parseSignedClaims(token);
            if (!"HS256".equals(jws.getHeader().getAlgorithm())) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            Claims c = jws.getPayload();
            return Optional.of(new AuthenticatedUser(
                    Long.parseLong(c.getSubject()),
                    c.get("email", String.class),
                    Role.valueOf(c.get("role", String.class))));
        } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/RestAccessDeniedHandler.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.security;
@Component
public class RestAccessDeniedHandler implements AccessDeniedHandler {
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public RestAccessDeniedHandler(SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void handle(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AccessDeniedException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN",
                "You do not have permission to perform this action.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/RestAuthenticationEntryPoint.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.security;
@Component
public class RestAuthenticationEntryPoint implements AuthenticationEntryPoint {
    private final SecurityErrorWriter writer;
    public RestAuthenticationEntryPoint(SecurityErrorWriter writer) {
        this.writer = writer;
    }
    @Override
    public void commence(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res, AuthenticationException ex)
            throws IOException {
        writer.write(req, res, HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.");
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/security/SecurityErrorWriter.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.security;
@Component
public class SecurityErrorWriter {
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    public SecurityErrorWriter(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }
    public void write(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse res,
                      HttpStatus status, String code, String message) throws IOException {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.SECONDS),
                status.value(), status.getReasonPhrase(), code, message, req.getRequestURI(), List.of());
        res.setStatus(status.value());
        res.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE);
        res.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        objectMapper.writeValue(res.getWriter(), body);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/service/AuthService.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.service;
@Service
public class AuthService {
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final JwtService jwtService;
    private final UserMapper userMapper;
    public AuthService(UserRepository userRepository, PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder,
                       JwtService jwtService, UserMapper userMapper) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.passwordEncoder = passwordEncoder;
        this.jwtService = jwtService;
        this.userMapper = userMapper;
    }
    public AuthResponse register(RegisterRequest req) {
        String email = normalize(req.email());
        if (userRepository.existsByEmail(email)) {
            throw emailExists();
        }
        User user = new User(req.fullName().trim(), email,
                passwordEncoder.encode(req.password()), req.phone(), Role.CUSTOMER);
        try {
            userRepository.saveAndFlush(user);
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException race) {
            throw emailExists();
        }
        return toAuthResponse(user);
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AuthResponse login(LoginRequest req) {
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(normalize(req.email()))
                .filter(User::isActive)
                .filter(u -> passwordEncoder.matches(req.password(), u.getPasswordHash()))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "INVALID_CREDENTIALS",
                        "Invalid email or password."));
        return toAuthResponse(user);
    }
    private AuthResponse toAuthResponse(User user) {
        return new AuthResponse(jwtService.issue(user), "Bearer",
                jwtService.getExpirySeconds(), userMapper.toSummary(user));
    }
    private ApiException emailExists() {
        return new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS",
                "An account with this email already exists.");
    }
    private String normalize(String email) {
        return email.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/java/com/quickcart/user/service/UserService.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.service;
@Service
public class UserService {
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final UserMapper userMapper;
    public UserService(UserRepository userRepository, UserMapper userMapper) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.userMapper = userMapper;
    }
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public UserProfile getProfile(long userId) {
        return userMapper.toProfile(loadActive(userId));
    }
    @Transactional
    public UserProfile updateProfile(long userId, UpdateProfileRequest req) {
        User user = loadActive(userId);
        user.setFullName(req.fullName().trim());
        user.setPhone(req.phone());
        return userMapper.toProfile(user);
    }
    private User loadActive(long userId) {
        return userRepository.findById(userId)
                .filter(User::isActive)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ApiException(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED",
                        "Authentication is required."));
    }
}

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__init.sql =====
CREATE TABLE users (
    id            BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    full_name     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email         VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    phone         VARCHAR(10)  NOT NULL,
    role          VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL,
    active        BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_users_email UNIQUE (email),
    CONSTRAINT ck_users_role CHECK (role IN ('CUSTOMER', 'ADMIN'))
);
CREATE TABLE addresses (
    id             BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id        BIGINT       NOT NULL,
    label          VARCHAR(30)  NOT NULL,
    recipient_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    phone          VARCHAR(10)  NOT NULL,
    line1          VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    line2          VARCHAR(150),
    city           VARCHAR(60)  NOT NULL,
    state          VARCHAR(60)  NOT NULL,
    pincode        VARCHAR(6)   NOT NULL,
    is_default     BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at     TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT fk_addresses_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX idx_addresses_user_id ON addresses (user_id);

===== quickcartbackend/user-service/src/test/java/com/quickcart/user/exception/GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.java =====
package com.quickcart.user.exception;
@WebMvcTest
@AutoConfigureMockMvc(addFilters = false)
@Import(GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ProbeController.class)
class GlobalExceptionHandlerTest {
    @Autowired
    MockMvc mockMvc;
    @Test
    void beanValidationFailure_returns400WithFieldDetails() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"name\":\"\"}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value(400))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.path").value("/probe/validate"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.timestamp").exists())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details[0].field").value("name"));
    }
    @Test
    void malformedJson_returns400WithEmptyDetailsArray() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/validate").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{not json"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("VALIDATION_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.details").isArray());
    }
    @Test
    void apiException_usesItsOwnStatusAndCode() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/conflict"))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.error").value("Conflict"));
    }
    @Test
    void accessDenied_returns403Forbidden() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/forbidden"))
                .andExpect(status().isForbidden())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("FORBIDDEN"));
    }
    @Test
    void unexpectedError_returns500WithoutLeakingInternals() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/boom"))
                .andExpect(status().isInternalServerError())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INTERNAL_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message", not(containsString("secret internal detail"))));
    }
    @Test
    void unknownPath_returns404NotFound() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/does-not-exist"))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
    }
    @RestController
    @RequestMapping("/probe")
    static class ProbeController {
        record Body(@NotBlank String name) { }
        @PostMapping("/validate")
        void validate(@Valid @RequestBody Body body) { }
        @GetMapping("/conflict")
        void conflict() {
            throw new ApiException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS", "Email already registered.");
        }
        @GetMapping("/forbidden")
        void forbidden() {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("nope");
        }
        @GetMapping("/boom")
        void boom() {
            throw new IllegalStateException("secret internal detail");
        }
    }
}

FROM node:22-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
ARG VITE_API_BASE_URL=""
ENV VITE_API_BASE_URL=$VITE_API_BASE_URL
ENV VITE_USE_MOCK_API=false
RUN npm run build
FROM nginx:1.27-alpine
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
EXPOSE 80
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1/ >/dev/null || exit 1

===== quickcartfrontend/nginx.conf =====
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index index.html;
    gzip on;
    gzip_types text/css application/javascript application/json image/svg+xml;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
    add_header X-Frame-Options "DENY" always;
    add_header Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" always;
    # Docker's built-in DNS. Re-resolving means a restarted api-gateway is found again.
    resolver 127.0.0.11 valid=10s ipv6=off;
    # Decision D14: one origin for the browser, so no CORS in Docker or on AWS.
    location /api/ {
        set $gateway http://api-gateway:8080;
        proxy_pass $gateway;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $http_host;   # keeps the port, so Origin == Host (see note below)
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_connect_timeout 5s;
        proxy_read_timeout 30s;             # above our 15 s Axios timeout, so the browser gives up first
    }
    # Vite fingerprints these file names, so they can be cached for a year
    location /assets/ {
        expires 1y;
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    # The entry file must always be re-checked, or users keep an old app
    location = /index.html {
        expires -1;
    }
    # SPA fallback: /orders/42 on refresh must serve the app, not a 404
    location / {
        try_files $uri /index.html;
    }
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
        <Route path="/account" element={<Account />} />
        <Route path="/products" element={<ProductList />} />
        <Route path="/products/:id" element={<ProductDetail />} />
        <Route element={<GuestRoute />}>
          <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
          <Route path="/register" element={<Register />} />
        </Route>
        <Route element={<ProtectedRoute role="CUSTOMER" />}>
          <Route path="/cart" element={<Cart />} />
          <Route path="/checkout" element={<Checkout />} />
          <Route path="/orders" element={<Orders />} />
          <Route path="/orders/:id" element={<OrderDetail />} />
          <Route path="/account" element={<Placeholder name="Account" />} />
        </Route>
        <Route path="/admin" element={<Navigate to="/admin/orders" replace />} />
        <Route element={<ProtectedRoute role="ADMIN" />}>
          <Route path="/admin/orders" element={<AdminOrders />} />
          <Route path="/admin/orders/:id" element={<AdminOrderDetail />} />
          <Route path="/admin/products" element={<AdminProducts />} />
          <Route path="/admin/products/new" element={<AdminProductForm />} />
          <Route path="/admin/products/:id/edit" element={<AdminProductForm />} />
          <Route path="/admin/categories" element={<AdminCategories />} />
          <Route path="/admin/inventory" element={<AdminInventory />} />
        </Route>
        <Route path="*" element={<h2>Page not found</h2>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/api/httpClient.js =====
export const TOKEN_KEY = 'quickcart.token';
export const USER_KEY = 'quickcart.user';
const httpClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? '',
  timeout: 15000,
  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
});
httpClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  if (token) config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  return config;
});
const AUTH_PATHS = ['/api/v1/auth/login', '/api/v1/auth/register'];
let redirecting = false;
httpClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    const status = error.response?.status;
    const url = error.config?.url || '';
    const isAuthCall = AUTH_PATHS.some((p) => url.includes(p));
    if (status === 401 && !isAuthCall && !redirecting) {
      redirecting = true;
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
      window.location.assign('/login?expired=1');
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);
export default httpClient;
===== quickcartfrontend/src/api/orderApi.js =====
export async function placeOrder({ paymentMethod, deliveryAddress }) {
  const { data } = await httpClient.post('/api/v1/orders', { paymentMethod, deliveryAddress });
  return data;
}
export async function getOrders({ page = 0, size = 10 } = {}, signal) {
  const { data } = await httpClient.get('/api/v1/orders', { params: { page, size }, signal });
  return data;
}
export async function getOrder(id, signal) {
  const { data } = await httpClient.get(`/api/v1/orders/${id}`, { signal });
  return data;
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/AddToCartButton.jsx =====
export default function AddToCartButton({ product, quantity = 1 }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { addItem } = useCart();
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (isAdmin) return null;
  if (!product.inStock) {
    return <button type="button" disabled>Out of stock</button>;
  }
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() =>
          navigate('/login', { state: { from: location.pathname + location.search } })
        }
      >
        Add
      </button>
    );
  }
  const handleAdd = () =>
    run(async () => {
      setError('');
      try {
        await addItem(product.id, quantity);
      } catch (err) {
        setError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      }
    });
  return (
    <span className="add-to-cart">
      <button type="button" onClick={handleAdd} disabled={busy}>
        {busy ? 'Adding...' : 'Add'}
      </button>
      {error && <span className="add-to-cart__error" role="alert">{error}</span>}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function AddressCard({ address, busy, onEdit, onDelete, onMakeDefault }) {

        <strong>{address.label}</strong>
        {address.isDefault && <span className="tag">Default</span>}

      <p>{address.city}, {address.state} {address.pincode}</p>
