        <button type="button" onClick={onEdit} disabled={busy}>Edit</button>
        {!address.isDefault && (
          <button type="button" onClick={onMakeDefault} disabled={busy}>Set as default</button>
        )}
        <button type="button" onClick={onDelete} disabled={busy}>Delete</button>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/AddressForm.jsx =====
const FIELD_ORDER = ['label', 'recipientName', 'phone', 'line1', 'line2', 'city', 'state', 'pincode'];
export default function AddressForm({
  initialValues, defaultLocked = false, submitLabel = 'Save address', busy, onSubmit, onCancel,
}) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(initialValues);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const errors = validateDeliveryFields(form);
    const labelError = validateLabel(form.label);
    if (labelError) errors.label = labelError;
    setFieldErrors(errors);
    setFormError('');
    const firstInvalid = FIELD_ORDER.find((f) => errors[f]);
    if (firstInvalid) {
      document.getElementById(firstInvalid)?.focus();
      return;
    }
    try {
      await onSubmit({
        label: form.label.trim(),
        recipientName: form.recipientName.trim(),
        phone: form.phone.trim(),
        line1: form.line1.trim(),
        line2: form.line2.trim() || null,
        city: form.city.trim(),
        state: form.state.trim(),
        pincode: form.pincode.trim(),
        isDefault: form.isDefault,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
      if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
        setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
      } else {
        setFormError(message);
      }
    }
  };
  const field = (id, label, extra = {}) => (
    <FormField id={id} label={label} value={form[id]} onChange={handleChange}
      error={fieldErrors[id]} {...extra} />
  );
  return (
    <form className="address-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
      <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
      <div className="address-form__grid">
        {field('label', 'Label (e.g. Home)', { maxLength: 30 })}
        {field('recipientName', 'Recipient name', { autoComplete: 'name' })}
        {field('phone', 'Mobile number', { type: 'tel', inputMode: 'numeric', maxLength: 10 })}
        {field('line1', 'Address line 1', { autoComplete: 'address-line1' })}
        {field('line2', 'Address line 2 (optional)', { autoComplete: 'address-line2' })}
        {field('city', 'City')}
        {field('state', 'State')}
        {field('pincode', 'Pincode', { inputMode: 'numeric', maxLength: 6 })}
      </div>
      <label className="checkbox">
        <input type="checkbox" name="isDefault" checked={form.isDefault}
          onChange={handleChange} disabled={defaultLocked} />
        Use as my default address
      </label>
      {defaultLocked && (
        <p className="muted">
          This is already your default. To change it, set another address as default.
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="address-form__actions">
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : submitLabel}</button>
        <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/AddressSection.jsx =====
const MAX_ADDRESSES = 5;
const BLANK = {
  label: '', recipientName: '', phone: '', line1: '', line2: '',
  city: '', state: '', pincode: '', isDefault: false,
};
const toFormValues = (a) => ({
  label: a.label, recipientName: a.recipientName, phone: a.phone, line1: a.line1,
  line2: a.line2 ?? '', city: a.city, state: a.state, pincode: a.pincode, isDefault: a.isDefault,
});
const toRequest = (a) => ({
  label: a.label, recipientName: a.recipientName, phone: a.phone, line1: a.line1,
  line2: a.line2, city: a.city, state: a.state, pincode: a.pincode, isDefault: true,
});
export default function AddressSection() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { data: addresses, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getAddresses(signal), []);
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(null);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  if (loading && !addresses) return <Loader />;
  if (error && !addresses) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!addresses) return null;
  const atLimit = addresses.length >= MAX_ADDRESSES;
  const save = (values) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      if (editing === 'new') await createAddress(values);
      else await updateAddress(editing, values);
      setEditing(null);
      reload();
    });
  const makeDefault = (address) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      try {
        await updateAddress(address.id, toRequest(address));
      } catch (err) {
        setActionError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      } finally {
        reload();
      }
    });
  const confirmDelete = () =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      try {
        await deleteAddress(deleting.id);
      } catch (err) {
        setActionError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      } finally {
        setDeleting(null);
        reload();
      }
    });
  return (
    <div aria-busy={loading}>
      <ErrorBanner message={actionError} />
      {addresses.length === 0 && editing !== 'new' && (
        <EmptyState message="No saved addresses." />
      )}
      <ul className={`address-list${loading ? ' is-refreshing' : ''}`}>
        {addresses.map((a) => (
          <li key={a.id}>
            {editing === a.id ? (
              <AddressForm
                initialValues={toFormValues(a)}
                defaultLocked={a.isDefault}
                submitLabel="Save changes"
                busy={busy}
                onSubmit={save}
                onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
              />
            ) : (
              <AddressCard
                address={a}
                busy={busy}
                onEdit={() => setEditing(a.id)}
                onDelete={() => setDeleting(a)}
                onMakeDefault={() => makeDefault(a)}
              />
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {editing === 'new' ? (
        <AddressForm
          initialValues={{
            ...BLANK,
            recipientName: user?.fullName ?? '',
            isDefault: addresses.length === 0,
          }}
          defaultLocked={addresses.length === 0}
          busy={busy}
          onSubmit={save}
          onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
        />
      ) : (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing('new')} disabled={atLimit || busy}>
          Add address
        </button>
      )}
      {atLimit && (
        <p className="muted">You can save up to {MAX_ADDRESSES} addresses. Delete one to add another.</p>
      )}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={Boolean(deleting)}
        title="Delete address?"
        message={deleting ? `Remove "${deleting.label}" (${deleting.line1})?` : ''}
        confirmLabel="Delete"
        busy={busy}
        onConfirm={confirmDelete}
        onCancel={() => !busy && setDeleting(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/CartLine.jsx =====
export default function CartLine({ line, busy, onQuantityChange, onRemove }) {
  return (
    <li className={`cart-line${line.available ? '' : ' cart-line--unavailable'}`}>
      <ProductImage src={line.imageUrl} name={line.name} />
      <div className="cart-line__info">
        <strong>{line.name}</strong>
        {line.unit && <span className="muted">{line.unit}</span>}
        {line.available ? (
          <span>{formatPrice(line.unitPrice)}</span>
        ) : (
          <span className="cart-line__warning">
            Unavailable. Remove to continue.
            {line.availableQuantity > 0 && ` Only ${line.availableQuantity} in stock.`}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {line.available && (
        <div className="cart-line__controls">
          <QuantityStepper
            value={line.quantity}
            onChange={(n) => n !== line.quantity && onQuantityChange(n)}
            disabled={busy}
            inputReadOnly
            label={`Quantity of ${line.name}`}
          />
          <strong>{formatPrice(line.lineTotal)}</strong>
        </div>
      )}
      <button type="button" onClick={onRemove} disabled={busy}
        aria-label={`Remove ${line.name}`}>
        Remove
      </button>
    </li>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/CategoryForm.jsx =====
export default function CategoryForm({ initialValues, submitLabel, busy, onSubmit, onCancel }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(initialValues);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const handleChange = (e) => setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const errors = {};
    const add = (field, msg) => { if (msg) errors[field] = msg; };
    add('name', validateCategoryName(form.name));
    add('description', validateMaxLength(form.description, 255, 'Description'));
    add('imageUrl', validateMaxLength(form.imageUrl, 500, 'Image URL'));
    setFieldErrors(errors);
    setFormError('');
    const first = ['name', 'description', 'imageUrl'].find((f) => errors[f]);
    if (first) {
      document.getElementById(first)?.focus();
      return;
    }
    try {
      await onSubmit({
        name: form.name.trim(),
        description: form.description.trim() || null,
        imageUrl: form.imageUrl.trim() || null,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
      if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
        setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
      } else if (code === 'CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS') {
        setFieldErrors({ name: message });
      } else {
        setFormError(message);
      }
    }
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
      <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
      <FormField id="name" label="Name" autoFocus value={form.name}
        onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.name} />
      <FormField id="description" label="Description (optional)" as="textarea" rows={3}
        value={form.description} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.description} />
      <FormField id="imageUrl" label="Image URL (optional)" value={form.imageUrl}
        onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.imageUrl} />
      <div className="address-form__actions">
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : submitLabel}</button>
        <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/ConfirmDialog.jsx =====
export default function ConfirmDialog({
  open, title, message, confirmLabel = 'Confirm', cancelLabel = 'Cancel',
  busy = false, onConfirm, onCancel,
}) {
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div
      className="modal-backdrop"
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && !busy) onCancel();
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="modal"
        role="alertdialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="confirm-title"
        aria-describedby="confirm-message"
      >
        <h3 id="confirm-title">{title}</h3>
        <p id="confirm-message">{message}</p>
        <div className="modal__actions">
          <button type="button" autoFocus onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>{cancelLabel}</button>
          <button type="button" className="danger" onClick={onConfirm} disabled={busy}>
            {busy ? 'Working...' : confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

      {field('line1', 'Address line 1', { autoComplete: 'address-line1' })}
      {field('line2', 'Address line 2 (optional)', { autoComplete: 'address-line2' })}
      {field('city', 'City')}
      {field('state', 'State')}
      {field('pincode', 'Pincode', { inputMode: 'numeric', maxLength: 6 })}
    </div>
  );

export default function FormField({ id, label, error, as: Control = 'input', ...controlProps }) {
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <Control
        id={id}
        name={id}
        aria-invalid={error ? 'true' : 'false'}
        aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined}
        {...controlProps}
      />
      {error && (
        <p id={`${id}-error`} className="form-field__error">{error}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/Navbar.jsx =====
export default function Navbar() {
  const { isAuthenticated, isAdmin, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { itemCount } = useCart();
  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate('/');
  };
  return (
    <header className="navbar">
      {!isAdmin && <SearchBox />}
      <Link to={isAdmin ? '/admin/orders' : '/'} className="navbar__logo">
        QuickCart
      </Link>
      <nav className="navbar__links">
        {!isAuthenticated && (
          <>
            <NavLink to="/login">Login</NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/register">Register</NavLink>
          </>
        )}
        {isAuthenticated && !isAdmin && (
          <>
            <NavLink to="/cart">
              Cart
              {itemCount > 0 && (
                <span className="badge" aria-label={`${itemCount} items in cart`}>
                  {itemCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/orders">Orders</NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/account">Account</NavLink>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleLogout}>Logout</button>
          </>
        )}
        {isAdmin && (
          <>
            <NavLink to="/admin/products">Products</NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/admin/categories">Categories</NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/admin/inventory">Inventory</NavLink>
            <NavLink to="/admin/orders">Orders</NavLink>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleLogout}>Logout</button>
          </>
        )}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/OrderTimeline.jsx =====
export default function OrderTimeline({ status }) {
  if (status === 'CANCELLED') {
    return <p className="timeline-cancelled" role="status">This order was cancelled.</p>;
  }
  const current = TIMELINE.indexOf(status);
  return (
    <ol className="timeline" aria-label="Order progress">
      {TIMELINE.map((step, i) => (
        <li
          key={step}
          className={i < current ? 'is-done' : i === current ? 'is-current' : ''}
          aria-current={i === current ? 'step' : undefined}
        >
          {statusLabel(step)}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/Pagination.jsx =====
export default function Pagination({ page, totalPages, onChange }) {
  if (totalPages <= 1) return null;
  return (
    <nav className="pagination" aria-label="Pagination">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onChange(page - 1)} disabled={page <= 0}>
        Previous
      </button>
      <span>Page {page + 1} of {totalPages}</span>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onChange(page + 1)} disabled={page >= totalPages - 1}>
        Next
      </button>
    </nav>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/ProductImage.jsx =====
export default function ProductImage({ src, name }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  if (src && !failed) {
    return (
      <img className="product-image" src={src} alt={name} loading="lazy"
        onError={() => setFailed(true)} />
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="product-image product-image--placeholder" aria-hidden="true">
      {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
    </div>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/ProfileForm.jsx =====
export default function ProfileForm() {
  const { data: profile, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getProfile(signal), []);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!profile) return null;
  return <ProfileEditor profile={profile} />;
}
function ProfileEditor({ profile }) {
  const { updateFullName } = useAuth();
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ fullName: profile.fullName, phone: profile.phone });
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    setSaved(false);
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    return run(async () => {
      const errors = {};
      const nameError = validateFullName(form.fullName);
      const phoneError = validatePhone(form.phone);
      if (nameError) errors.fullName = nameError;
      if (phoneError) errors.phone = phoneError;
      setFieldErrors(errors);
      setFormError('');
      setSaved(false);
      if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;
      try {
        const updated = await updateProfile({
          fullName: form.fullName.trim(),
          phone: form.phone.trim(),
        });
        setForm({ fullName: updated.fullName, phone: updated.phone });
        updateFullName(updated.fullName);
        setSaved(true);
      } catch (err) {
        const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
          setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
        } else {
          setFormError(message);
        }
      }
    });
  };
  return (
    <form className="profile-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
      <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
      <FormField id="email" label="Email" value={profile.email} readOnly disabled />
      <FormField id="fullName" label="Full name" autoComplete="name" value={form.fullName}
        onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.fullName} />
      <FormField id="phone" label="Mobile number" type="tel" inputMode="numeric" maxLength={10}
        value={form.phone} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.phone} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save profile'}</button>
      {saved && <p className="notice" role="status">Profile updated.</p>}
    </form>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx =====
export default function ProtectedRoute({ role }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, user } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location.pathname }} />;
  }
  if (role === 'ADMIN' && user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace state={{ notAuthorised: true }} />;
  }
  if (role === 'CUSTOMER' && user.role === 'ADMIN') {
    return <Navigate to="/admin/orders" replace />;
  }
  return <Outlet />;
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/QuantityStepper.jsx =====
export default function QuantityStepper({
  value, onChange, min = 1, max = 20, disabled = false, label = 'Quantity', inputReadOnly = false,
}) {
  const clamp = (n) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, n));
  const handleInput = (e) => {
    const n = Number.parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
    onChange(Number.isNaN(n) ? min : clamp(n));
  };
  return (
    <div className="stepper" role="group" aria-label={label}>
      <button type="button" aria-label="Decrease quantity"
        onClick={() => onChange(clamp(value - 1))} disabled={disabled || value <= min}>
        −
      </button>
      <input type="number" aria-label={label} min={min} max={max}
        value={value} onChange={handleInput} disabled={disabled} readOnly={inputReadOnly}/>
      <button type="button" aria-label="Increase quantity"
        onClick={() => onChange(clamp(value + 1))} disabled={disabled || value >= max}>
        +
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/components/SearchBox.jsx =====
export default function SearchBox() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const onProducts = pathname === '/products';
  const urlQ = onProducts ? params.get('q') ?? '' : '';
  const [text, setText] = useState(urlQ);
  const [seenUrlQ, setSeenUrlQ] = useState(urlQ);
  const timer = useRef(null);
  if (urlQ !== seenUrlQ) {
    setSeenUrlQ(urlQ);
    if (text.trim() !== urlQ) setText(urlQ);
  }
  const applySearch = (value) => {
    const q = value.trim();
    if (!onProducts && !q) return;
    const next = new URLSearchParams(onProducts ? params : undefined);
    if (q) next.set('q', q);
    else next.delete('q');
    next.delete('page');
    navigate({ pathname: '/products', search: next.toString() });
  };
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    setText(value);
    clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = setTimeout(() => applySearch(value), 300);
  };
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearTimeout(timer.current);
    applySearch(text);
  };
  return (
    <form className="search" role="search" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <input
        type="search"
        aria-label="Search products"
        placeholder="Search products"
        value={text}
        onChange={handleChange}
      />
    </form>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx =====
const AuthContext = createContext(null);
function readStoredUser() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [token, setToken] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY));
  const [user, setUser] = useState(readStoredUser);
  const saveSession = useCallback((authResponse) => {
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, authResponse.accessToken);
    localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(authResponse.user));
    setToken(authResponse.accessToken);
    setUser(authResponse.user);
  }, []);
  const login = useCallback(
    async (email, password) => {
      const auth = await loginUser({ email: email.trim().toLowerCase(), password });
      saveSession(auth);
      return auth.user;
    },
    [saveSession]
  );
  const register = useCallback(
    async (form) => {
      const auth = await registerUser({
        ...form,
        email: form.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        fullName: form.fullName.trim(),
        phone: form.phone.trim(),
      });
      saveSession(auth);
      return auth.user;
    },
    [saveSession]
  );
  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  }, []);
  const updateFullName = useCallback(
    (fullName) => {
      if (!user) return;
      const next = { ...user, fullName };
      localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
      setUser(next);
    },
    [user]
  );
  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      user,
      token,
      isAuthenticated: Boolean(token && user),
      isAdmin: user?.role === 'ADMIN',
      login,
      register,
      saveSession,
      logout,
      updateFullName,
    }),
    [user, token, login, register, saveSession, logout, updateFullName]
  );
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}
export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used inside <AuthProvider>');
  return ctx;
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/context/CartContext.jsx =====
const CartContext = createContext(null);
const EMPTY_CART = {
  items: [], itemCount: 0, subtotal: 0, deliveryFee: 0, total: 0, hasUnavailableItems: false,
};
export function CartProvider({ children }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  return <CartState key={user?.id ?? 'guest'}>{children}</CartState>;
}
function CartState({ children }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const enabled = isAuthenticated && !isAdmin;
  const [cart, setCart] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(enabled);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const queue = useRef(Promise.resolve());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return undefined;
    let cancelled = false;
    getCart()
      .then((next) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setCart(next);
        setError(null);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setError(parseApiError(err));
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [enabled]);
  const refreshCart = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setCart(await getCart());
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(parseApiError(err));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);
  const reloadCart = useCallback(() => {
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    return refreshCart();
  }, [refreshCart]);
  const mutate = useCallback(
    (call) => {
      const task = queue.current.then(async () => {
        try {
          const next = await call();
          setCart(next);
          return next;
        } catch (err) {
          const { code } = parseApiError(err);
          if (code === 'INSUFFICIENT_STOCK' || code === 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE') {
            refreshCart();
          }
          throw err;
        }
      });
      queue.current = task.catch(() => {});
      return task;
    },
    [refreshCart]
  );
  const addItem = useCallback(
    (productId, quantity) => mutate(() => addCartItem(productId, quantity)),
    [mutate]
  );
  const setQuantity = useCallback(
    (productId, quantity) => mutate(() => setCartItemQuantity(productId, quantity)),
    [mutate]
  );
  const removeItem = useCallback(
    (productId) => mutate(() => removeCartItem(productId)),
    [mutate]
  );
  const clear = useCallback(
    () => mutate(async () => { await clearCart(); return EMPTY_CART; }),
    [mutate]
  );
  const resetCart = useCallback(() => setCart(EMPTY_CART), []);
  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      cart,
      loading,
      error,
      itemCount: cart?.itemCount ?? 0,
      reloadCart,
      refreshCart,
      addItem,
      setQuantity,
      removeItem,
      clear,
      resetCart,
    }),
    [cart, loading, error, reloadCart, refreshCart, addItem, setQuantity, removeItem, clear, resetCart]
  );
  return <CartContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CartContext.Provider>;
}
export function useCart() {
  const ctx = useContext(CartContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useCart must be used inside <CartProvider>');
  return ctx;
}

console.log('mock flag:', import.meta.env.VITE_USE_MOCK_API, '| base:', JSON.stringify(import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL));
async function enableMocking() {
  if (import.meta.env.VITE_USE_MOCK_API !== 'true') return;
  const { worker } = await import('./mocks/browser');
  await worker.start({ onUnhandledRequest: 'warn' });
  console.info('[QuickCart] Mock API is ON. Not talking to the real backend.');
}
enableMocking().then(() => {
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <AuthProvider>
          <CartProvider>
            <App />
          </CartProvider>
        </AuthProvider>
      </BrowserRouter>
    </React.StrictMode>
  );
});

===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/db.js =====
const DB_KEY = 'quickcart.mockdb';
export function formatOrderNumber(seq, isoDate) {
  return `QC${isoDate.slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '')}-${String(seq).padStart(6, '0')}`;
}
const HOME_ADDRESS = {
  recipientName: 'Asha Rao', phone: '9876543210', line1: '12 MG Road', line2: 'Near Park',
  city: 'Hyderabad', state: 'Telangana', pincode: '500001',
};
const orderLine = (productId, productName, unitPrice, quantity) => ({
  productId, productName, unitPrice, quantity, lineTotal: unitPrice * quantity,
});
function seedOrder(id, createdAt, status, paymentMethod, paymentStatus, items, deliveryFee) {
  const subtotal = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.lineTotal, 0);
  return {
    id, orderNumber: formatOrderNumber(id, createdAt), userId: 2, status, paymentMethod, paymentStatus,
    subtotal, deliveryFee, total: subtotal + deliveryFee, deliveryAddress: HOME_ADDRESS,
    items, createdAt, updatedAt: createdAt,
  };
}
const CATEGORY_ROWS = [

const STOCK_OVERRIDES = { 8: 0, 12: 3 };
const INACTIVE_PRODUCT_IDS = [20];
function seed() {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const products = PRODUCT_ROWS.map(([categoryId, name, description, unit, price], i) => ({
    id: i + 1, categoryId, name, description, price, unit,
    imageUrl: null, active: !INACTIVE_PRODUCT_IDS.includes(i + 1),
  }));
  return {
    nextUserId: 3,
    carts: {},
    nextAddressId: 3,
    nextOrderId: 4,
    orderSeq: 3,
    nextCategoryId: 7,
    nextProductId: 21,
    inventoryUpdated: {},
    orders: [
      seedOrder(1, '2026-11-01T10:15:30Z', 'DELIVERED', 'MOCK_PAYMENT', 'PAID',
        [orderLine(5, 'Toned Milk', 28, 2), orderLine(6, 'Curd', 35, 1)], 25),
      seedOrder(2, '2026-11-03T08:30:00Z', 'OUT_FOR_DELIVERY', 'COD', 'PENDING',
        [orderLine(9, 'Brown Bread', 45, 2), orderLine(7, 'Eggs', 54, 1)], 25),
      seedOrder(3, '2026-11-04T18:45:10Z', 'CANCELLED', 'COD', 'PENDING',
        [orderLine(18, 'Cola', 40, 2)], 25),
    ],
    addresses: [
      { id: 1, userId: 2, label: 'Home', recipientName: 'Asha Rao', phone: '9876543210',
        line1: '12 MG Road', line2: 'Near Park', city: 'Hyderabad', state: 'Telangana',
        pincode: '500001', isDefault: true },
      { id: 2, userId: 2, label: 'Work', recipientName: 'Asha Rao', phone: '9876543210',
        line1: '4 Cyber Towers', line2: null, city: 'Hyderabad', state: 'Telangana',
        pincode: '500081', isDefault: false },
    ],
    users: [
      {
        id: 1, fullName: 'Store Admin', email: 'admin@quickcart.local',
        password: 'Admin12345', phone: '9000000000', role: 'ADMIN',
        active: true, createdAt: now,
      },
      {
        id: 2, fullName: 'Asha Rao', email: 'asha@example.com',
        password: 'Passw0rd123', phone: '9876543210', role: 'CUSTOMER',
        active: true, createdAt: now,
      },
    ],
    categories: CATEGORY_ROWS.map(([id, name, description, active]) => ({
      id, name, description, imageUrl: null, active,
    })),
    products,
    inventory: Object.fromEntries(products.map((p) => [p.id, STOCK_OVERRIDES[p.id] ?? 50])),
  };
}
function load() {
  const base = seed();
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(DB_KEY);
    if (raw) return { ...base, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch {
  }
  return base;
}
let state = load();
function save() {
  localStorage.setItem(DB_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
}
export const db = {
  findUserByEmail(email) {
    return state.users.find((u) => u.email === email) || null;
  },
  findUserById(id) {
    return state.users.find((u) => u.id === id) || null;
  },
  addUser({ fullName, email, password, phone }) {
    const user = {
      id: state.nextUserId++, fullName, email, password, phone,
      role: 'CUSTOMER',
      active: true, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    state.users.push(user);
    save();
    return user;
  },
  updateUser(id, changes) {
    const user = state.users.find((u) => u.id === id);
    if (!user) return null;
    Object.assign(user, changes);
    save();
    return user;
  },
  inventoryUpdatedAt(productId) {
    return state.inventoryUpdated[productId] ?? null;
  },
  addCategory({ name, description, imageUrl }) {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const category = {
      id: state.nextCategoryId, name, description, imageUrl, active: true, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
    };
    state.nextCategoryId += 1;
    state.categories.push(category);
    save();
    return category;
  },
  updateCategory(id, changes) {
    const category = state.categories.find((c) => c.id === id);
    if (!category) return null;
    Object.assign(category, changes, { updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    save();
    return category;
  },
  addProduct(fields, initialQuantity) {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const product = {
      id: state.nextProductId, ...fields, active: true, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
    };
    state.nextProductId += 1;
    state.products.push(product);
    state.inventory[product.id] = initialQuantity;
    state.inventoryUpdated[product.id] = now;
    save();
    return product;
  },
  updateProduct(id, changes) {
    const product = state.products.find((p) => p.id === id);
    if (!product) return null;
    Object.assign(product, changes, { updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    save();
    return product;
  },
  categories() {
    return state.categories;
  },
  products() {
    return state.products;
  },
  stock(productId) {
    return state.inventory[productId] ?? 0;
  },
  setStock(productId, quantity) {
    state.inventory[productId] = quantity;
    state.inventoryUpdated[productId] = new Date().toISOString();
    save();
  },
  setProductActive(productId, active) {
    const product = state.products.find((p) => p.id === productId);
    if (product) product.active = active;
    save();
  },
  cartItems(userId) {
    return state.carts[userId] ?? [];
  },
  saveCartItems(userId, items) {
    state.carts[userId] = items;
    save();
  },
  addressesOf(userId) {
    return state.addresses.filter((a) => a.userId === userId);
  },
  saveAddresses(userId, list) {
    state.addresses = [...state.addresses.filter((a) => a.userId !== userId), ...list];
    save();
  },
  nextAddressId() {
    const id = state.nextAddressId;
    state.nextAddressId += 1;
    return id;
  },
  allOrders() {
    return state.orders;
  },
  ordersOf(userId) {
    return state.orders.filter((o) => o.userId === userId);
  },
  findOrder(id) {
    return state.orders.find((o) => o.id === id) || null;
  },
  updateOrder(id, changes) {
    const order = state.orders.find((o) => o.id === id);
    if (!order) return null;
    Object.assign(order, changes);
    save();
    return order;
  },
  reserveOrderIdentity(createdAtIso) {
    const id = state.nextOrderId;
    state.nextOrderId += 1;
    state.orderSeq += 1;
    return { id, orderNumber: formatOrderNumber(state.orderSeq, createdAtIso) };
  },
  addOrder(order) {
    state.orders.push(order);
    save();
  },
  addDemoOrders(userId, count) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
      const createdAt = new Date(Date.now() - i * 3600000).toISOString();
      const { id, orderNumber } = this.reserveOrderIdentity(createdAt);
      state.orders.push({
        id, orderNumber, userId, status: 'DELIVERED', paymentMethod: 'MOCK_PAYMENT', paymentStatus: 'PAID',
        subtotal: 49, deliveryFee: 25, total: 74, deliveryAddress: HOME_ADDRESS,
        items: [orderLine(1, 'Banana', 49, 1)], createdAt, updatedAt: createdAt,
      });
    }
    save();
  },
};
export function resetMockDb() {
  state = seed();
  save();
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/errors.js =====
const REASONS = {
  400: 'Bad Request', 401: 'Unauthorized', 403: 'Forbidden', 404: 'Not Found',
  409: 'Conflict', 500: 'Internal Server Error', 503: 'Service Unavailable',
};
export function apiError(request, status, code, message, details = []) {
  return HttpResponse.json(
    {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status,
      error: REASONS[status],
      code,
      message,
      path: new URL(request.url).pathname,
      details,
    },
    { status }
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/adminCatalog.js =====
const SEED_TIME = '2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z';
const str = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '');
const len = (s, min, max) => s.length >= min && s.length <= max;
const optionalText = (v, max) => v == null || (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim().length <= max);
const isInt = (v, min, max) => Number.isInteger(v) && v >= min && v <= max;
const validPrice = (v) =>
  typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0 && v <= 99999.99 && Math.round(v * 100) / 100 === v;
const findCategory = (id) => db.categories().find((c) => c.id === id);
const findProduct = (id) => db.products().find((p) => p.id === id);
const nameTaken = (name, exceptId) =>
  db.categories().some((c) => c.id !== exceptId && c.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
const toCategory = ({ id, name, description, imageUrl, active }) => ({ id, name, description, imageUrl, active });
function toAdminProduct(p) {
  const qty = db.stock(p.id);
  return {
    id: p.id, categoryId: p.categoryId, categoryName: findCategory(p.categoryId).name,
    name: p.name, description: p.description, price: p.price, unit: p.unit, imageUrl: p.imageUrl,
    availableQuantity: qty, inStock: qty > 0,
    active: p.active, createdAt: p.createdAt ?? SEED_TIME, updatedAt: p.updatedAt ?? p.createdAt ?? SEED_TIME,
  };
}
const toInventoryItem = (p) => ({
  productId: p.id, productName: p.name, unit: p.unit, quantity: db.stock(p.id),
  updatedAt: db.inventoryUpdatedAt(p.id) ?? p.updatedAt ?? SEED_TIME,
});
const badBody = (request, details = []) =>
  apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
    details.length ? 'Please correct the highlighted fields.' : 'Malformed JSON request.', details);
function validateCategory(b) {
  const d = [];
  if (!len(str(b.name), 2, 80)) d.push({ field: 'name', message: 'size must be between 2 and 80' });
  if (!optionalText(b.description, 255)) d.push({ field: 'description', message: 'size must be at most 255' });
  if (!optionalText(b.imageUrl, 500)) d.push({ field: 'imageUrl', message: 'size must be at most 500' });
  return d;
}
const categoryFields = (b) => ({
  name: str(b.name), description: str(b.description) || null, imageUrl: str(b.imageUrl) || null,
});
function validateProductDetails(b, creating) {
  const d = [];
  if (!isInt(b.categoryId, 1, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)) d.push({ field: 'categoryId', message: 'must be a positive number' });
  if (!len(str(b.name), 2, 150)) d.push({ field: 'name', message: 'size must be between 2 and 150' });
  if (!optionalText(b.description, 1000)) d.push({ field: 'description', message: 'size must be at most 1000' });
  if (!len(str(b.unit), 1, 30)) d.push({ field: 'unit', message: 'size must be between 1 and 30' });
  if (!optionalText(b.imageUrl, 500)) d.push({ field: 'imageUrl', message: 'size must be at most 500' });
  if (creating) {
    if (!validPrice(b.price)) d.push({ field: 'price', message: 'must be above 0, at most 99999.99, with up to 2 decimals' });
    if (b.initialQuantity != null && !isInt(b.initialQuantity, 0, 100000)) {
      d.push({ field: 'initialQuantity', message: 'must be between 0 and 100000' });
    }
  }
  return d;
}
const productFields = (b) => ({
  categoryId: b.categoryId, name: str(b.name), description: str(b.description) || null,
  unit: str(b.unit), imageUrl: str(b.imageUrl) || null,
});
function pageOf(list, page, size) {
  return {
    content: list.slice(page * size, (page + 1) * size),
    page, size, totalElements: list.length, totalPages: Math.ceil(list.length / size),
  };
}
const admin = (request) => requireRole(request, 'ADMIN');
const byName = (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
export const adminCatalogHandlers = [
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/categories', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    return HttpResponse.json([...db.categories()].sort(byName).map(toCategory));
  }),
  http.post('*/api/v1/admin/categories', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateCategory(body);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const fields = categoryFields(body);
    if (nameTaken(fields.name)) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS', 'A category with this name already exists.');
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(toCategory(db.addCategory(fields)), { status: 201 });
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/admin/categories/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateCategory(body);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    if (!findCategory(id)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Category not found.');
    const fields = categoryFields(body);
    if (nameTaken(fields.name, id)) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'CATEGORY_ALREADY_EXISTS', 'A category with this name already exists.');
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(toCategory(db.updateCategory(id, fields)));
  }),
  http.patch('*/api/v1/admin/categories/:id/status', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    if (typeof body.active !== 'boolean') return badBody(request, [{ field: 'active', message: 'must be true or false' }]);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    if (!findCategory(id)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Category not found.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toCategory(db.updateCategory(id, { active: body.active })));
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/products', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const sp = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    const page = intParam(sp.get('page'), 0);
    const size = intParam(sp.get('size'), 20);
    const categoryId = intParam(sp.get('categoryId'), null);
    const active = sp.get('active');
    const details = [];
    if (Number.isNaN(page) || page < 0) details.push({ field: 'page', message: 'must be 0 or greater' });
    if (Number.isNaN(size) || size < 1 || size > 50) details.push({ field: 'size', message: 'must be between 1 and 50' });
    if (Number.isNaN(categoryId)) details.push({ field: 'categoryId', message: 'must be a number' });
    if (active !== null && active !== 'true' && active !== 'false') details.push({ field: 'active', message: 'must be true or false' });
    if (details.length) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request parameters.', details);
    const q = (sp.get('q') ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = [...db.products()];
    if (q) list = list.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if (categoryId !== null) list = list.filter((p) => p.categoryId === categoryId);
    if (active !== null) list = list.filter((p) => p.active === (active === 'true'));
    list.sort(byName);
    const result = pageOf(list, page, size);
    return HttpResponse.json({ ...result, content: result.content.map(toAdminProduct) });
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/products/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const product = findProduct(Number(params.id));
    if (!product) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toAdminProduct(product));
  }),
  http.post('*/api/v1/admin/products', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(400);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateProductDetails(body, true);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    if (!findCategory(body.categoryId)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Category not found.');
    const product = db.addProduct({ ...productFields(body), price: body.price }, body.initialQuantity ?? 0);
    return HttpResponse.json(toAdminProduct(product), { status: 201 });
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/admin/products/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateProductDetails(body, false);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    if (!findProduct(id)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    if (!findCategory(body.categoryId)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Category not found.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toAdminProduct(db.updateProduct(id, productFields(body))));
  }),
  http.patch('*/api/v1/admin/products/:id/price', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    if (!validPrice(body.price)) {
      return badBody(request, [{ field: 'price', message: 'must be above 0, at most 99999.99, with up to 2 decimals' }]);
    }
    const id = Number(params.id);
    if (!findProduct(id)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toAdminProduct(db.updateProduct(id, { price: body.price })));
  }),
  http.patch('*/api/v1/admin/products/:id/status', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    if (typeof body.active !== 'boolean') return badBody(request, [{ field: 'active', message: 'must be true or false' }]);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    if (!findProduct(id)) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toAdminProduct(db.updateProduct(id, { active: body.active })));
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/inventory', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const sp = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    const page = intParam(sp.get('page'), 0);
    const size = intParam(sp.get('size'), 20);
    const details = [];
    if (Number.isNaN(page) || page < 0) details.push({ field: 'page', message: 'must be 0 or greater' });
    if (Number.isNaN(size) || size < 1 || size > 50) details.push({ field: 'size', message: 'must be between 1 and 50' });
    if (details.length) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request parameters.', details);
    const q = (sp.get('q') ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = [...db.products()];
    if (q) list = list.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    list.sort(byName);
    const result = pageOf(list, page, size);
    return HttpResponse.json({ ...result, content: result.content.map(toInventoryItem) });
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/admin/inventory/:productId', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = admin(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    if (!isInt(body.quantity, 0, 100000)) {
      return badBody(request, [{ field: 'quantity', message: 'must be a whole number between 0 and 100000' }]);
    }
    const product = findProduct(Number(params.productId));
    if (!product) return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    db.setStock(product.id, body.quantity);
    return HttpResponse.json(toInventoryItem(product));
  }),
];

const STATUSES = ['PLACED', 'CONFIRMED', 'PACKED', 'OUT_FOR_DELIVERY', 'DELIVERED', 'CANCELLED'];
const ALLOWED = {
  PLACED: ['CONFIRMED', 'CANCELLED'],
  CONFIRMED: ['PACKED', 'CANCELLED'],
  PACKED: ['OUT_FOR_DELIVERY'],
  OUT_FOR_DELIVERY: ['DELIVERED'],
  DELIVERED: [],
  CANCELLED: [],
};
export const catalogControl = { down: false };
const toAdminSummary = (o) => ({
  id: o.id, orderNumber: o.orderNumber, userId: o.userId,
  recipientName: o.deliveryAddress.recipientName,
  status: o.status, paymentStatus: o.paymentStatus, total: o.total,
  itemCount: o.items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.quantity, 0), createdAt: o.createdAt,
});
const notFound = (request) => apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Order not found.');
export const adminOrderHandlers = [
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/orders', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'ADMIN');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const sp = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    const page = intParam(sp.get('page'), 0);
    const size = intParam(sp.get('size'), 20);
    const status = sp.get('status');
    const details = [];
    if (Number.isNaN(page) || page < 0) details.push({ field: 'page', message: 'must be 0 or greater' });
    if (Number.isNaN(size) || size < 1 || size > 50) details.push({ field: 'size', message: 'must be between 1 and 50' });
    if (status !== null && !STATUSES.includes(status)) details.push({ field: 'status', message: 'must be a valid order status' });
    if (details.length) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request parameters.', details);
    let list = [...db.allOrders()];
    if (status) list = list.filter((o) => o.status === status);
    list.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt) || b.id - a.id);
    return HttpResponse.json({
      content: list.slice(page * size, (page + 1) * size).map(toAdminSummary),
      page, size, totalElements: list.length, totalPages: Math.ceil(list.length / size),
    });
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/admin/orders/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'ADMIN');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const order = db.findOrder(Number(params.id));
    if (!order) return notFound(request);
    return HttpResponse.json({ ...toOrder(order), userId: order.userId });
  }),
  http.patch('*/api/v1/admin/orders/:id/status', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(400);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'ADMIN');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    if (!STATUSES.includes(body.status)) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.',
        [{ field: 'status', message: 'must be a valid order status' }]);
    }
    const order = db.findOrder(Number(params.id));
    if (!order) return notFound(request);
    if (!(ALLOWED[order.status] ?? []).includes(body.status)) {
            return apiError(request, 409, 'INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION',
        `Cannot change order status from ${order.status} to ${body.status}.`,
        [{ field: 'status', message: `Allowed: [${(ALLOWED[order.status] ?? []).join(', ')}]` }]);
    }
    if (body.status === 'CANCELLED') {
      if (catalogControl.down) {
        return apiError(request, 503, 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',
          'A dependent service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.');
      }
      order.items.forEach((i) => db.setStock(i.productId, db.stock(i.productId) + i.quantity));
    }
    const updated = db.updateOrder(order.id, {
      status: body.status,
      paymentStatus: order.paymentMethod === 'COD' && body.status === 'DELIVERED' ? 'PAID' : order.paymentStatus,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
    return HttpResponse.json(toOrder(updated));
  }),
];
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/auth.js =====
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const PHONE_RE = /^[6-9][0-9]{9}$/;
function authResponse(user) {
  return {
    accessToken: signToken(user),
    tokenType: 'Bearer',
    expiresIn: 3600,
    user: { id: user.id, fullName: user.fullName, email: user.email, role: user.role },
  };
}
function validateRegister(b) {
  const d = [];
  const name = typeof b.fullName === 'string' ? b.fullName.trim() : '';
  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 100) d.push({ field: 'fullName', message: 'size must be between 2 and 100' });
  const email = typeof b.email === 'string' ? b.email.trim() : '';
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email) || email.length > 150) d.push({ field: 'email', message: 'must be a well-formed email address' });
  const pw = typeof b.password === 'string' ? b.password : '';
  if (pw.length < 8 || pw.length > 72 || !/[A-Za-z]/.test(pw) || !/[0-9]/.test(pw)) {
    d.push({ field: 'password', message: 'must be 8-72 characters with at least one letter and one digit' });
  }
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(typeof b.phone === 'string' ? b.phone.trim() : '')) {
    d.push({ field: 'phone', message: 'must be a valid 10-digit mobile number' });
  }
  return d;
}
function validateLogin(b) {
  const d = [];
  if (typeof b.email !== 'string' || !EMAIL_RE.test(b.email.trim())) d.push({ field: 'email', message: 'must be a well-formed email address' });
  if (typeof b.password !== 'string' || !b.password) d.push({ field: 'password', message: 'must not be blank' });
  return d;
}
async function readJson(request) {
  try {
    return await request.json();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
export const authHandlers = [
  http.post('*/api/v1/auth/register', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(400);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    const details = validateRegister(body);
    if (details.length) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.', details);
    }
    const email = body.email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (db.findUserByEmail(email)) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS', 'An account with this email already exists.');
    }
    const user = db.addUser({
      fullName: body.fullName.trim(), email, password: body.password, phone: body.phone.trim(),
    });
    return HttpResponse.json(authResponse(user), { status: 201 });
  }),
  http.post('*/api/v1/auth/login', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(400);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    const details = validateLogin(body);
    if (details.length) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.', details);
    }
    const user = db.findUserByEmail(body.email.trim().toLowerCase());
    if (!user || user.password !== body.password || !user.active) {
      return apiError(request, 401, 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 'Invalid email or password.');
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(authResponse(user), { status: 200 });
  }),
];
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/cart.js =====
const DELIVERY_FEE = 25;
const FREE_DELIVERY_THRESHOLD = 200;
const MAX_LINE_QTY = 20;
const round2 = (n) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
const findProduct = (id) => db.products().find((p) => p.id === id);
const isVisible = (p) => p.active && Boolean(db.categories().find((c) => c.id === p.categoryId)?.active);
const isIntInRange = (v, min, max) => Number.isInteger(v) && v >= min && v <= max;
function toLine(item) {
  const p = findProduct(item.productId);
  const stock = p ? db.stock(p.id) : 0;
  const available = Boolean(p) && isVisible(p) && stock >= item.quantity;
  return {
    productId: item.productId,
    name: p ? p.name : 'Unavailable item',
    unit: p ? p.unit : null,
    imageUrl: p ? p.imageUrl : null,
    unitPrice: available ? p.price : 0,
    quantity: item.quantity,
    lineTotal: available ? round2(p.price * item.quantity) : 0,
    availableQuantity: stock,
    available,
  };
}
export function buildCart(userId) {
  const items = db.cartItems(userId).map(toLine);
  const purchasable = items.filter((l) => l.available);
  const subtotal = round2(purchasable.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.lineTotal, 0));
  const itemCount = purchasable.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.quantity, 0);
  const deliveryFee = itemCount === 0 ? 0 : subtotal >= FREE_DELIVERY_THRESHOLD ? 0 : DELIVERY_FEE;
  return {
    items,
    itemCount,
    subtotal,
    deliveryFee,
    total: round2(subtotal + deliveryFee),
    hasUnavailableItems: items.some((l) => !l.available),
  };
}
const stockError = (request, productId, requested, available) =>
  apiError(request, 409, 'INSUFFICIENT_STOCK', 'Some items are not available in the requested quantity.', [
    { field: `productId:${productId}`, message: `Requested ${requested}, available ${available}` },
  ]);
export const cartHandlers = [
  http.get('*/api/v1/cart', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    return HttpResponse.json(buildCart(Number(auth.claims.sub)));
  }),
  http.post('*/api/v1/cart/items', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = Number(auth.claims.sub);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    const details = [];
    if (!isIntInRange(body.productId, 1, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)) details.push({ field: 'productId', message: 'must be a positive number' });
    if (!isIntInRange(body.quantity, 1, MAX_LINE_QTY)) details.push({ field: 'quantity', message: `must be between 1 and ${MAX_LINE_QTY}` });
    if (details.length) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.', details);
    const product = findProduct(body.productId);
    if (!product || !isVisible(product)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    }
    const items = db.cartItems(userId);
    const existing = items.find((i) => i.productId === product.id);
    const resulting = (existing?.quantity ?? 0) + body.quantity;
    if (resulting > MAX_LINE_QTY) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', `You can add at most ${MAX_LINE_QTY} of one item.`, [
        { field: 'quantity', message: `resulting quantity ${resulting} exceeds ${MAX_LINE_QTY}` },
      ]);
    }
    const stock = db.stock(product.id);
    if (resulting > stock) return stockError(request, product.id, resulting, stock);
    db.saveCartItems(
      userId,
      existing
        ? items.map((i) => (i.productId === product.id ? { ...i, quantity: resulting } : i))
        : [...items, { productId: product.id, quantity: resulting }]
    );
    return HttpResponse.json(buildCart(userId));
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/cart/items/:productId', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = Number(auth.claims.sub);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    if (!isIntInRange(body.quantity, 1, MAX_LINE_QTY)) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.', [
        { field: 'quantity', message: `must be between 1 and ${MAX_LINE_QTY}` },
      ]);
    }
    const productId = Number(params.productId);
    const items = db.cartItems(userId);
    if (!items.some((i) => i.productId === productId)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND', 'That item is not in your cart.');
    }
    const product = findProduct(productId);
    if (!product || !isVisible(product)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    }
    const stock = db.stock(productId);
    if (body.quantity > stock) return stockError(request, productId, body.quantity, stock);
    db.saveCartItems(userId, items.map((i) => (i.productId === productId ? { ...i, quantity: body.quantity } : i)));
    return HttpResponse.json(buildCart(userId));
  }),
  http.delete('*/api/v1/cart/items/:productId', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = Number(auth.claims.sub);
    const productId = Number(params.productId);
    const items = db.cartItems(userId);
    if (!items.some((i) => i.productId === productId)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND', 'That item is not in your cart.');
    }
    db.saveCartItems(userId, items.filter((i) => i.productId !== productId));
    return HttpResponse.json(buildCart(userId));
  }),
  http.delete('*/api/v1/cart', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    db.saveCartItems(Number(auth.claims.sub), []);
    return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
  }),
];
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/catalog.js =====
const SORTS = ['name', 'price_asc', 'price_desc'];
const categoryOf = (id) => db.categories().find((c) => c.id === id);
function isVisible(p) {
  const c = categoryOf(p.categoryId);
  return p.active && Boolean(c?.active);
}
function toProduct(p) {
  const qty = db.stock(p.id);
  return {
    id: p.id,
    categoryId: p.categoryId,
    categoryName: categoryOf(p.categoryId).name,
    name: p.name,
    description: p.description,
    price: p.price,
    unit: p.unit,
    imageUrl: p.imageUrl,
    availableQuantity: qty,
    inStock: qty > 0,
  };
}
function intParam(value, fallback) {
  if (value === null) return fallback;
  return /^-?\d+$/.test(value) ? Number(value) : NaN;
}
export const catalogHandlers = [
  http.get('*/api/v1/categories', async () => {
    await delay(300);
    const list = db
      .categories()
      .filter((c) => c.active)
      .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
      .map(({ id, name, description, imageUrl, active }) => ({ id, name, description, imageUrl, active }));
    return HttpResponse.json(list);
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/products', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const sp = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    const page = intParam(sp.get('page'), 0);
    const size = intParam(sp.get('size'), 20);
    const sort = sp.get('sort') ?? 'name';
    const categoryId = intParam(sp.get('categoryId'), null);
    const details = [];
    if (Number.isNaN(page) || page < 0) details.push({ field: 'page', message: 'must be 0 or greater' });
    if (Number.isNaN(size) || size < 1 || size > 50) details.push({ field: 'size', message: 'must be between 1 and 50' });
    if (!SORTS.includes(sort)) details.push({ field: 'sort', message: 'must be one of name, price_asc, price_desc' });
    if (Number.isNaN(categoryId)) details.push({ field: 'categoryId', message: 'must be a number' });
    if (details.length) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request parameters.', details);
    }
    const q = (sp.get('q') ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = db.products().filter(isVisible);
    if (categoryId !== null) list = list.filter((p) => p.categoryId === categoryId);
    if (q) list = list.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    const byName = (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    if (sort === 'price_asc') list.sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price || byName(a, b));
    else if (sort === 'price_desc') list.sort((a, b) => b.price - a.price || byName(a, b));
    else list.sort(byName);
    return HttpResponse.json({
      content: list.slice(page * size, (page + 1) * size).map(toProduct),
      page,
      size,
      totalElements: list.length,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(list.length / size),
    });
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/products/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    const product = Number.isInteger(id) ? db.products().find((p) => p.id === id) : undefined;
    if (!product || !isVisible(product)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Product not found.');
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(toProduct(product));
  }),
];

===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/orders.js =====
const PAYMENT_METHODS = ['MOCK_PAYMENT', 'COD'];
const PHONE_RE = /^[6-9][0-9]{9}$/;
const PINCODE_RE = /^[1-9][0-9]{5}$/;
export const raceControl = { productId: null };
const str = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '');
const inRange = (s, min, max) => s.length >= min && s.length <= max;
const userIdOf = (auth) => Number(auth.claims.sub);
function validatePlaceOrder(b) {
  const d = [];
  if (!PAYMENT_METHODS.includes(b.paymentMethod)) {
    d.push({ field: 'paymentMethod', message: 'must be MOCK_PAYMENT or COD' });
  }
  const a = b.deliveryAddress;
  if (!a || typeof a !== 'object') {
    d.push({ field: 'deliveryAddress', message: 'must not be null' });
    return d;
  }
  const p = 'deliveryAddress.';
  if (!inRange(str(a.recipientName), 2, 100)) d.push({ field: `${p}recipientName`, message: 'size must be between 2 and 100' });
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(str(a.phone))) d.push({ field: `${p}phone`, message: 'must be a valid 10-digit mobile number' });
  if (!inRange(str(a.line1), 3, 150)) d.push({ field: `${p}line1`, message: 'size must be between 3 and 150' });
  if (a.line2 != null && (typeof a.line2 !== 'string' || a.line2.trim().length > 150)) {
    d.push({ field: `${p}line2`, message: 'size must be at most 150' });
  }
  if (!inRange(str(a.city), 2, 60)) d.push({ field: `${p}city`, message: 'size must be between 2 and 60' });
  if (!inRange(str(a.state), 2, 60)) d.push({ field: `${p}state`, message: 'size must be between 2 and 60' });
  if (!PINCODE_RE.test(str(a.pincode))) d.push({ field: `${p}pincode`, message: 'must be a valid 6-digit pincode' });
  return d;
}
export const toOrder = (o) => ({
  id: o.id, orderNumber: o.orderNumber, status: o.status,
  paymentMethod: o.paymentMethod, paymentStatus: o.paymentStatus,
  subtotal: o.subtotal, deliveryFee: o.deliveryFee, total: o.total,
  deliveryAddress: { ...o.deliveryAddress },
  items: o.items.map((i) => ({ ...i })),
  createdAt: o.createdAt, updatedAt: o.updatedAt,
});
const toSummary = (o) => ({
  id: o.id, orderNumber: o.orderNumber, status: o.status, total: o.total,
  itemCount: o.items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.quantity, 0), createdAt: o.createdAt,
});
export function intParam(value, fallback) {
  if (value === null) return fallback;
  return /^-?\d+$/.test(value) ? Number(value) : NaN;
}
export const orderHandlers = [
  http.post('*/api/v1/orders', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(500);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = userIdOf(auth);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Malformed JSON request.');
    const details = validatePlaceOrder(body);
    if (details.length) {
      return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Please correct the highlighted fields.', details);
    }
    const cart = buildCart(userId);
    if (cart.items.length === 0) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'CART_EMPTY', 'Your cart is empty.');
    }
    const unavailable = cart.items.filter((l) => !l.available);
    if (unavailable.length > 0) {
            return apiError(request, 409, 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE', 'Some items in your cart are unavailable. Remove them to continue.',
        unavailable.map((l) => ({ field: `productId:${l.productId}`, message: 'No longer available in the requested quantity' })));
      }
    if (raceControl.productId !== null) {
      db.setStock(raceControl.productId, 0);
      raceControl.productId = null;
    }
    const shortages = [...cart.items]
      .sort((a, b) => a.productId - b.productId)
      .filter((l) => db.stock(l.productId) < l.quantity)
      .map((l) => ({
        field: `productId:${l.productId}`,
        message: `Requested ${l.quantity}, available ${db.stock(l.productId)}`,
      }));
    if (shortages.length > 0) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'INSUFFICIENT_STOCK',
        'Some items are not available in the requested quantity.', shortages);
    }
    cart.items.forEach((l) => db.setStock(l.productId, db.stock(l.productId) - l.quantity));
    const a = body.deliveryAddress;
    const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
    const { id, orderNumber } = db.reserveOrderIdentity(createdAt);
    const order = {
      id, orderNumber, userId,
      status: 'PLACED',
      paymentMethod: body.paymentMethod,
      paymentStatus: body.paymentMethod === 'MOCK_PAYMENT' ? 'PAID' : 'PENDING',
      subtotal: cart.subtotal, deliveryFee: cart.deliveryFee, total: cart.total,
      deliveryAddress: {
        recipientName: str(a.recipientName), phone: str(a.phone), line1: str(a.line1),
        line2: str(a.line2) || null, city: str(a.city), state: str(a.state), pincode: str(a.pincode),
      },
      items: cart.items.map((l) => ({
        productId: l.productId, productName: l.name, unitPrice: l.unitPrice,
        quantity: l.quantity, lineTotal: l.lineTotal,
      })),
      createdAt, updatedAt: createdAt,
    };
    db.addOrder(order);
    db.saveCartItems(userId, []);
    return HttpResponse.json(toOrder(order), { status: 201 });
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/orders', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const sp = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    const page = intParam(sp.get('page'), 0);
    const size = intParam(sp.get('size'), 20);
    const details = [];
    if (Number.isNaN(page) || page < 0) details.push({ field: 'page', message: 'must be 0 or greater' });
    if (Number.isNaN(size) || size < 1 || size > 50) details.push({ field: 'size', message: 'must be between 1 and 50' });
    if (details.length) return apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'Invalid request parameters.', details);
    const list = [...db.ordersOf(userIdOf(auth))].sort(
      (a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt) || b.id - a.id
    );
    return HttpResponse.json({
      content: list.slice(page * size, (page + 1) * size).map(toSummary),
      page, size, totalElements: list.length, totalPages: Math.ceil(list.length / size),
    });
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/orders/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const order = db.findOrder(Number(params.id));
    if (!order || order.userId !== userIdOf(auth)) {
      return apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Order not found.');
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(toOrder(order));
  }),

===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/handlers/users.js =====
const PHONE_RE = /^[6-9][0-9]{9}$/;
const PINCODE_RE = /^[1-9][0-9]{5}$/;
const MAX_ADDRESSES = 5;
const str = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '');
const inRange = (s, min, max) => s.length >= min && s.length <= max;
const userIdOf = (auth) => Number(auth.claims.sub);
const toProfile = (u) => ({
  id: u.id, fullName: u.fullName, email: u.email, phone: u.phone, role: u.role, createdAt: u.createdAt,
});
const toAddress = (a) => ({
  id: a.id, label: a.label, recipientName: a.recipientName, phone: a.phone, line1: a.line1,
  line2: a.line2, city: a.city, state: a.state, pincode: a.pincode, isDefault: a.isDefault,
});
function validateAddress(b) {
  const d = [];
  if (!inRange(str(b.label), 1, 30)) d.push({ field: 'label', message: 'size must be between 1 and 30' });
  if (!inRange(str(b.recipientName), 2, 100)) d.push({ field: 'recipientName', message: 'size must be between 2 and 100' });
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(str(b.phone))) d.push({ field: 'phone', message: 'must be a valid 10-digit mobile number' });
  if (!inRange(str(b.line1), 3, 150)) d.push({ field: 'line1', message: 'size must be between 3 and 150' });
  if (b.line2 != null && (typeof b.line2 !== 'string' || b.line2.trim().length > 150)) {
    d.push({ field: 'line2', message: 'size must be at most 150' });
  }
  if (!inRange(str(b.city), 2, 60)) d.push({ field: 'city', message: 'size must be between 2 and 60' });
  if (!inRange(str(b.state), 2, 60)) d.push({ field: 'state', message: 'size must be between 2 and 60' });
  if (!PINCODE_RE.test(str(b.pincode))) d.push({ field: 'pincode', message: 'must be a valid 6-digit pincode' });
  if (b.isDefault != null && typeof b.isDefault !== 'boolean') d.push({ field: 'isDefault', message: 'must be true or false' });
  return d;
}
const fieldsFrom = (b) => ({
  label: str(b.label), recipientName: str(b.recipientName), phone: str(b.phone),
  line1: str(b.line1), line2: str(b.line2) || null, city: str(b.city),
  state: str(b.state), pincode: str(b.pincode),
});
const notFound = (request) => apiError(request, 404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Address not found.');
const badBody = (request, details = []) =>
  apiError(request, 400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', details.length ? 'Please correct the highlighted fields.' : 'Malformed JSON request.', details);
export const userHandlers = [
  http.get('*/api/v1/users/me', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const user = db.findUserById(userIdOf(auth));
    if (!user) return apiError(request, 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'Authentication is required.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toProfile(user));
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/users/me', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request);
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = [];
    if (!inRange(str(body.fullName), 2, 100)) details.push({ field: 'fullName', message: 'size must be between 2 and 100' });
    if (!PHONE_RE.test(str(body.phone))) details.push({ field: 'phone', message: 'must be a valid 10-digit mobile number' });
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const user = db.updateUser(userIdOf(auth), { fullName: str(body.fullName), phone: str(body.phone) });
    if (!user) return apiError(request, 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'Authentication is required.');
    return HttpResponse.json(toProfile(user));
  }),
  http.get('*/api/v1/users/me/addresses', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const list = [...db.addressesOf(userIdOf(auth))].sort(
      (a, b) => Number(b.isDefault) - Number(a.isDefault) || a.id - b.id
    );
    return HttpResponse.json(list.map(toAddress));
  }),
  http.post('*/api/v1/users/me/addresses', async ({ request }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = userIdOf(auth);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateAddress(body);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const list = db.addressesOf(userId);
    if (list.length >= MAX_ADDRESSES) {
      return apiError(request, 409, 'ADDRESS_LIMIT_REACHED', `You can save up to ${MAX_ADDRESSES} addresses.`);
    }
    const makeDefault = list.length === 0 || body.isDefault === true;
    const address = { id: db.nextAddressId(), userId, ...fieldsFrom(body), isDefault: makeDefault };
    db.saveAddresses(userId, [...list.map((a) => (makeDefault ? { ...a, isDefault: false } : a)), address]);
    return HttpResponse.json(toAddress(address), { status: 201 });
  }),
  http.put('*/api/v1/users/me/addresses/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = userIdOf(auth);
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (!body) return badBody(request);
    const details = validateAddress(body);
    if (details.length) return badBody(request, details);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    const list = db.addressesOf(userId);
    if (!list.some((a) => a.id === id)) return notFound(request);
    const makeDefault = body.isDefault === true;
    let updated;
    const next = list.map((a) => {
      if (a.id === id) {
        updated = { ...a, ...fieldsFrom(body), isDefault: a.isDefault || makeDefault };
        return updated;
      }
      return makeDefault ? { ...a, isDefault: false } : a;
    });
    db.saveAddresses(userId, next);
    return HttpResponse.json(toAddress(updated));
  }),
  http.delete('*/api/v1/users/me/addresses/:id', async ({ request, params }) => {
    await delay(300);
    const auth = requireRole(request, 'CUSTOMER');
    if (auth.response) return auth.response;
    const userId = userIdOf(auth);
    const id = Number(params.id);
    const list = db.addressesOf(userId);
    const target = list.find((a) => a.id === id);
    if (!target) return notFound(request);
    let remaining = list.filter((a) => a.id !== id);
    if (target.isDefault && remaining.length > 0) {
      const oldest = remaining.reduce((min, a) => (a.id < min.id ? a : min));
      remaining = remaining.map((a) => (a.id === oldest.id ? { ...a, isDefault: true } : a));
    }
    db.saveAddresses(userId, remaining);
    return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
  }),
];
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/request.js =====
export async function readJson(request) {
  try {
    return await request.json();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
export function requireRole(request, role) {
  const claims = readToken(request);
  if (!claims) {
    return { response: apiError(request, 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'Authentication is required or your session has expired.') };
  }
  if (role && claims.role !== role) {
    return { response: apiError(request, 403, 'FORBIDDEN', 'You are not allowed to perform this action.') };
  }
  return { claims };
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/mocks/token.js =====
const b64 = (obj) =>
  btoa(JSON.stringify(obj)).replace(/=+$/, '').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\
export function signToken(user) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const payload = {
    sub: String(user.id),
    email: user.email,
    role: user.role,
    iss: 'quickcart-user-service',
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  };
  return `${b64({ alg: 'HS256', typ: 'JWT' })}.${b64(payload)}.mock-signature`;
}
export function readToken(request) {
  const header = request.headers.get('Authorization') || '';
  if (!header.startsWith('Bearer ')) return null;
  try {
    const part = header.slice(7).split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const payload = JSON.parse(atob(part));
    return payload.exp * 1000 > Date.now() ? payload : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
