===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/admin/AdminCategories.jsx =====
const BLANK = { name: '', description: '', imageUrl: '' };
export default function AdminCategories() {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getAdminCategories(signal), []);
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [deactivating, setDeactivating] = useState(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  if (loading && !data) return <Loader />;
  if (error && !data) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!data) return null;
  const save = (values) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      setNotice('');
      const creating = editing === 'new';
      if (creating) await createCategory(values);
      else await updateCategory(editing.id, values);
      setNotice(creating ? 'Category added.' : 'Category saved.');
      setEditing(null);
      reload();
    });
  const applyActive = (category, active) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      setNotice('');
      try {
        await setCategoryActive(category.id, active);
        setNotice(`${category.name} ${active ? 'activated' : 'deactivated'}.`);
      } catch (err) {
        setActionError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      } finally {
        setDeactivating(null);
        reload();
      }
    });
  return (
    <section>
      <div className="page-header">
        <h2>Categories</h2>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing('new')} disabled={busy}>Add category</button>
      </div>
      {notice && <p className="notice" role="status">{notice}</p>}
      <ErrorBanner message={actionError} />
      {data.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState message="No categories yet." />
      ) : (
        <div className={`table-scroll${loading ? ' is-refreshing' : ''}`}>
          <table className="data-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th scope="col">Name</th>
                <th scope="col">Description</th>
                <th scope="col">Status</th>
                <th scope="col">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {data.map((c) => (
                <tr key={c.id}>
                  <td>{c.name}</td>
                  <td>{c.description ?? '—'}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`tag${c.active ? '' : ' tag--muted'}`}>
                      {c.active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="row-actions">
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing(c)} disabled={busy}>Edit</button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={busy}
                      onClick={() => (c.active ? setDeactivating(c) : applyActive(c, true))}
                    >
                      {c.active ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
      {editing && (
        <Modal
          title={editing === 'new' ? 'Add category' : 'Edit category'}
          onClose={() => !busy && setEditing(null)}
        >
          <CategoryForm
            key={editing === 'new' ? 'new' : editing.id}
            initialValues={
              editing === 'new'
                ? BLANK
                : { name: editing.name, description: editing.description ?? '', imageUrl: editing.imageUrl ?? '' }
            }
            submitLabel={editing === 'new' ? 'Add category' : 'Save changes'}
            busy={busy}
            onSubmit={save}
            onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
          />
        </Modal>
      )}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={Boolean(deactivating)}
        title="Deactivate category?"
        message={deactivating
          ? `${deactivating.name} and every product in it will be hidden from customers. You can activate it again later.`
          : ''}
        confirmLabel="Deactivate"
        busy={busy}
        onConfirm={() => applyActive(deactivating, false)}
        onCancel={() => !busy && setDeactivating(null)}
      />
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/admin/AdminInventory.jsx =====
const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export default function AdminInventory() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const page = readPage(searchParams);
  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? '';
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch(
    (signal) => getInventory({ q, page, size: PAGE_SIZE }, signal),
    [q, page]
  );
  const setQuery = (value) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    if (value) next.set('q', value);
    else next.delete('q');
    next.delete('page');
    setSearchParams(next);
  };
  const goToPage = (p) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    if (p === 0) next.delete('page');
    else next.set('page', String(p));
    setSearchParams(next);
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>Inventory</h2>
      <div className="toolbar">
        <form
          key={q}
          role="search"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setQuery(String(new FormData(e.currentTarget).get('q') ?? '').trim());
          }}
        >
          <label>
            Search{' '}
            <input type="search" name="q" defaultValue={q} placeholder="Product name" />
          </label>{' '}
          <button type="submit">Search</button>
        </form>
      </div>
      {loading && <Loader />}
      {!loading && error && <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />}
      {!loading && !error && data && (
        data.totalElements === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No products found." />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="table-scroll">
              <table className="data-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col">Product</th>
                    <th scope="col">Unit</th>
                    <th scope="col">Quantity</th>
                    <th scope="col">Updated</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {data.content.map((item) => (
                    <InventoryRow key={`${item.productId}:${item.quantity}:${item.updatedAt}`} item={item} />
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            <Pagination page={data.page} totalPages={data.totalPages} onChange={goToPage} />
          </>
        )
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
function InventoryRow({ item }) {
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(item);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(item.quantity));
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const dirty = draft.trim() !== String(current.quantity);
  const handleSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    return run(async () => {
      setError('');
      setSaved(false);
      const msg = validateQuantity(draft);
      if (msg) {
        setError(msg);
        return;
      }
      try {
        const result = await setInventoryQuantity(current.productId, Number(draft.trim()));
        setCurrent(result);
        setDraft(String(result.quantity));
        setSaved(true);
      } catch (err) {
        const { message, fieldErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        setError(fieldErrors.quantity ?? message);
      }
    });
  };
  return (
    <tr>
      <td>{current.productName}</td>
      <td>{current.unit}</td>
      <td>
        <form className="inline-form" onSubmit={handleSave} noValidate>
          <input
            aria-label={`Quantity for ${current.productName}`}
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={draft}
            disabled={busy}
            aria-invalid={error ? 'true' : 'false'}
            onChange={(e) => { setSaved(false); setDraft(e.target.value); }}
          />
          <button type="submit" disabled={busy || !dirty}>{busy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save'}</button>
          {saved && <span className="tag" role="status">Saved</span>}
        </form>
        {error && <p className="form-field__error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      </td>
      <td>{formatDateTime(current.updatedAt)}</td>
    </tr>
  );
}

const notFound = (
  <EmptyState message="Order not found." action={<Link to="/admin/orders">Back to orders</Link>} />
);
export default function AdminOrderDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(id)) return notFound;
  return <AdminOrderDetailContent key={id} id={Number(id)} />;
}
function AdminOrderDetailContent({ id }) {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getAdminOrder(id, signal), [id]);
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [updated, setUpdated] = useState(null);
  const [nextStatus, setNextStatus] = useState('');
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  const [retryStatus, setRetryStatus] = useState(null);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return notFound;
    return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  }
  if (!data) return null;
  const order = updated ? { ...data, ...updated } : data;
  const options = nextStatuses(order.status);
  const applyStatus = (status) =>
    run(async () => {
              setActionError(code === 'INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION' ? message : messageWithDetails({ message, fieldErrors }));
      setNotice('');
      setRetryStatus(null);
      try {
        const result = await updateOrderStatus(id, status);
        setUpdated(result);
        setNextStatus('');
        setNotice(`Status updated to ${statusLabel(result.status)}.`);
      } catch (err) {
        const { code, message, fieldErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        setActionError(messageWithDetails({ message, fieldErrors }));
        if (code === 'INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION') {
          setUpdated(null);
          setNextStatus('');
          reload();
        } else if (code === 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE') {
          setRetryStatus(status);
        }
      } finally {
        setConfirming(false);
      }
    });
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!options.includes(nextStatus)) return;
    if (nextStatus === 'CANCELLED') setConfirming(true);
    else applyStatus(nextStatus);
  };
  return (
    <article className="order-detail">
      <p><Link to="/admin/orders">← All orders</Link></p>
      <header className="order-detail__header">
        <div>
          <h2>Order {order.orderNumber}</h2>
          <p className="muted">
            Placed on {formatDateTime(order.createdAt)} · Customer: user #{order.userId}
          </p>
        </div>
        <OrderStatusBadge status={order.status} />
      </header>
      <OrderTimeline status={order.status} />
      {notice && <p className="notice" role="status">{notice}</p>}
      <ErrorBanner
        message={actionError}
        onRetry={retryStatus ? () => applyStatus(retryStatus) : undefined}
      />
      {options.length > 0 ? (
        <form className="status-panel" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label>
            Move order to{' '}
            <select value={nextStatus} onChange={(e) => setNextStatus(e.target.value)} disabled={busy}>
              <option value="">Choose next status</option>
              {options.map((s) => (
                <option key={s} value={s}>{statusLabel(s)}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <button type="submit" disabled={busy || !nextStatus}>
            {busy ? 'Updating...' : 'Update status'}
          </button>
        </form>
      ) : (
        <p className="muted">No further status changes are possible for this order.</p>
      )}
      <OrderBody order={order} />
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={confirming}
        title="Cancel this order?"
        message={`Order ${order.orderNumber} will be cancelled and its items returned to stock. This cannot be undone.`}
        confirmLabel="Cancel order"
        cancelLabel="Keep order"
        busy={busy}
        onConfirm={() => applyStatus('CANCELLED')}
        onCancel={() => !busy && setConfirming(false)}
      />
    </article>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/admin/AdminProductForm.jsx =====
const FIELD_ORDER = ['categoryId', 'name', 'description', 'unit', 'imageUrl', 'price', 'initialQuantity'];
const notFound = (
  <EmptyState message="Product not found." action={<Link to="/admin/products">Back to products</Link>} />
);
export default function AdminProductForm() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  if (id === undefined) return <CreateLoader />;
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(id)) return notFound;
  return <EditLoader key={id} id={Number(id)} />;
}
function CreateLoader() {
  const { data: categories, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getAdminCategories(signal), []);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!categories) return null;
  return <ProductEditor categories={categories} />;
}
function EditLoader({ id }) {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch(async (signal) => {
    const [categories, product] = await Promise.all([
      getAdminCategories(signal),
      getAdminProduct(id, signal),
    ]);
    return { categories, product };
  }, [id]);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return notFound;
    return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  }
  if (!data) return null;
  return <ProductEditor categories={data.categories} product={data.product} />;
}
function validateDetails(f, creating) {
  const errors = {};
  const add = (field, msg) => { if (msg) errors[field] = msg; };
  if (!f.categoryId) errors.categoryId = 'Choose a category.';
  add('name', validateProductName(f.name));
  add('description', validateMaxLength(f.description, 1000, 'Description'));
  add('unit', validateUnit(f.unit));
  add('imageUrl', validateMaxLength(f.imageUrl, 500, 'Image URL'));
  if (creating) {
    add('price', validatePrice(f.price));
    if (f.initialQuantity.trim()) add('initialQuantity', validateQuantity(f.initialQuantity));
  }
  return errors;
}
const detailsBody = (f) => ({
  categoryId: Number(f.categoryId),
  name: f.name.trim(),
  description: f.description.trim() || null,
  unit: f.unit.trim(),
  imageUrl: f.imageUrl.trim() || null,
});
function ProductEditor({ categories, product }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const isEdit = Boolean(product);
  const detailsLock = useSubmitLock();
  const priceLock = useSubmitLock();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    categoryId: product ? String(product.categoryId) : '',
    name: product?.name ?? '',
    description: product?.description ?? '',
    unit: product?.unit ?? '',
    imageUrl: product?.imageUrl ?? '',
    price: '',
    initialQuantity: '',
  });
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [currentPrice, setCurrentPrice] = useState(product?.price ?? null);
  const [priceDraft, setPriceDraft] = useState(product ? product.price.toFixed(2) : '');
  const [priceError, setPriceError] = useState('');
  const [priceNotice, setPriceNotice] = useState('');
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    setNotice('');
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    return detailsLock.run(async () => {
      setFormError('');
      setNotice('');
      const errors = validateDetails(form, !isEdit);
      setFieldErrors(errors);
      const first = FIELD_ORDER.find((f) => errors[f]);
      if (first) {
        document.getElementById(first)?.focus();
        return;
      }
      try {
        if (isEdit) {
          const updated = await updateProduct(product.id, detailsBody(form));
          setCurrentPrice(updated.price);
          setNotice('Product details saved.');
        } else {
          await createProduct({
            ...detailsBody(form),
            price: Number(form.price.trim()),
            initialQuantity: form.initialQuantity.trim() ? Number(form.initialQuantity.trim()) : undefined,
          });
          navigate('/admin/products', { state: { notice: `${form.name.trim()} created.` } });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
          setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
        } else {
          setFormError(message);
        }
      }
    });
  };
  const handlePrice = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    return priceLock.run(async () => {
      setPriceError('');
      setPriceNotice('');
      const msg = validatePrice(priceDraft);
      if (msg) {
        setPriceError(msg);
        return;
      }
      try {
        const updated = await updateProductPrice(product.id, Number(priceDraft.trim()));
        setCurrentPrice(updated.price);
        setPriceDraft(updated.price.toFixed(2));
        setPriceNotice('Price updated.');
      } catch (err) {
        const { message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        setPriceError(serverErrors.price ?? message);
      }
    });
  };
  return (
    <section className="product-editor">
      <p><Link to="/admin/products">← All products</Link></p>
      <h2>{isEdit ? `Edit ${product.name}` : 'New product'}</h2>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="editor-card">
        <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
        {notice && <p className="notice" role="status">{notice}</p>}
        <FormField id="categoryId" label="Category" as="select" value={form.categoryId}
          onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.categoryId}>
          <option value="">Select a category</option>
          {categories.map((c) => (
            <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}{c.active ? '' : ' (inactive)'}</option>
          ))}
        </FormField>
        <FormField id="name" label="Name" value={form.name} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.name} />
        <FormField id="description" label="Description (optional)" as="textarea" rows={3}
          value={form.description} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.description} />
        <FormField id="unit" label="Unit (e.g. 500 ml)" value={form.unit}
          onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.unit} />
        <FormField id="imageUrl" label="Image URL (optional)" value={form.imageUrl}
          onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.imageUrl} />
        {!isEdit && (
          <>
            <FormField id="price" label="Price (INR)" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="e.g. 28.00"
              value={form.price} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.price} />
            <FormField id="initialQuantity" label="Initial stock (optional, defaults to 0)" inputMode="numeric"
              value={form.initialQuantity} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.initialQuantity} />
          </>
        )}
        {isEdit && (
          <p className="muted">
            Current stock: {product.availableQuantity}.{' '}
            <Link to={`/admin/inventory?q=${encodeURIComponent(product.name)}`}>Change it on the Inventory screen</Link>.
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="address-form__actions">
          <button type="submit" disabled={detailsLock.busy}>
            {detailsLock.busy ? 'Saving...' : isEdit ? 'Save details' : 'Create product'}
          </button>
          <Link to="/admin/products" className="button-link">Cancel</Link>
        </div>
      </form>
      {isEdit && (
        <form onSubmit={handlePrice} noValidate className="editor-card">
          <h3>Price</h3>
          <p>Current price: <strong>{formatPrice(currentPrice)}</strong></p>
          <FormField id="priceDraft" label="New price (INR)" inputMode="decimal"
            value={priceDraft}
            onChange={(e) => { setPriceNotice(''); setPriceDraft(e.target.value); }}
            error={priceError} />
          <p className="muted">Carts show the new price immediately. Existing orders keep the price they were placed at.</p>
          <button type="submit" disabled={priceLock.busy}>
            {priceLock.busy ? 'Updating...' : 'Update price'}
          </button>
          {priceNotice && <p className="notice" role="status">{priceNotice}</p>}
        </form>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/admin/AdminProducts.jsx =====
const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export default function AdminProducts() {
  const { state } = useLocation();
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const page = readPage(searchParams);
  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? '';
  const rawCategory = searchParams.get('categoryId') ?? '';
  const categoryId = /^\d+$/.test(rawCategory) ? rawCategory : '';
  const rawActive = searchParams.get('active') ?? '';
  const active = rawActive === 'true' || rawActive === 'false' ? rawActive : '';
  const categories = useFetch((signal) => getAdminCategories(signal), []);
  const products = useFetch(
    (signal) => getAdminProducts({ q, categoryId, active, page, size: PAGE_SIZE }, signal),
    [q, categoryId, active, page]
  );
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState(state?.notice ?? '');
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  const setParams = (changes) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    Object.entries(changes).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value) next.set(key, value);
      else next.delete(key);
    });
    next.delete('page');
    setSearchParams(next);
  };
  const goToPage = (p) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    if (p === 0) next.delete('page');
    else next.set('page', String(p));
    setSearchParams(next);
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };
  const applyActive = (product, nextActive) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      setNotice('');
      try {
        await setProductActive(product.id, nextActive);
        setNotice(`${product.name} ${nextActive ? 'activated' : 'deactivated'}.`);
      } catch (err) {
        setActionError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      } finally {
        setConfirming(null);
        products.reload();
      }
    });
  const data = products.data;
  return (
    <section>
      <div className="page-header">
        <h2>Products</h2>
        <Link to="/admin/products/new" className="button button--inline">Add product</Link>
      </div>
      {notice && <p className="notice" role="status">{notice}</p>}
      <ErrorBanner message={actionError} />
      <ErrorBanner message={categories.error?.message} onRetry={categories.reload} />
      <div className="toolbar">
        <form
          key={q}
          role="search"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setParams({ q: String(new FormData(e.currentTarget).get('q') ?? '').trim() });
          }}
        >
          <label>
            Search{' '}
            <input type="search" name="q" defaultValue={q} placeholder="Product name" />
          </label>{' '}
          <button type="submit">Search</button>
        </form>
        <label>
          Category{' '}
          <select value={categoryId} onChange={(e) => setParams({ categoryId: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">All categories</option>
            {(categories.data ?? []).map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}{c.active ? '' : ' (inactive)'}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Status{' '}
          <select value={active} onChange={(e) => setParams({ active: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">All</option>
            <option value="true">Active</option>
            <option value="false">Inactive</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      {products.loading && !data && <Loader />}
      {!products.loading && products.error && (
        <ErrorBanner message={products.error.message} onRetry={products.reload} />
      )}
      {data && !products.error && (
        data.totalElements === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No products found." />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className={`table-scroll${products.loading ? ' is-refreshing' : ''}`}>
              <table className="data-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col">Name</th>
                    <th scope="col">Category</th>
                    <th scope="col">Price</th>
                    <th scope="col">Stock</th>
                    <th scope="col">Status</th>
                    <th scope="col">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {data.content.map((p) => (
                    <tr key={p.id}>
                      <td>
                        <Link to={`/admin/products/${p.id}/edit`}>{p.name}</Link>
                        <div className="muted">{p.unit}</div>
                      </td>
                      <td>{p.categoryName}</td>
                      <td>{formatPrice(p.price)}</td>
                      <td>{p.availableQuantity}{!p.inStock && <span className="tag tag--muted">Out</span>}</td>
                      <td>
                        <span className={`tag${p.active ? '' : ' tag--muted'}`}>
                          {p.active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="row-actions">
                        <Link to={`/admin/products/${p.id}/edit`}>Edit</Link>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={busy}
                          onClick={() => (p.active ? setConfirming(p) : applyActive(p, true))}
                        >
                          {p.active ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            <Pagination page={data.page} totalPages={data.totalPages} onChange={goToPage} />
          </>
        )
      )}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={Boolean(confirming)}
        title="Deactivate product?"
        message={confirming
          ? `${confirming.name} will be hidden from customers, and carts that contain it will show it as unavailable. Existing orders are not affected. You can activate it again later.`
          : ''}
        confirmLabel="Deactivate"
        busy={busy}
        onConfirm={() => applyActive(confirming, false)}
        onCancel={() => !busy && setConfirming(null)}
      />
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/auth/Login.jsx =====
export default function Login() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const expired = searchParams.get('expired') === '1';
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ email: '', password: '' });
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    const errors = {};
    const emailError = validateEmail(form.email);
    if (emailError) errors.email = emailError;
    if (!form.password) errors.password = 'Password is required.';
    setFieldErrors(errors);
    setFormError('');
    if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;
    inFlight.current = true;
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await login(form.email, form.password);
    } catch (err) {
      const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
      if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
        setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
      } else {
        setFormError(message);
      }
      inFlight.current = false;
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <section className="auth-card">
      <h2>Log in</h2>
      {expired && (
        <p className="notice" role="status">Session expired. Please log in again.</p>
      )}
      <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
        <FormField
          id="email"
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          autoComplete="email"
          value={form.email}
          onChange={handleChange}
          error={fieldErrors.email}
        />
        <FormField
          id="password"
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          value={form.password}
          onChange={handleChange}
          error={fieldErrors.password}
        />
        <button type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? 'Logging in...' : 'Log in'}
        </button>
      </form>
      <p>
        New here? <Link to="/register">Create an account</Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/auth/Register.jsx =====
const EMPTY = { fullName: '', email: '', phone: '', password: '' };
export default function Register() {
  const { register } = useAuth();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  };
  const validate = () => {
    const errors = {};
    const checks = {
      fullName: validateFullName(form.fullName),
      email: validateEmail(form.email),
      phone: validatePhone(form.phone),
      password: validatePassword(form.password),
    };
    Object.entries(checks).forEach(([field, msg]) => {
      if (msg) errors[field] = msg;
    });
    return errors;
  };
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    const errors = validate();
    setFieldErrors(errors);
    setFormError('');
    if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;
    inFlight.current = true;
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await register(form);
    } catch (err) {
      const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
      if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' && Object.keys(serverErrors).length > 0) {
        setFieldErrors(serverErrors);
      } else if (code === 'EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS') {
        setFieldErrors({ email: message });
      } else {
        setFormError(message);
      }
      inFlight.current = false;
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <section className="auth-card">
      <h2>Create account</h2>
      <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
        <FormField id="fullName" label="Full name" autoComplete="name"
          value={form.fullName} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.fullName} />
        <FormField id="email" label="Email" type="email" autoComplete="email"
          value={form.email} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.email} />
        <FormField id="phone" label="Mobile number" type="tel" inputMode="numeric"
          maxLength={10} autoComplete="tel"
          value={form.phone} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.phone} />
        <FormField id="password" label="Password" type="password"
          autoComplete="new-password"
          value={form.password} onChange={handleChange} error={fieldErrors.password} />
        <button type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? 'Creating account...' : 'Register'}
        </button>
      </form>
      <p>
        Already registered? <Link to="/login">Log in</Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/Cart.jsx =====
export default function Cart() {
  const { cart, loading, error, reloadCart, setQuantity, removeItem, clear } = useCart();
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  const { state } = useLocation();
  const perform = (task) =>
    run(async () => {
      setActionError('');
      try {
        await task();
      } catch (err) {
        setActionError(messageWithDetails(parseApiError(err)));
      }
    });
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (!cart) {
    return <ErrorBanner message={error?.message ?? 'Could not load your cart.'} onRetry={reloadCart} />;
  }
  if (cart.items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        message="Your cart is empty."
        action={<Link to="/products">Browse products</Link>}
      />
    );
  }
  const canCheckout = !cart.hasUnavailableItems && !busy;
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>Your cart</h2>
      <ErrorBanner message={state?.notice} />
      <ErrorBanner message={actionError} />
      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reloadCart} />}
      <div className="cart-layout">
        <ul className="cart-lines">
          {cart.items.map((line) => (
            <CartLine
              key={line.productId}
              line={line}
              busy={busy}
              onQuantityChange={(qty) => perform(() => setQuantity(line.productId, qty))}
              onRemove={() => perform(() => removeItem(line.productId))}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
        <aside className="cart-summary">
          <h3>Order summary</h3>
          <dl>
            <div><dt>Items</dt><dd>{cart.itemCount}</dd></div>
            <div><dt>Subtotal</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.subtotal)}</dd></div>
            <div><dt>Delivery fee</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.deliveryFee)}</dd></div>
            <div className="cart-summary__total"><dt>Total</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.total)}</dd></div>
          </dl>
          {cart.hasUnavailableItems && (
            <p className="cart-summary__warning">Remove unavailable items to continue.</p>
          )}
          {canCheckout ? (
            <Link to="/checkout" className="button">Checkout</Link>
          ) : (
            <button type="button" disabled>Checkout</button>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="link-button" disabled={busy}
            onClick={() => perform(clear)}>
            Clear cart
          </button>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/Checkout.jsx =====
const MAX_ADDRESSES = 5;
const FIELD_ORDER = ['recipientName', 'phone', 'line1', 'line2', 'city', 'state', 'pincode', 'saveLabel'];
const BLANK = { recipientName: '', phone: '', line1: '', line2: '', city: '', state: '', pincode: '' };
const toDeliveryAddress = (s) => ({
  recipientName: s.recipientName.trim(),
  phone: s.phone.trim(),
  line1: s.line1.trim(),
  line2: (s.line2 ?? '').trim() || null,
  city: s.city.trim(),
  state: s.state.trim(),
  pincode: s.pincode.trim(),
});
const stripPrefix = (errors) =>
  Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(errors).map(([key, msg]) => [key.replace(/^deliveryAddress\./, ''), msg])
  );
function describeProblems(message, fieldErrors, cart) {
  const parts = Object.entries(fieldErrors).map(([field, detail]) => {
    const line = cart.items.find((l) => `productId:${l.productId}` === field);
    return line ? `${line.name}: ${detail}.` : `${detail}.`;
  });
  return parts.length ? `${message} ${parts.join(' ')}` : message;
}
export default function Checkout() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { cart, loading: cartLoading, error: cartError, reloadCart, refreshCart, resetCart } = useCart();
  const addresses = useFetch((signal) => getAddresses(signal), []);
  const { busy, run } = useSubmitLock();
  const placedRef = useRef(false);
  const [placedOrderId, setPlacedOrderId] = useState(null);
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ ...BLANK, recipientName: user?.fullName ?? '' });
  const [saveIt, setSaveIt] = useState(false);
  const [saveLabel, setSaveLabel] = useState('');
  const [paymentMethod, setPaymentMethod] = useState('MOCK_PAYMENT');
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    refreshCart();
  }, [refreshCart]);
  const saved = addresses.data ?? [];
  const defaultAddress = saved.find((a) => a.isDefault) ?? saved[0];
  const effectiveSelection = selection ?? defaultAddress?.id ?? 'new';
  const usingNew = effectiveSelection === 'new';
  const canSave = usingNew && !addresses.error && saved.length < MAX_ADDRESSES;
  if (placedOrderId) {
    return <Navigate to={`/orders/${placedOrderId}`} replace state={{ justPlaced: true }} />;
  }
  if (cartLoading) return <Loader />;
  if (!cart) {
    return <ErrorBanner message={cartError?.message ?? 'Could not load your cart.'} onRetry={reloadCart} />;
  }
  if (cart.items.length === 0) return <Navigate to="/cart" replace />;
  const handleFieldChange = (e) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
  };
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (placedRef.current) return undefined;
    return run(async () => {
      setFormError('');
      let deliveryAddress;
      if (usingNew) {
        const errors = validateDeliveryFields(form);
        if (saveIt && canSave) {
          const labelError = validateLabel(saveLabel);
          if (labelError) errors.saveLabel = labelError;
        }
        setFieldErrors(errors);
        const first = FIELD_ORDER.find((f) => errors[f]);
        if (first) {
          document.getElementById(first)?.focus();
          return;
        }
        deliveryAddress = toDeliveryAddress(form);
      } else {
        setFieldErrors({});
        const chosen = saved.find((a) => a.id === effectiveSelection);
        if (!chosen) {
          setFormError('Choose a delivery address.');
          return;
        }
        deliveryAddress = toDeliveryAddress(chosen);
      }
      try {
        const order = await placeOrder({ paymentMethod, deliveryAddress });
        placedRef.current = true;
        if (usingNew && saveIt && canSave) {
          createAddress({ label: saveLabel.trim(), ...deliveryAddress, isDefault: false }).catch(() => {});
        }
        resetCart();
        setPlacedOrderId(order.id);
      } catch (err) {
        const { code, message, fieldErrors: serverErrors } = parseApiError(err);
        if (code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR') {
          setFieldErrors(stripPrefix(serverErrors));
          setFormError(message);
        } else if (code === 'CART_EMPTY') {
          refreshCart();
          navigate('/cart', { replace: true, state: { notice: message } });
        } else if (code === 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE') {
          const notice = describeProblems(message, serverErrors, cart);
          refreshCart();
          navigate('/cart', { state: { notice } });
        } else if (code === 'INSUFFICIENT_STOCK') {
          setFormError(describeProblems(message, serverErrors, cart));
          refreshCart();
        } else if (code === 'NETWORK_ERROR') {
          setFormError(`${message} Your order may have been placed, so check My orders before trying again.`);
        } else {
          setFormError(message);
        }
      }
    });
  };
  return (
    <form className="checkout-layout" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
      <div>
        <h2>Checkout</h2>
        <ErrorBanner message={formError} />
        <fieldset className="checkout-section" disabled={busy}>
          <legend>Delivery address</legend>
          {addresses.loading && <Loader />}
          {addresses.error && (
            <ErrorBanner message={addresses.error.message} onRetry={addresses.reload} />
          )}
          {!addresses.loading && (
            <>
              {saved.map((a) => (
                <label key={a.id} className="radio-card">
                  <input type="radio" name="address" checked={effectiveSelection === a.id}
                    onChange={() => setSelection(a.id)} />
                  <span>
                    <strong>{a.label}</strong>{a.isDefault && <span className="tag">Default</span>}
                    <br />{a.recipientName} · {a.phone}
                    <br />{a.line1}{a.line2 ? `, ${a.line2}` : ''}, {a.city}, {a.state} {a.pincode}
                  </span>
                </label>
              ))}
              <label className="radio-card">
                <input type="radio" name="address" checked={usingNew}
                  onChange={() => setSelection('new')} />
                <span><strong>Use a new address</strong></span>
              </label>
              {usingNew && (
                <DeliveryFields form={form} errors={fieldErrors} onChange={handleFieldChange} />
              )}
              {canSave && (
                <>
                  <label className="checkbox">
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={saveIt} onChange={(e) => setSaveIt(e.target.checked)} />
                    Save this address for next time
                  </label>
                  {saveIt && (
                    <FormField id="saveLabel" label="Label (e.g. Home)" maxLength={30}
                      value={saveLabel} onChange={(e) => setSaveLabel(e.target.value)}
                      error={fieldErrors.saveLabel} />
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </fieldset>
        <fieldset className="checkout-section" disabled={busy}>
          <legend>Payment</legend>
          {Object.entries(PAYMENT_METHOD_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => (
            <label key={value} className="radio-card">
              <input type="radio" name="paymentMethod" value={value}
                checked={paymentMethod === value} onChange={() => setPaymentMethod(value)} />
              <span>{label}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </fieldset>
      </div>
      <aside className="cart-summary">
        <h3>Order summary</h3>
        <ul className="summary-lines">
          {cart.items.map((l) => (
            <li key={l.productId}>
              <span>{l.name} × {l.quantity}</span>
              <span>{l.available ? formatPrice(l.lineTotal) : 'Unavailable'}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <dl>
          <div><dt>Subtotal</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.subtotal)}</dd></div>
          <div><dt>Delivery fee</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.deliveryFee)}</dd></div>
          <div className="cart-summary__total"><dt>Total</dt><dd>{formatPrice(cart.total)}</dd></div>
        </dl>
        {cart.hasUnavailableItems && (
          <p className="cart-summary__warning">
            Some items are unavailable. <Link to="/cart">Review your cart</Link>
          </p>
        )}
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy || cart.hasUnavailableItems}>
          {busy ? 'Placing order...' : 'Place order'}
        </button>
      </aside>
    </form>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/Home.jsx =====
export default function Home() {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch(async (signal) => {
    const [categories, products] = await Promise.all([
      getCategories(signal),
      getProducts({ page: 0, size: 12 }, signal),
    ]);
    return { categories, products };
  }, []);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!data) return null;
  const { categories, products } = data;
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>Shop by category</h2>
      <div className="chips">
        {categories.map((c) => (
          <Link key={c.id} to={`/products?categoryId=${c.id}`} className="chip">
            {c.name}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
      <h2>Products</h2>
      {products.content.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState message="No products available yet." />
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="product-grid">
            {products.content.map((p) => (
              <ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />
            ))}
          </div>
          <p><Link to="/products">View all products</Link></p>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/OrderDetail.jsx =====
const notFound = (
  <EmptyState message="Order not found." action={<Link to="/orders">Back to my orders</Link>} />
);
export default function OrderDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(id)) return notFound;
  return <OrderDetailContent key={id} id={Number(id)} />;
}
function OrderDetailContent({ id }) {
  const { state } = useLocation();
  const { data: order, loading, error, reload } = useFetch((signal) => getOrder(id, signal), [id]);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return notFound;
    return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  }
  if (!order) return null;
  return (
    <article className="order-detail">
      {state?.justPlaced && (
        <p className="notice" role="status">Order placed successfully. Thank you!</p>
      )}
      <header className="order-detail__header">
        <div>
          <h2>Order {order.orderNumber}</h2>
          <p className="muted">Placed on {formatDateTime(order.createdAt)}</p>
        </div>
        <OrderStatusBadge status={order.status} />
      </header>
      <OrderTimeline status={order.status} />
      <OrderBody order={order} />
    </article>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/Orders.jsx =====
const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export default function Orders() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const page = readPage(searchParams);
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useFetch(
    (signal) => getOrders({ page, size: PAGE_SIZE }, signal),
    [page]
  );
  const goToPage = (p) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    if (p === 0) next.delete('page');
    else next.set('page', String(p));
    setSearchParams(next);
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  if (!data) return null;
  if (data.totalElements === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        message="You haven't placed any orders yet."
        action={<Link to="/products">Start shopping</Link>}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>My orders</h2>
      <div className="table-scroll">
        <table className="data-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col">Order</th>
              <th scope="col">Date</th>
              <th scope="col">Status</th>
              <th scope="col">Items</th>
              <th scope="col">Total</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {data.content.map((o) => (
              <tr key={o.id}>
                <td><Link to={`/orders/${o.id}`}>{o.orderNumber}</Link></td>
                <td>{formatDateTime(o.createdAt)}</td>
                <td><OrderStatusBadge status={o.status} /></td>
                <td>{o.itemCount}</td>
                <td>{formatPrice(o.total)}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <Pagination page={data.page} totalPages={data.totalPages} onChange={goToPage} />
    </section>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/ProductDetail.jsx =====
const notFound = (
  <EmptyState
    message="Product not found."
    action={<Link to="/products">Back to products</Link>}
  />
);
export default function ProductDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(id)) return notFound;
  return <ProductDetailContent key={id} id={Number(id)} />;
}
function ProductDetailContent({ id }) {
  const { isAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { data: product, loading, error, reload } = useFetch(
    (signal) => getProduct(id, signal),
    [id]
  );
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
  if (loading) return <Loader />;
  if (error) {
    if (error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return notFound;
    return <ErrorBanner message={error.message} onRetry={reload} />;
  }
  if (!product) return null;
  return (
    <article className="product-detail">
      <ProductImage src={product.imageUrl} name={product.name} />
      <div className="product-detail__info">
        <p className="muted">{product.categoryName}</p>
        <h2>{product.name}</h2>
        <p className="muted">{product.unit}</p>
        {product.description && <p>{product.description}</p>}
        <p className="product-detail__price">{formatPrice(product.price)}</p>
        <p className={product.inStock ? 'in-stock' : 'out-of-stock'}>
          {product.inStock ? 'In stock' : 'Out of stock'}
        </p>
        {!isAdmin && product.inStock && (
          <div className="product-detail__actions">
            <QuantityStepper value={quantity} onChange={setQuantity} />
            <AddToCartButton product={product} quantity={quantity} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/pages/customer/ProductList.jsx =====
const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'name', label: 'Name (A-Z)' },
  { value: 'price_asc', label: 'Price: low to high' },
  { value: 'price_desc', label: 'Price: high to low' },
];
const VALID_SORTS = SORT_OPTIONS.map((o) => o.value);
export default function ProductList() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? '';
  const categoryId = searchParams.get('categoryId') ?? '';
  const rawPage = Number.parseInt(searchParams.get('page') ?? '0', 10);
  const page = Number.isNaN(rawPage) || rawPage < 0 ? 0 : rawPage;
  const rawSort = searchParams.get('sort');
  const sort = VALID_SORTS.includes(rawSort) ? rawSort : 'name';
  const categories = useFetch((signal) => getCategories(signal), []);
  const products = useFetch(
    (signal) => getProducts({ q, categoryId, page, size: PAGE_SIZE, sort }, signal),
    [q, categoryId, page, sort]
  );
  const updateParams = (changes) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    Object.entries(changes).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) next.delete(key);
      else next.set(key, String(value));
    });
    if (!('page' in changes)) next.delete('page');
    setSearchParams(next);
  };
  const goToPage = (p) => {
    updateParams({ page: p === 0 ? '' : p });
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };
  return (
    <section>
      <h2>{q ? `Results for "${q}"` : 'All products'}</h2>
      <ErrorBanner message={categories.error?.message} onRetry={categories.reload} />
      <div className="toolbar">
        <label>
          Category{' '}
          <select
            value={categoryId}
            onChange={(e) => updateParams({ categoryId: e.target.value })}
          >
            <option value="">All categories</option>
            {(categories.data ?? []).map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Sort by{' '}
          <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => updateParams({ sort: e.target.value })}>
            {SORT_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
              <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      {products.loading && <Loader />}
      {!products.loading && products.error && (
        <ErrorBanner message={products.error.message} onRetry={products.reload} />
      )}
      {!products.loading && !products.error && products.data && (
        products.data.content.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No products match your search." />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="product-grid">
              {products.data.content.map((p) => (
                <ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />
              ))}
            </div>
            <Pagination
              page={products.data.page}
              totalPages={products.data.totalPages}
              onChange={goToPage}
            />
          </>
        )
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

const optionLabels = (select) =>
  within(select)
    .getAllByRole('option')
    .filter((o) => o.value !== '')
    .map((o) => o.textContent);
describe('AdminOrderDetail status control', () => {
  it.each([
    ['PLACED', ['Confirmed', 'Cancelled']],
    ['CONFIRMED', ['Packed', 'Cancelled']],
    ['PACKED', ['Out for delivery']],
    ['OUT_FOR_DELIVERY', ['Delivered']],
  ])('offers only the allowed next statuses for %s', async (status, expected) => {
    renderDetail(status);
    const select = await screen.findByRole('combobox');
    expect(optionLabels(select)).toEqual(expected);
  });
  it.each(['DELIVERED', 'CANCELLED'])('has no control at all for the terminal status %s', async (status) => {
    renderDetail(status);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/no further status changes/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('combobox')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('asks for confirmation before cancelling, then calls the API', async () => {
    updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue({ ...order, status: 'CANCELLED' });
    const user = renderDetail('PLACED');
    await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByRole('combobox'), 'CANCELLED');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Update status' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('alertdialog');
    expect(updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel order' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(42, 'CANCELLED'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Status updated to Cancelled.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('combobox')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('does nothing when the admin backs out of the dialog', async () => {
    const user = renderDetail('PLACED');
    await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByRole('combobox'), 'CANCELLED');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Update status' }));
    await screen.findByRole('alertdialog');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep order' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

    });

    expect(screen.getByText('Enter a valid 6-digit pincode.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(placeOrder).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('shows the stock problem by product name and refreshes the cart', async () => {
    placeOrder.mockRejectedValue(
      apiFailure(409, 'INSUFFICIENT_STOCK', 'Some items are not available in the requested quantity.', [
        { field: 'productId:11', message: 'Requested 5, available 2' },
      ])
    );
    const { user } = renderCheckout();
    await screen.findByText('Home');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place order' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Toned Milk: Requested 5, available 2/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('order page')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(getCart.mock.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3));
  });
  it('sends the user back to the cart when a product is unavailable', async () => {
    placeOrder.mockRejectedValue(
      apiFailure(409, 'PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE', 'Some items in your cart are no longer available.')
    );
    const { user } = renderCheckout();
    await screen.findByText('Home');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place order' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('cart page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('sends one request when the button is double-clicked', async () => {
    placeOrder.mockReturnValue(new Promise(() => {}));
    const { user } = renderCheckout();
    await screen.findByText('Home');
    await user.dblClick(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place order' }));
    expect(placeOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

vi.mock('../api/authApi');
function renderLogin() {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/login']}>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route element={<GuestRoute />}>
            <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
          </Route>
          <Route path="/" element={<p>home page</p>} />
          <Route path="/admin/orders" element={<p>admin orders page</p>} />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </MemoryRouter>
  );
  return user;
}
async function fillAndSubmit(user) {
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'someone@example.com');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'Passw0rd123');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
}
const authResponse = (u) => ({ accessToken: 'jwt-123', tokenType: 'Bearer', expiresIn: 3600, user: u });
describe('Login', () => {
  it('stores the token and sends a customer to the home page', async () => {
    loginUser.mockResolvedValue(authResponse(customer));
    const user = renderLogin();
    await fillAndSubmit(user);
    expect(await screen.findByText('home page')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)).toBe('jwt-123');
    expect(loginUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: 'someone@example.com', password: 'Passw0rd123' });
  });
  it('sends an admin to /admin/orders', async () => {
    loginUser.mockResolvedValue(authResponse(admin));
    const user = renderLogin();
    await fillAndSubmit(user);
    expect(await screen.findByText('admin orders page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('shows the server message when the credentials are wrong', async () => {
    loginUser.mockRejectedValue(apiFailure(401, 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS', 'Invalid email or password.'));
    const user = renderLogin();
    await fillAndSubmit(user);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Invalid email or password.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText('home page')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('validates on the client before calling the API', async () => {
    const user = renderLogin();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Log in' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Email is required.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(loginUser).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

    const { user } = renderList();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Something broke.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retry' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Toned Milk')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(getProducts).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
  it('shows the products, formatted by the formatter and not by us', async () => {
    getCategories.mockResolvedValue([category]);
    getProducts.mockResolvedValue(productPage);
    renderList();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Toned Milk')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('500 ml')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('₹28.00')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('reads q, categoryId, sort and page from the URL', async () => {
    getCategories.mockResolvedValue([category]);
    getProducts.mockResolvedValue(productPage);
    renderList('/products?q=milk&categoryId=2&sort=price_desc&page=1');
    await screen.findByText('Toned Milk');
    expect(getProducts).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      { q: 'milk', categoryId: '2', page: 1, size: 12, sort: 'price_desc' },
      expect.anything()
    );
  });
});
describe('SearchBox', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());
  function Probe() {
    const { pathname, search } = useLocation();
    return <p data-testid="where">{pathname}{search}</p>;
  }
  it('waits for the typing to pause, then puts q in the URL', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const user = userEvent.setup({ advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime });
    render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/']}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="*" element={<><SearchBox /><Probe /></>} />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>
    );
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Search products'), 'milk');
    expect(screen.getByTestId('where')).not.toHaveTextContent('q=milk');
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(300);
    });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('where')).toHaveTextContent('/products?q=milk');
  });
  it('searches immediately on Enter', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/']}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="*" element={<><SearchBox /><Probe /></>} />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>
    );
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Search products'), 'curd{Enter}');
    expect(screen.getByTestId('where')).toHaveTextContent('/products?q=curd');
  });
});
===== quickcartfrontend/src/test/ProtectedRoute.test.jsx =====
function renderAt(route) {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[route]}>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<p>home page</p>} />
          <Route path="/login" element={<p>login page</p>} />
          <Route element={<ProtectedRoute role="CUSTOMER" />}>
            <Route path="/cart" element={<p>cart page</p>} />
          </Route>
          <Route element={<ProtectedRoute role="ADMIN" />}>
            <Route path="/admin/orders" element={<p>admin orders page</p>} />
          </Route>
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </MemoryRouter>
  );
}
describe('ProtectedRoute', () => {
  it('sends a guest to the login page', () => {
    renderAt('/cart');
    expect(screen.getByText('login page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('sends a guest away from admin routes too', () => {
    renderAt('/admin/orders');
    expect(screen.getByText('login page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('blocks a customer from admin routes', () => {
    loginAs(customer);
    renderAt('/admin/orders');
    expect(screen.getByText('home page')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('admin orders page')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('lets an admin into admin routes', () => {
    loginAs(admin);
    renderAt('/admin/orders');
    expect(screen.getByText('admin orders page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('keeps admins out of customer routes (Section 10.5)', () => {
    loginAs(admin);
    renderAt('/cart');
    expect(screen.getByText('admin orders page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it('lets a customer into customer routes', () => {
    loginAs(customer);
    renderAt('/cart');
    expect(screen.getByText('cart page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== quickcartfrontend/src/test/renderApp.jsx =====
export function loginAs(user) {
  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, 'test-token');
  localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
}
export function renderApp(ui, { route = '/', as = null } = {}) {
  if (as) loginAs(as);
  return {
    user: userEvent.setup(),
    ...render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[route]}>
        <AuthProvider>
          <CartProvider>{ui}</CartProvider>
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>
    ),
  };
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/test/validators.test.js =====
const check = (validator, cases) =>
  it.each(cases)('%j -> valid: %s', (input, valid) => {
    expect(validator(input) === '').toBe(valid);
  });
describe('validatePhone', () => {
  check(validatePhone, [['9876543210', true], ['5876543210', false], ['987654321', false], ['98765432101', false], ['', false]]);
});
describe('validatePincode', () => {
  check(validatePincode, [['500001', true], ['050001', false], ['50000', false], ['5000011', false]]);
});
describe('validatePassword', () => {
  check(validatePassword, [
    ['abcdefg1', true],
    ['abcdefgh', false],
    ['12345678', false],
    ['abc123', false],
    ['a1'.padEnd(72, 'x'), true],
    ['a1'.padEnd(73, 'x'), false],
  ]);
});
describe('validatePrice (text in, no arithmetic)', () => {
  check(validatePrice, [
    ['1', true], ['28.5', true], ['28.50', true], ['99999.99', true], ['0.01', true],
    ['0', false], ['0.00', false], ['100000', false], ['12.345', false],
    ['.5', false], ['28.', false], ['-5', false], ['', false], ['abc', false],
  ]);
});
describe('validateQuantity', () => {
  check(validateQuantity, [['0', true], ['100000', true], ['100001', false], ['-1', false], ['1.5', false], ['', false]]);
});
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/errorMessage.js =====
export function parseApiError(error) {
  const data = error.response.data || {};
  const fieldErrors = {};
  if (Array.isArray(data.details)) {
    data.details.forEach((d) => {
      if (d?.field && !(d.field in fieldErrors)) fieldErrors[d.field] = d.message;
    });
  }
  if (error?.isAxiosError && !error.response) {
    return {
      code: 'NETWORK_ERROR',
      message: 'Cannot reach the server. Please retry.',
      fieldErrors: {},
    };
  }
  if (!error?.response) {
    console.error('Unexpected client error:', error);
    return {
      code: 'CLIENT_ERROR',
      message: 'Something went wrong. Please try again.',
      fieldErrors: {},
    };
  }
  return {
    code: data.code || 'UNKNOWN_ERROR',
    message: data.message || 'Something went wrong. Please try again.',
    fieldErrors,
  };
}
export function messageWithDetails({ message, fieldErrors }) {
  const extra = Object.values(fieldErrors);
  return extra.length ? `${message} ${extra.join(' ')}` : message;
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/formatDate.js =====
const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-IN', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
export function formatDateTime(iso) {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? '' : formatter.format(date);
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/formatPrice.js =====
const formatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
  style: 'currency',
  currency: 'INR',
});
export function formatPrice(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return formatter.format(value);
}

===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/pageParam.js =====
export function readPage(searchParams) {
  const raw = Number.parseInt(searchParams.get('page') ?? '0', 10);
  return Number.isNaN(raw) || raw < 0 ? 0 : raw;
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/useFetch.js =====
const sameDeps = (a, b) => a.length === b.length && a.every((v, i) => Object.is(v, b[i]));
export default function useFetch(fetcher, deps) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ data: null, loading: true, error: null });
  const [tick, setTick] = useState(0);
  const [seenDeps, setSeenDeps] = useState(deps);
  if (!sameDeps(seenDeps, deps)) {
    setSeenDeps(deps);
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true, error: null }));
  }
  const reload = useCallback(() => {
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true, error: null }));
    setTick((t) => t + 1);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    fetcher(controller.signal)
      .then((data) => setState({ data, loading: false, error: null }))
      .catch((err) => {
        if (axios.isCancel(err)) return;
        setState({ data: null, loading: false, error: parseApiError(err) });
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [...deps, tick]);
  return { ...state, reload };
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/useSubmitLock.js =====
export default function useSubmitLock() {
  const locked = useRef(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const run = useCallback(async (task) => {
    if (locked.current) return undefined;
    locked.current = true;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      return await task();
    } finally {
      locked.current = false;
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }, []);
  return { busy, run };
}
===== quickcartfrontend/src/utils/validators.js =====
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const PHONE_RE = /^[6-9][0-9]{9}$/;
export function validateEmail(value) {
  const v = value.trim();
  if (!v) return 'Email is required.';
  if (v.length > 150 || !EMAIL_RE.test(v)) return 'Enter a valid email address.';
  return '';
}
export function validatePassword(value) {
  if (value.length < 8 || value.length > 72) return 'Password must be 8-72 characters.';
  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(value) || !/[0-9]/.test(value)) {
    return 'Password must contain at least one letter and one digit.';
  }
  return '';
}
export function validateFullName(value) {
  const v = value.trim();
  if (v.length < 2 || v.length > 100) return 'Name must be 2-100 characters.';
  return '';
}
export function validatePhone(value) {
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(value.trim())) return 'Enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.';
  return '';
}
const PINCODE_RE = /^[1-9][0-9]{5}$/;
const within = (v, min, max) => v.length >= min && v.length <= max;
export function validateLabel(value) {
  return within(value.trim(), 1, 30) ? '' : 'Label must be 1-30 characters.';
}
export function validateLine1(value) {
  return within(value.trim(), 3, 150) ? '' : 'Address line must be 3-150 characters.';
}
export function validateLine2(value) {
  return value.trim().length <= 150 ? '' : 'Line 2 must be at most 150 characters.';
}
export function validateCityOrState(value, what) {
  return within(value.trim(), 2, 60) ? '' : `${what} must be 2-60 characters.`;
}
export function validatePincode(value) {
  return PINCODE_RE.test(value.trim()) ? '' : 'Enter a valid 6-digit pincode.';
}
export function validateDeliveryFields(f) {
  const checks = {
    recipientName: validateFullName(f.recipientName),
    phone: validatePhone(f.phone),
    line1: validateLine1(f.line1),
    line2: validateLine2(f.line2),
    city: validateCityOrState(f.city, 'City'),
    state: validateCityOrState(f.state, 'State'),
    pincode: validatePincode(f.pincode),
  };
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(checks).filter(([, msg]) => msg));
}
export function validateCategoryName(value) {
  return within(value.trim(), 2, 80) ? '' : 'Name must be 2-80 characters.';
}
export function validateMaxLength(value, max, what) {
  return value.trim().length <= max ? '' : `${what} must be at most ${max} characters.`;
}
export function validateProductName(value) {
  return within(value.trim(), 2, 150) ? '' : 'Name must be 2-150 characters.';
}
export function validateUnit(value) {
  return within(value.trim(), 1, 30) ? '' : 'Unit must be 1-30 characters.';
}
export function validatePrice(value) {
  const t = value.trim();
  if (!/^\d{1,5}(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(t) || !/[1-9]/.test(t)) {
    return 'Enter a price above 0, up to 99999.99, with at most 2 decimals.';
  }
  return '';
}
export function validateQuantity(value) {
  const t = value.trim();
  return /^\d{1,6}$/.test(t) && Number(t) <= 100000
    ? ''
    : 'Quantity must be a whole number from 0 to 100000.';
}
